*K. Jack Scott,Narun Pat,Veronica Liesaputra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对三个不同规模（117M到32B）的decoder-only transformer模型进行逐层分析，研究了模型如何解决词汇歧义问题。研究发现表示在中层达到最大区分度后又在深层部分重聚，但激活修补实验证明深层表示差异直接决定输出结果，而单层消融实验表明不同模型存在不同的层间脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观察发现模型内部嵌入相似度与行为输出之间的相关性很低，尽管模型表现良好。作者希望揭示这一现象背后的机制，即模型是如何在嵌入空间中看似相似的情况下仍能正确消歧。

Method: 采用三种方法：(1) 对三个规模不同的模型（GPT-2-Small-117M, Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-32B）进行逐层分析，追踪同音词和多义词的表示变化；(2) 激活修补实验提供因果关系证据，证明深层表示差异直接决定输出；(3) 单层消融实验比较不同模型的层间脆弱性差异。

Result: 同音词和多义词的表示在中层达到最大区分度，随后在深层部分重聚；KL散度在最终层达到最大值；激活修补实验确证深层表示差异直接影响输出；单层消融实验显示不同模型在实现同等消歧效果时存在不同的层间脆弱性模式。

Conclusion: 语义区分在模型内部仍然存在，但随着层数的加深，这种区分在嵌入空间中变得对相似度度量越来越不可见。这对依赖后期层余弦相似度的嵌入方法（如语义搜索、检索和聚类）具有重要启示。

Abstract: In this work we investigate how decoder-only transformers resolve lexical ambiguity through layer-by-layer analysis of three models spanning three parameter sizes (GPT-2-Small-117M, Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-32B). For both homonyms and polysemes, we find that representations become maximally distinct in middle layers, then partially reconverge in late layers, while the KL divergence between their next-token predictions reaches its maximum in the final layers. The activation patching experiment provides causal evidence that late-layer representational differences directly determine outputs despite apparent increased similarity in embedding space. Our single-layer ablation experiment indicates that models achieve equivalent disambiguation despite qualitatively different layer-wise vulnerabilities. These findings offer a mechanism for recent observations where models' internal embedding similarities show low correlation with their behavioural outputs despite strong performance. The semantic distinctions therefore remain present but become increasingly invisible to similarity measures over the embeddings, with implications for embedding-based methods such as semantic search, retrieval, and clustering that rely on late-layer cosine similarity.

</details>


### [102] [Analyzing Speech Condition Effects in Dysarthric ASR: A Layer-wise Probing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01865)
*Darwin Jelestin Muthu,Navya Gupta,Wei Lin Tay,Zhengchen Zhang,Daniel Wang Zhengkui,Rong Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Transformer ASR编码器在普通话构音障碍语音上的各层表示进行探针分析，发现音素边界信息在所有层级均较弱、音素身份在上层可恢复、识别难度编码在最深层；在此基础上提出基于层的参数高效微调（单层LoRA在第7层和5-8层子集适配），性能接近全编码器适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍语音会严重降低ASR性能，但此前缺乏对"失序发音如何重塑模型内部表示"这一问题的深入探究。理解这一机制有助于指导针对低资源普通话构音障碍语音识别的高效适配策略。

Method: 采用逐层探针（layer-wise probing）分析Transformer ASR编码器在三种转录匹配条件下（原始构音障碍语音、说话人条件零样本TTS重合成、无条件TTS）的内部表示。在音素边界、音素身份和识别难度等维度进行探针，并结合声调敏感评估考察普通话词调这一持续性错误源；基于探针发现设计单层LoRA（第7层）和子集层（5-8层）的参数高效微调。

Result: 探针显示任务相关层级结构：音素边界信息在每层对构音障碍语音都很弱，音素身份在上层变得可恢复，识别难度编码在深层。跨条件相似性分歧随深度增大，表明失序语音对高层表示影响更大。单层LoRA第7层和5-8层子集适配分别达到全编码器适配性能3.5%和2.48%的相对差距，而上层适配效果较差。

Conclusion: 研究将表示分析与参数高效微调联系起来，证明低层和中层表示承载关键信息，为低资源普通话构音障碍语音ASR提供了层级感知的适配策略指导。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) performance degrades sharply on dysarthric speech, yet how disordered articulation reshapes a model's internal representations is underexplored. We present a layer-wise probing analysis of a transformer ASR encoder on Mandarin dysarthric speech under three transcript-matched conditions: original dysarthric speech, speaker conditioned zero-shot TTS resynthesis, and unconditioned TTS. The probes reveal a task-dependent hierarchy: phoneme boundary information stays weak for dysarthric speech at every layer, phoneme identity becomes recoverable toward the upper layers, and recognition difficulty is encoded in the deepest layers. Tone-sensitive evaluation shows Mandarin lexical tone is a persistent error source. Cross-condition similarity divergence grows with depth, indicating that disordered speech affects high-level representations more than low-level acoustic features. Guided by these findings, single-layer LoRA at layer 7 and adaptation on subset layers 5-8 achieve performance within 3.5% and 2.48% relative margins of full encoder adaptation, respectively, while upper-layer adaptation is less effective for dysarthric speech. These findings link representation analysis to parameter-efficient fine-tuning and motivate layer-aware adaptation for low-resource Mandarin dysarthric ASR.

</details>


### [103] [CRISP: Critical Step Perception for Training Efficient Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01867)
*Haosi Mo,Zihao Yan,Ruiqing Zhang,Zhongli Li,Hexuan Deng,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRISP框架，通过关键步骤感知训练高效的深度搜索智能体。CRISP利用后向证据归纳构建关键步骤标签，蒸馏到小型识别器中，在策略优化时仅对成功轨迹施加效率感知奖励，在不牺牲答案准确率的前提下减少了15.1%和33.2%的平均交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索智能体通过多步与外部工具交互解决复杂问题，但产生大量冗余查询、低效探索和无关观察，导致高昂的计算和交互成本。已有的效率方法对所有工具交互统一惩罚，可能抑制收集必要证据的步骤，因此需要一种能区分必要证据收集步骤与冗余步骤的训练框架。

Method: CRISP包含三个核心组件：(1) 后向证据归纳（Backward Evidence Induction）：从最终答案出发，由强模型反向遍历完整搜索轨迹，逐步骤判断每个工具交互步骤是否为最终答案提供或保留了证据，构建关键步骤标签；(2) 关键步骤识别器：将这些步骤级判断蒸馏到较小的模型中，支持单次全轨迹分析；(3) 效率感知奖励：仅在成功rollout上应用奖励，保留必要证据收集步骤并剪除冗余步骤。

Result: 在BrowseComp和HLE-Verified基准上，CRISP在保持竞争力的最终答案准确率的同时，分别将平均交互轮次减少了15.1%和33.2%，展示了显著的交互效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过区分必要证据收集与冗余交互并进行差异化的效率奖励塑造，可以在不大幅牺牲答案准确率的前提下显著提升深度搜索智能体的交互效率，为效率优化提供了比统一惩罚工具使用更精细的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly extended into deep search agents that solve complex questions through multi-step interaction with external search and browsing tools. However, existing agents often incur substantial computational and interaction costs, generating lengthy trajectories that contain redundant queries, inefficient exploration, and irrelevant observations. Existing efficiency-oriented methods usually encourage agents to use tools less frequently, but treating all tool interactions uniformly may also suppress steps that gather necessary evidence. In this paper, we propose CRISP, a framework for training efficient deep search agents through critical step perception. Unlike prior efficiency methods that uniformly penalize tool use, CRISP distinguishes interactions that gather necessary evidence from redundant ones and shapes the training reward to preserve the former while pruning the latter, improving efficiency without sacrificing the evidence needed for correct answers. Specifically, CRISP first constructs critical-step labels with Backward Evidence Induction: starting from the final answer, a strong model traverses a completed search trajectory backward and judges whether each tool-interaction step provides or preserves evidence for the final answer. We then distill these step-wise judgments into a smaller critical-step recognizer, enabling full-trajectory analysis in a single pass. During policy optimization, an efficiency-aware reward is applied only to successful rollouts. Experiments on BrowseComp and HLE-Verified show that CRISP maintains competitive final-answer accuracy while reducing average interaction turns by 15.1% and 33.2%, respectively, demonstrating substantial improvements in interaction efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [TRAM: Enhancing Multimodal Reasoning with Trajectory-Derived Auxiliary Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01922)
*Kang Liu,Zijing Wang,Yongkang Liu,Mengjie Zhao,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAM（Trajectory-derived Auxiliary Memory），一种免训练的方法，通过将模型自身推理轨迹中的派生信息压缩为紧凑的潜在记忆，并通过快慢循环流在线更新、以轻量残差路径反馈至解码器层，来缓解多模态大推理模型（MLRM）在长推理轨迹中的信息衰减问题，在四个MLRM变体和八个基准上显著提升数学、科学和通用视觉推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大推理模型在长推理轨迹中，不仅视觉信息会被削弱，推理过程中产生的任务相关关系、约束和中间结论等信息也会逐渐失效，导致推理错误。现有方法主要聚焦于维持视觉grounding，忽略了推理派生信息在长轨迹中的保留与整合问题。

Method: 通过归因分析发现正确性并非仅由图像归因区分，而更依赖于轨迹是否跨阶段保留并整合推理派生信息。基于此，TRAM采用免训练的解码增强方案：将已完成推理整合为紧凑的潜在记忆，通过快慢循环流在线更新，并通过轻量残差路径将记忆反馈到选定的解码器层。

Result: 在四个MLRM变体、八个基准上的实验表明，TRAM在数学、科学和通用视觉推理任务上均优于标准解码（vanilla decoding），且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: TRAM验证了推理派生信息（而非仅视觉信息）的持续保留与整合对长轨迹推理正确性的关键作用，提供了一种简单有效的免训练推理增强方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Reasoning Models (MLRMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks requiring visual understanding and multi-step inference. However, as reasoning trajectories grow, models may become less effective at using information established earlier in the context, increasing the risk of reasoning errors. Existing approaches primarily address this problem by sustaining visual grounding throughout reasoning. However, reasoning also transforms visual observations into task-specific relations, constraints, and intermediate conclusions whose influence may weaken over long trajectories. Our attribution analysis suggests that correctness is not consistently separated by image attribution alone, but is more closely associated with whether trajectories retain and integrate such reasoning-derived information across stages. Motivated by this, we introduce TRAM (TRajectory-derived Auxiliary Memory), a training-free method that augments standard decoding with an auxiliary memory pathway derived from the model's own reasoning trajectory. TRAM consolidates completed reasoning into a compact latent memory, updates it online through fast and slow recurrent streams, and feeds it back into selected decoder layers through a lightweight residual pathway. Experiments across four MLRM variants on eight benchmarks show that TRAM improves performance over vanilla decoding on mathematical, scientific, and general visual reasoning tasks without additional training.

</details>


### [105] [Automatic Annotation of Ancient Greek Vowel Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01935)
*Albin Thörn Cleland,Eric Cullhed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个适用于任意古希腊语输入的通用水准标记器（macronizer），利用递归模块从常见词形向罕见词形传递元音长度标记，并证明其输出可用于训练字符级Transformer模型，后者能推广到规则系统未覆盖的情形，在黄金标准基准上达到或超过规则系统的准确率，同时改善下游韵律NLP任务如诗歌格律分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语中存在被称为dichrona的字母（alpha、iota、ypsilon）在音素元音长度上含糊不清，而现有语料库未对其进行消歧。为语料添加正确元音长度的过程称为macronizing，该问题因词形繁多且依赖上下文而呈长尾分布。目前没有公开的大规模已标注元音长度的古希腊语语料库，需要一个独立的水准标记器。此前工作仅构建了静态的、针对特定语料库的元音长度词典，缺乏通用性。

Method: 作者构建了首个通用的古希腊语macronizer：输入为标准CoNLL-U格式（含词元、词性及形态标注），通过一组递归模块让较少出现的词形从同一词汇词（lexical word）中较常见词形继承元音长度标记。随后训练一个小的字符级Transformer模型，以macronizer自身的输出为训练数据，使其学会推广到规则系统未标记的情形。

Result: 在手工标注的诗歌与散文黄金标准基准上，该字符级Transformer的准确率与规则系统相当甚至超过之，并能在规则系统留下未标记的情况上进行泛化。此外，macronization还能改善下游韵律NLP任务（如诗歌格律分析）的性能。

Conclusion: 该通用macronizer填补了古希腊语NLP中缺乏可扩展元音长度标注工具的空白，其主要应用场景是为机器学习生成训练数据，并证明macronization对下游韵律任务具有正向促进作用。

Abstract: Prior work in Ancient Greek NLP relies on corpora that do not disambiguate the phonemic vowel length of alpha, iota, and ypsilon, together known as the dichrona. Depending on lexeme, morphology, sandhi, syntax, and conventions of period, genre, and verse form, each of these letters can represent either a long or a short vowel. Deciding and marking the correct length is known as "macronizing", a long-tail problem given the sheer mass of word forms and the context dependency of individual instances. No macronized corpus of Ancient Greek is publicly available at scale, so a stand-alone macronizer is needed. While previous work has shown how to build a static, corpus-bespoke vowel-length dictionary, the present paper constructs the first general-purpose macronizer for arbitrary Ancient Greek input. Given input carrying lemma, part-of-speech, and morphological annotation in the standard CoNLL-U format, a set of recursive modules lets less common word forms inherit markup from more common forms of the same lexical word. The macronizer's chief application is generating training data for machine learning: we show that a small character-level transformer trained on the macronizer's own output learns to generalize past the cases the rule-based system leaves unmarked, matching or exceeding its accuracy on a gold-standard, manually annotated benchmark of verse and prose. We also show that macronization can improve downstream prosodical NLP tasks like verse scansion.

</details>


### [106] [Look Ahead Before You Distill: Future Trajectory Validation of Teacher Guidance for Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01953)
*Chishui Chen,Yaoyou Fan,Te Sun,Yi Yang,Chenghao Sun,Delin Mao,Hongbo Qiao,Zuowei Zhang,Junxi Wang,Chenxing Sun,Yangen Hu,Lu Pan,Xuyang Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureBridge-OPD (FTB)，一种面向多轮智能体任务的在线蒸馏改进方法。FTB在高分歧状态处执行短教师桥（short teacher bridge），通过评估桥接后学生轨迹是否提升了正向蒸馏信号的密度来判断该教师指导是否有益，从而解决传统OPD中学生偏差累积导致教师带离有效区域的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏（OPD）通过在学生访问的状态上提供教师监督来缩小训练与推理的分布差距，但在多轮智能体任务中，学生的偏差会随时间累积，逐渐偏离教师指导仍然有效的状态区域。定量分析表明高分歧状态为教师指导提供了有前景的机会，但判断该指导是否有益需要考察其对后续学生轨迹的影响。因此需要一个机制来甄别并利用高分歧状态下的有效教师指导。

Method: FTB在高分歧状态处执行一个短教师桥（short teacher bridge），然后观察在该桥接作用下产生的后续学生轨迹连续性（student continuation），通过比较桥接前后正向蒸馏信号的密度变化来评估该教师桥是否带来正收益（即提升正向蒸馏信号相对于教师的密度）。若桥接增加了正向信号密度，则认为该教师指导有益并保留；否则放弃。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，以Qwen3-32B教师蒸馏至Qwen3-1.7B学生为主要设定下，FTB相较于vanilla OPD和TCOD分别平均提升16.6分和7.6分，并在不同学生规模与教师设定下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: FTB通过在执行教师桥后评估其对学生后续轨迹的影响，有效解决了多轮智能体任务中在线蒸馏因学生偏差累积而失效的问题，能够在高分歧状态下精准利用有价值的教师指导，显著提升蒸馏性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides teacher supervision on states visited by the student, reducing the distribution gap between training and inference. However, in multi-turn agentic tasks, student deviations may accumulate over time, gradually moving the trajectory away from states where teacher guidance remains effective. Our quantitative analysis further shows that high-disagreement states offer promising opportunities for teacher guidance, but determining whether such guidance is beneficial requires examining its effect on subsequent student trajectories. We propose FutureBridge-OPD (FTB), which executes a short teacher bridge at a high disagreement state and uses the resulting student continuation to assess whether the bridge increases the density of positive distillation signals relative to the teacher. On ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, under the main Qwen3-32B teacher to Qwen3-1.7B student setting, FTB outperforms vanilla OPD and TCOD by an average of 16.6 and 7.6 points, respectively, and remains effective across student scales and teacher settings. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ChenChiShui/FutureBridge-OPD.

</details>


### [107] [CompanionBench: A Theory-Anchored, Real-World-Grounded Benchmark for AI Emotional Companionship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02046)
*Yao Liu,Guangjia Chai,Yuming Huang,Jihao Huang,Lei Wang,Junchen Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CompanionBench，一个基于真实世界去标识化数据构建的中英双语交互式LLM伴侣基准。它通过隐藏披露门控制交互状态空间，基于25种心理学和咨询理论中的10项能力进行评估（其中4项是先前未明确评分的），并用跨家族评审小组和项目反应理论模型来消除评判者偏差。该基准能暴露总分掩盖下的能力级差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM伴侣系统已被大规模部署于个人重要场景，但现有评估存在多重缺陷：场景为手写而非真实、用提示模拟器而非训练过的用户模拟器、将共情聚合为单一分数、忽视评判者偏见（如同家族偏好和尺度漂移）。需要一个更严谨、以真实数据和理论为基础的评估方法。

Method: CompanionBench采用双管齐下的评估框架：(1) 场景和训练的用户模拟器均基于去标识化的真实世界数据；(2) 隐藏的披露门根据智能体自身行为分支每个人的轨迹，在不编写脚本的情况下控制交互状态空间；(3) 基于心理学和咨询领域的25种理论操作化10项能力，其中4项（holding ambiguity、selfobject responsiveness、positive resonance、calibrated challenge）是先前未明确评分的新能力；(4) 跨家族评审小组削弱同家族偏袒，项目反应理论模型将智能体质量与评判者严格程度分离。

Result: 评估28个智能体的结果表明：排名在两种语言中具有可复现性（中文rho=0.996，英文rho=0.953）。能力层面的差异被聚合分数掩盖。情绪调节和校准性挑战是常见弱点，而holding ambiguity最具区分度。角色扮演类智能体排名接近垫底——沉浸感不等于关系能力。最普遍的失败模式是用表面温暖替代实质性的关系支持。将发布500对中英文平行数据对和评估代码。

Conclusion: 理论决定测量内容和人物结构设计，真实数据提供事件、历史和档案——覆盖率来自理论，真实性来自数据。CompanionBench验证了这种理论-数据结合的基准构建范式，揭示了聚合评分掩盖能力差异的问题，并指出LLM伴侣的核心缺陷在于以表面温暖替代深层关系支持。

Abstract: LLM companions are deployed at scale in personally consequential settings, yet poorly evaluated. Existing benchmarks use hand-authored scenarios and prompted simulators, aggregate empathy into one score, and overlook judge biases such as same-family favoritism and scale drift. We introduce CompanionBench, an interactive bilingual benchmark. To our knowledge, it is the first companion benchmark to ground both its scenarios and a trained user simulator in de-identified real-world data. A hidden disclosure gate branches each persona's trajectory on the agent's own behavior, controlling the interaction state space without scripting dialogue. We operationalize ten capabilities derived from 25 theories across psychology and counseling, four of them not graded explicitly by prior work: holding ambiguity, selfobject responsiveness, positive resonance and calibrated challenge. Agents are assessed on two complementary axes: a subjective ten-capability rubric and a deterministic measure of whether deeper disclosure was earned. A cross-family panel dilutes same-family favoritism; an Item Response Theory model separates agent quality from judge severity. Theory fixes what to measure and how personas are structured; real data supply events, history, and profiles -- coverage from theory, authenticity from data. Rankings are reproducible in both languages (rho = 0.996 ZH / 0.953 EN). Evaluating 28 agents reveals capability-level differences obscured by aggregate scores. Emotion regulation and calibrated challenge remain common weaknesses; holding ambiguity discriminates most. Role-play agents rank near the bottom: immersion does not imply relational competence. Across agents, the dominant failure mode is substituting surface warmth for substantive relational support. We will release 500 Chinese-English parallel pairs and the evaluation code.

</details>


### [108] [TextNCA: Neural Cellular Automata for Language Modeling via Hierarchical Local Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02050)
*Avni Mittal,Avinash Anand,Ashutosh Kumar,Dikshant Kukreja,Kritarth Prasad,Sushane Dulloo,Erik Cambria,Timothy Liu,Zhengkui Wang,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TextNCA，一个基于1D因果窗口注意力的神经细胞自动机（NCA）风格语言模型，并在约30M参数规模下于WikiText-103上剖析其行为。结果显示该模型（PPL 60.3）不如同规模Transformer（PPL 44.7-52.8），但作为分析探针揭示：性能主要由"由窄到宽"的分级窗口调度驱动，而非迭代本身；迭代带来有限的边际收益（最优T_s=4，超过后性能呈U型劣化）；GRU门控与学习到的逐步嵌入对迭代收益至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答一个核心问题：严格局部、迭代、权重共享的计算原语能否支撑语言建模？更重要的是，这三种特性（局部性、迭代、权重共享）中究竟哪一种真正驱动模型行为。该研究并非为了提出替代Transformer的模型，而是作为受控实验来解读NCA式计算在语言建模中的实际贡献。

Method: 定义TextNCA——1D因果窗口化注意力实现的NCA原语。构建三级级联变体，窗口大小w∈{8,32,128}，每级进行T_s次共享权重迭代，在WikiText-103上以约30M参数和60k训练步训练。通过系统地反转、展平或打破调度的单调性来隔离各因素贡献，并测试不带迭代的滑动窗口Transformer（复用同一调度）作为对照，同时消融GRU门控、逐步嵌入等组件。

Result: Hier-TextNCA达60.3 PPL，低于同规模Transformer-6L（52.8）和Transformer-12L（44.7）。非迭代的滑动窗口Transformer复用相同调度仅比迭代模型差+4.1 PPL，而反转/展平/打破调度单调性导致+16.7到+70.8 PPL的退化。迭代提供有限的额外收益，最优T_s=4，超过后呈U型退化。GRU门控和逐步嵌入是迭代收益出现的必要条件；随机T_s训练提供推理时迭代次数调节旋钮，但显著提高绝对PPL。

Conclusion: NCA风格的严格局部分层计算在30M参数规模下无法匹配Transformer的语言建模能力。其行为主要由窄到宽的分级窗口调度解释，迭代本身仅贡献有限的附加收益，且高度依赖特定门控机制。该工作定位为对NCA式计算各组成部分的语言建模贡献的受控剖析。

Abstract: Can a strictly local, iterated, weight-shared computation primitive support language modelling, and which of those three properties actually drives the model's behaviour? We define \textsc{TextNCA}, a 1D causal windowed-attention realisation of the Neural Cellular Automaton primitive, and study a hierarchical variant that cascades three stages with windows $w \in \{8, 32, 128\}$ and $T_s$ shared-weight iterations per stage, all on WikiText-103 at roughly 30M parameters and 60k training steps. The model does not match a parameter-matched Transformer at this scale (Hier-TextNCA $60.3$ vs.\ Transformer-6L $52.8$ and Transformer-12L $44.7$ PPL), so we treat it as an analytical probe rather than a proposed alternative. The behaviour we observe is largely explained by the staged narrow-to-wide schedule: a non-iterating sliding-window Transformer that reuses the same schedule comes within $+4.1$ PPL of the iterated model, while reversing, flattening, or breaking the monotonic ordering of the schedule costs between $+16.7$ and $+70.8$ PPL. Iteration adds a smaller bounded benefit on top of the schedule, with a clear optimum at $T_s{=}4$ and a U-shaped degradation beyond it. The GRU gate and learned per-step embeddings are required for that benefit to appear, and training with random $T_s$ yields an inference-time iteration-count knob at the cost of substantially higher absolute PPL. We position the work as a controlled reading of which parts of NCA-style computation carry the weight in language modelling.

</details>


### [109] [CAVE: Competence-Aware Visual Boundary Evidence Alignment for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02078)
*Wei Jia,Zhicong Lu,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Wenqian Lv,Jiayue Cao,Huaxing liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CAVE方法，通过引入边界特定视觉证据对齐奖励，解决大规模视觉语言模型在视频时间定位（VTG）中通过强化学习微调时视觉证据与预测时间戳错位的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs在VTG任务中通过RL取得了显著性能提升，但主要依赖仅评估最终预测区间的结果正确性奖励，缺乏对边界相关视觉证据及其与时间戳预测对应关系的充分约束，导致普遍存在视觉证据与预测时间戳错位的问题。

Method: CAVE方法包括三个关键组件：(1) 引入边界特定证据token来显式表示边界级视觉证据，并通过轻量级监督式热启动初始化其结构生成和边界语义；(2) 在RL阶段设计视觉边界证据对齐奖励，强化ground-truth边界内特殊证据token的视觉注意力，促进视觉证据与时间边界对齐；(3) 采用性能感知的证据监督门控，自适应保留对定位较差样本的证据引导，而在定位足够准确时减少引导以避免过度约束细粒度边界精修。

Result: 在多个公开VTG基准上的大量实验验证了CAVE方法的有效性，表明视觉边界证据对齐能够显著缓解证据-时间戳错位问题并提升定位性能。

Conclusion: 通过将边界特定视觉证据奖励引入定位优化，能够有效缓解证据-时间戳错位问题，为VTG任务中RL范式的奖励设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved substantial performance gains in Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) through reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing methods primarily rely on outcome correctness rewards that evaluate only the final predicted intervals, leaving boundary-related visual evidence and its correspondence with timestamp predictions insufficiently constrained. In this paper, we delve into timestamp prediction and its underlying boundary-level visual evidence, showing prevalent misalignment between visual evidence and predicted timestamps across widely used benchmarks. To address this issue, we propose Competence-Aware Visual Boundary Evidence Alignment (CAVE), which augments localization optimization with boundary-specific visual evidence rewards to mitigate evidence-timestamp misalignment. Specifically, to explicitly represent the boundary-specific visual evidence, CAVE introduces boundary-specific evidence tokens and initializes their structured generation and distinct boundary semantics through a lightweight supervised warm-up. During RL, the visual boundary evidence alignment reward reinforces the visual attention of special evidence tokens within the ground-truth boundaries, thereby promoting alignment between visual evidence and temporal boundaries. Moreover, performance-aware gating for evidence supervision is designed to adaptively retain evidence guidance for poorly localized groups while reducing it once localization becomes sufficiently accurate to avoid over-constraining fine-grained boundary refinement. Extensive experiments on several public VTG benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [110] [Cross-Domain Hybrid OPD for Generalizable Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02101)
*Hongzhan Chen,Xiaoyu Liu,Dengming Zhang,Minzhou Huang,Dongliang Xu,Jingcheng Xie,Dongxiang Fang,Bowen Qin,Minsheng Hao,Yaozong Shen,Xiaojun Quan,Mona Zhou,Haosheng Zou,Jeff Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了元宝（Yuanbao）搜索智能体的训练框架，基于Hunyuan3架构，通过结合智能体强化学习与跨领域专家在线策略蒸馏（OPD）管线，实现了搜索专业化与通用智能的兼顾，有效缓解了对齐税问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化语言模型以专门执行搜索行为通常会带来"对齐税"问题——搜索性能的提升以牺牲通用能力为代价，限制了模型作为通用助手的有效性。现有方法将专业化与通用能力视为竞争目标，缺乏能同时优化二者的训练框架。

Method: 框架基于Hunyuan3架构，包括两个核心组件：(1) 用于自主搜索的智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）；(2) 跨领域专家在线策略蒸馏（OPD）管线——将互补通用领域专家模型的知识蒸馏到搜索专化学生模型中，恢复并进一步增强其广泛能力。通过混合训练策略联合优化专化与通用能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提模型在搜索任务上达到有竞争力的性能，同时持续提升通用能力，在专化执行与广泛泛化之间实现了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作证明专化与通用能力并非不可调和的竞争目标，通过在训练中联合优化二者，可以有效缓解对齐税问题，为构建既擅长搜索又能作为通用助手的模型提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the capabilities of autonomous search agents, enabling sophisticated planning, and iterative retrieval over dynamic information sources. However, optimizing language models for specialized search behaviors often incurs an alignment tax, where gains in search performance come at the expense of general-purpose capabilities, limiting their effectiveness as universal assistants. In this technical report, we present the training framework behind the Yuanbao search agent, designed to achieve search specialization without sacrificing general intelligence. Built upon the Hunyuan3 architecture, our framework combines agentic reinforcement learning for autonomous search with a cross-domain expert On-Policy Distillation (OPD) pipeline. Experts specializing in complementary general-purpose domains are distilled into the search-specialized student, restoring and further enhancing its broad capabilities. Rather than treating specialization and general capability as competing objectives, our hybrid training strategy jointly optimizes both, effectively mitigating the alignment tax. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the resulting model achieves competitive search performance while consistently improving its general-purpose capabilities, providing a favorable balance between specialized execution and broad generalization in real-world search scenarios.

</details>


### [111] [IACM-RL: Intent-Aware Context Management and Reinforcement Learning for Complex Tool Invocation under Dynamic Intent Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02110)
*Dingwei Zhu,Jiahan Li,Chengjun Pan,Yunxian Yang,Yunbin Zhao,Yunke Zhang,Zhonghang Lu,Zhuohui Sheng,Chenhao Huang,Jiahang Lin,Yajie Yang,Junlin Shang,Shichun Liu,Yuhui Wang,Honglin Guo,Junjie Ye,Xin Guo,Jiazheng Zhang,Ming Zhang,Shihan Dou,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xipeng Qiu,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IACM-RL框架，针对真实环境中长程工具调用在动态用户意图噪声下的鲁棒性问题。通过DynamicIntent数据管线合成13种波动场景，并部署基于BeliefState的自生成上下文管理器，结合层次化意图奖励和三种辅助损失来优化策略，在多个基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用方法假设指令完美且场景简单，无法应对真实世界中动态变化的用户意图。在波动上下文下，过时约束会稀释模型注意力，导致灾难性意图偏离和无限API循环。需要一种能主动跟踪目标变化并隔离过期参数的鲁棒框架。

Method: 1) DynamicIntent数据管线：合成覆盖13种细粒度波动场景的轨迹，配套五维诊断指标套件。2) BeliefState-based Self-Generated Context Manager：主动跟踪变化目标，通过结构化过期标志隔离被覆盖的参数。3) 层次化意图驱动奖励与三种辅助损失（动作校准、CM提取、状态蒸馏）来训练策略自主内化状态追踪能力。

Result: 在DynamicIntent、BFCL-V3和τ²-Bench基准上，IACM-RL显著优于基线方法，减少了无限循环和过期上下文错误，同时提升了跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: IACM-RL通过显式的信念状态建模和自生成上下文管理，有效解决了动态意图噪声下的工具调用鲁棒性问题，为真实场景的长程工具执行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Executing long-horizon tool invocations in real-world environments is severely challenged by dynamic user intent noise. Existing methods attempt robustness via implicit history scanning or text compression, yet predominantly assume perfect instructions in simplistic scenarios. Inevitably, under fluctuating contexts, obsolete constraints dilute model attention, triggering catastrophic intent deviation and infinite API loops. To resolve this, we propose IACM-RL, a comprehensive framework for robust tool invocation. First, we introduce the DynamicIntent pipeline, synthesizing trajectories across 13 fine-grained fluctuation scenarios, paired with a five-dimensional diagnostic metric suite. Second, IACM-RL deploys a BeliefState-based Self-Generated Context Manager that proactively tracks shifting goals and isolates overwritten parameters using structural stale flags. To autonomously internalize this state-tracking capability, we optimize the policy using a hierarchical intent-driven reward alongside three auxiliary losses (action calibration, CM extraction, and state distillation). Experiments on DynamicIntent, BFCL-V3, and $\mathrmτ^2$-Bench demonstrate that IACM-RL significantly outperforms baselines, reducing infinite loops and stale context errors while enhancing out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [112] [From Chains to Trees: Parent-Conditioned Drafting for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02123)
*Zixian Li,Tong Li,Chi Xie,Xiaohui Song,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PCTree（Parent-Conditioned Drafting Tree），通过利用半自回归草稿模型DSpark中已学习到的条件结构，在不重新训练或增加额外骨干网络前向传播的前提下，将线性草稿解码转化为树形草稿解码，从而显著提升推测解码的端到端推理加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码仅在草稿续文通过目标模型验证时才能加速LLM推理。半自回归草稿器（如DSpark）虽能一次性预测整个token块，但其解码方式为单一链式结构，早期token不匹配会导致后续suffix全部失效，限制了增大草稿块长度的收益。作者希望挖掘模型中已存在的条件容量来实现多分支草稿，而无需重新训练。

Method: PCTree使用预训练的Markov头为每个具体父节点分别计算备选子节点的得分，并基于固定验证预算将概率最高的路径分配给树形结构。该方法将DSpark的线性草稿转化为树形草稿，同时保持其单次并行骨干网络前向传播的特性，实现推理时（inference-only）的改进。

Result: 在Qwen3-{4B,8B,14B}和九个基准上，B=7时相对匹配DSpark的AR加速提升为3.1%至29.5%。在Qwen3-4B GSM8K上B=16时，平均接受长度从9.41提升至11.16，三次运行平均AR加速从6.14倍提升至6.60倍。

Conclusion: 父条件分支能够将半自回归草稿器中已有的条件容量仅通过推理时改动转化为端到端推理增益，证明了无需重新训练的树形草稿策略的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference only when drafted continuations survive target-model verification. Semi-autoregressive drafters such as DSpark predict an entire token block with one backbone forward and refine it with a lightweight Markov head. However, DSpark decodes this block as a single chain, so an early mismatch invalidates the remaining suffix and limits the benefit of large draft blocks.
  We show that the conditional structure already learned by DSpark can support multiple parent-consistent continuations without retraining or additional backbone passes. We introduce Parent-Conditioned Drafting Tree (PCTree), which uses the pretrained Markov head to score alternative children separately for each concrete parent and allocates a fixed verification budget to the most probable paths. This converts DSpark's linear draft into a tree while preserving its one-pass parallel backbone.
  Across Qwen3-{4B,8B,14B} and nine benchmarks, at $B{=}7$, measured speedup gains over autoregressive (AR) decoding, relative to matched DSpark, range from $3.1\%$ to $29.5\%$. On Qwen3-4B GSM8K at $B{=}16$, PCTree increases mean acceptance length from $9.41$ to $11.16$ and three-run mean AR speedup from $6.14{\times}$ to $6.60{\times}$. These show that parent-conditioned branching can turn conditional capacity already present in a semi-autoregressive drafter into end-to-end inference gains through an inference-only change.

</details>


### [113] [The Role of Disfluencies in Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02138)
*Maike Züfle,Maria Teleki,Fabian Retkowski,Vilém Zouhar,Oliver Grabner,Alexander Waibel,James Caverlee,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Uh-Mazing基准，研究语音翻译系统在清理非流利现象（如填充停顿、错误开头）时丢失语义信息的问题。研究表明错误开头和自我修复是翻译质量损失的主因，并发现可通过推理时解码策略缓解，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音翻译系统（包括SpeechLLMs）在清理文本数据时倾向于删除而非翻译非流利现象（如填充停顿和错误开头），但作者指出这些非流利现象承载语义信息，清理会带来信息损失。为系统研究这一现象，需要构建专门基准。

Method: 构建Uh-Mazing基准，包含人工翻译并标注非流利现象的Switchboard语音数据，覆盖英语到八种目标语言；在多种架构上进行跨语言系统评测，并探索推理时解码策略作为无需重新训练的缓解方案。

Result: 跨语言和多架构实验发现：错误开头和自我修复（而非填充停顿或话语标记）是翻译质量损失的主要来源；模型在未能保留非流利现象时倾向于省略而非错译；推理时解码可以在不需要重新训练的情况下缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 非流利现象在语音翻译中承载重要语义，当前系统处理方式存在缺陷；提出的基准和推理时解码方案为社区提供了研究工具和实用缓解路径。

Abstract: Current speech translation systems, including SpeechLLMs, are trained on cleaned text and tend to strip disfluencies like filled pauses and false starts rather than translate them. We show this comes at a cost: disfluencies carry meaning that gets lost when speech is cleaned up. To study this systematically, we introduce Uh-Mazing, a benchmark of human-translated, disfluency-annotated Switchboard speech covering English into eight target languages. Across these languages and several architectures, we find that false starts and self-repairs, not filled pauses or discourse markers, drive most of the translation-quality loss, and that models which fail to preserve a disfluency tend to omit it rather than mistranslate it. We show inference-time decoding can mitigate this without retraining, and release the benchmark and code.

</details>


### [114] [Self-Improving Large Language Models via Progressive Experience Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02139)
*Shijie Ren,Xiting Wang,Meng Li,Yujie Guo,Yunhang Yao,Ziheng Peng,Xunlong Wang,Yuetan Chen,Haoyang Zhou,Yunlong Liang,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPEE是一个统一的自我进化后训练框架，通过显式经验演化（反思、提取、验证并渐进演化可迁移经验）和隐式策略优化（奖赏驱动的强化学习）两个阶段的顺序执行，弥合了测试时方法和训练时优化方法之间的鸿沟，在五个数学推理基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自我进化范式存在碎片化问题：测试时方法能显式提取经验但无法将经验内化到模型参数中；训练时优化方法能更新模型参数但缺乏显式积累可迁移经验的机制。两者之间缺少一个关键的中间阶段——经验蒸馏（experience distillation）。

Method: SPEE包含两个阶段：(1) 显式经验演化阶段：对多轮交互收集的轨迹进行反思，提取、验证并渐进演化可迁移经验，通过特权引导的在线自我蒸馏（OPSD）将其内化到策略中；持续演化的全局经验池整合成功与失败轨迹的知识，过滤低效用经验，减轻单条轨迹导致的回溯性合理化。(2) 隐式策略优化阶段：奖赏驱动的强化学习利用内化的先验知识探索新的解题策略。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上的实验表明，SPEE在三个模型规模上均一致优于测试时和训练时的自我进化基线。

Conclusion: SPEE通过将经验蒸馏作为连接测试时与训练时自我进化范式的关键桥梁，为LLM自我改进提供了统一框架，验证了显式经验提取与隐式策略优化结合的有效性，并公开了源代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) capable of self-improvement require not only effective policy optimization, but also a principled mechanism for transforming transient interaction experience into persistent model capabilities. Existing self-improvement paradigms remain fragmented: test-time methods can explicitly extract experience but cannot internalize it into model parameters, whereas training-time optimization methods can update model parameters but lack an explicit mechanism for accumulating transferable experience. Bridging these two paradigms requires a critical intermediate stage that remains underexplored, namely \emph{experience distillation}. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{SPEE} (\textbf{S}elf-\textbf{P}rogressive \textbf{E}xperience \textbf{E}volution), a unified post-training framework that sequentially performs explicit experience evolution followed by implicit policy optimization. During explicit experience evolution, SPEE reflects on trajectories collected from multiple interactions to extract, verify, and progressively evolve transferable experience, which is subsequently internalized into the policy through privilege-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD). During implicit policy optimization, reward-driven reinforcement learning leverages these internalized priors to explore novel solution strategies. In the experience evolution stage, a continuously evolving global experience pool consolidates knowledge from both successful and failed trajectories, filters out low-utility experience, and mitigates post-hoc rationalization induced by individual trajectories. Experiments on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPEE consistently outperforms both test-time and training-time self-evolution baselines across three model scales. The source code is available at https://github.com/rrrsj/SPEE.

</details>


### [115] [Domain-Specific Evaluation of Text-to-Speech Systems: A Multi-Metric Benchmarking Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02235)
*Ali Jafar,Amal Sarmad,Shifa Yousaf,Maryam Bashir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的多指标基准测试框架，用于系统地评估现代TTS系统在不同语音领域的表现，并在一种低资源语言上通过四个语音领域（正式、会话、文学/叙事、情感）和四种SOTA系统进行了综合案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统虽然在多种语言上取得了显著进步，但缺乏能够联合评估感知质量、说话人相似度和声学保真度的综合评价方法，尤其是针对低资源和代表性不足的语言。

Method: 提出了一个集成主观评估（MUSHRA听力测试、ABX辨别测试）和客观评估（Resemblyzer说话人相似度评分、基于MCD和F0 RMSE的声学分析）的多指标基准框架，在四个语音领域中对四种TTS系统进行960对音频的系统评估。

Result: 结果显示TTS性能在不同语音领域间存在显著差异：情感语音是最难合成的领域（平均MCD 12.03 dB，平均F0 RMSE 889音分），而会话语音的整体声学保真度最高。此外，公开了评估脚本、结果表和可执行的Colab笔记本。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复现的评估框架和基准数据，为低资源语言的TTS标准化评测和未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural text-to-speech (TTS) systems have substantially improved speech naturalness and intelligibility across many languages. However, comprehensive evaluation methodologies that jointly assess perceptual quality, speaker similarity, and acoustic fidelity across diverse speech domains remain limited, particularly for low-resource and underrepresented languages. This paper presents a reproducible, multi-metric benchmarking framework for systematic evaluation of modern TTS systems through domain-specific analysis. The proposed framework integrates complementary subjective and objective evaluation protocols and is demonstrated through a comprehensive case study on a representative low-resource language spanning four speech domains: Formal, Conversational, Literary/Storytelling, and Emotional. Four state-of-the-art TTS systems -- Indic-Parler-TTS, MMS-TTS, Microsoft Edge TTS, and Google Gemini TTS -- are evaluated using MUSHRA listening tests, ABX discrimination tests, speaker similarity scoring with Resemblyzer, and acoustic analyses based on mel-cepstral distortion (MCD) and F0 RMSE over 960 audio pairs. Results reveal substantial variation in TTS performance across speech domains, with emotional speech consistently presenting the greatest synthesis challenge (mean MCD 12.03 dB; mean F0 RMSE 889 cents), while conversational speech achieves the highest overall acoustic fidelity. Beyond the empirical findings, this work provides a reproducible evaluation framework, publicly releasing evaluation scripts, result tables, and executable Colab notebooks to support standardized benchmarking and future research on TTS evaluation for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [116] [An Evidence-Grounded Retrieval-Augmented Transformer Framework for Health Misinformation Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02310)
*Isah M. Bukar,Bala Mairiga Abduljalil,Bashir Saleh Maina,Abdulbasit Hassan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强的Transformer框架，利用WHO和尼日利亚疾控中心的可信证据验证健康相关声明的真实性。在67条人工标注的尼日利亚健康声明数据集上，BERT模型取得最佳表现（准确率71%，加权F1为0.66），但检索增强未提升性能，原因是证据库规模和覆盖度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假健康信息在数字平台上快速传播已成为重大公共卫生挑战，尤其是在传染病暴发期间，延迟验证会影响公众行为并阻碍疾病控制。现有自动化检测方法过度依赖全球生物医学资源，未能捕捉发展中国家验证声明所需的本地背景信息。

Method: 构建检索增强Transformer框架，结合语义证据检索与基于Transformer的分类模型，从WHO和尼日利亚疾控中心的权威来源检索证据，判断声明为真、假或误导。评估了三种Transformer模型及检索增强配置，并构建了涵盖新冠、拉沙热、霍乱、麻疹和猴痘的67条人工标注数据集。

Result: BERT模型表现最佳，准确率为71%，加权F1分数为0.66。检索增强未能提升分类性能，原因是当前证据库规模和覆盖度有限。

Conclusion: 该框架为尼日利亚及其他资源受限环境提供了开发上下文感知、证据驱动的健康信息验证系统的实用基础，同时强调了全面且权威的知识来源对可靠健康信息验证的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid spread of false and misleading health information through digital platforms has become a major public health challenge, particularly during infectious disease outbreaks where delayed verification can influence public behaviour and hinder effective disease control. Although recent advances in automated health misinformation detection have shown encouraging results, most existing approaches rely heavily on global biomedical resources and often fail to capture the local context needed to verify claims in developing countries. This study presents a retrieval-augmented transformer framework designed to verify health-related claims using trusted evidence from the World Health Organization and the Nigeria Centre for Disease Control and Prevention. The framework combines semantic evidence retrieval with transformer-based classification to determine whether a claim is true, false, or misleading. To evaluate the proposed approach, a manually annotated dataset of 67 verified health claims covering coronavirus disease, Lassa fever, cholera, measles, and monkeypox was compiled from Nigerian fact-checking sources.
  Three transformer models and a retrieval-augmented configuration were evaluated. The Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers model achieved the best performance, with an accuracy of 71% and a weighted F1-score of 0.66. Although retrieval augmentation did not improve classification performance because the current evidence repository was limited in size and coverage, the findings highlight the importance of comprehensive and authoritative knowledge sources for reliable health misinformation verification. The proposed framework provides a practical foundation for developing context-aware and evidence-driven health misinformation verification systems for Nigeria and other resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [117] [Can AI Agents Simulate A/B Test Outcomes? A Validation Framework for Agentic Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02345)
*Stefan Hut,Lorenzo Masoero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"模拟随机对照试验"(S-RCT)框架，用AI代理基于行为画像和干预上下文模拟A/B测试结果，建立了两层误差分解框架（代理近似误差+子采样误差），在67个历史营销A/B测试上验证，离线基础模型能捕捉方向信号但高估效应幅度，通过两阶段校准和受试者内设计分别将预测误差降低约77倍、标准误降低约2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A/B测试虽为行业标准，但每次实验消耗真实流量、工程时间和数周耗时。研究旨在探索AI代理能否在投入实时流量前准确模拟实验结果，以筛选候选治疗方案。

Method: 形式化S-RCT框架，推导两层误差分解（代理近似误差与子采样误差）；框架与代理无关，任何行为模型（从微调专家模型到通用基础模型）均可作为仿真引擎；提出两阶段前周期校准协议和受试者内设计以减少系统偏差和采样方差。

Result: 在67个历史营销A/B测试中，使用现成基础模型的基线S-RCT方向信号重叠率为0.70但系统性高估效应幅度；两阶段前周期校准将平方预测误差（去除固有测量噪声后）降低约77倍；受试者内设计将标准误降低约2.4倍。

Conclusion: 讨论了当前方法的局限性，并识别了实验者可从代理信号中获益的应用场景，表明S-RCT有望作为真实A/B测试的前置筛选工具。

Abstract: A/B testing remains the standard for rolling out new features in the technology industry. Each experiment, however, consumes real traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of wall-clock time. Can AI agents---conditioned on behavioral profiles and contextual descriptions of the intervention---simulate outcomes accurately enough to vet candidate treatments before committing live traffic? We formalize this question as a \emph{Simulated Randomized Controlled Trial} (S-RCT) and derive a two-layer error decomposition that separates agent approximation error from subsampling error, enabling targeted improvements to each. The framework is agent-agnostic: any behavioral model---from a fine-tuned specialist to a general-purpose foundation model---can serve as the simulation engine. Validated on 67 historical marketing A/B tests, a baseline S-RCT using an off-the-shelf foundation model captures directional signal (sign overlap 0.70) but systematically overshoots effect magnitudes. A two-phase pre-period calibration protocol reduces the squared prediction error (after removing irreducible measurement noise) by ${\sim}77\times$; a within-subject design---where each agent is exposed to both arms---reduces standard errors by ${\sim}2.4\times$. We discuss limitations of the current approach and identify applications where experimenters stand to benefit from agentic signals.

</details>


### [118] [Global Optimization and Inference-Time Region Grafting for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02353)
*Donghyeok Koh,Gyuwan Kim,Jinyeong Bak,Seung-Hoon Na,Tao Yang,Haneol Jang,Cheoneum Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT方法，在不重新优化全局工作流的前提下，利用执行时的无标签质量信号对工作流的局部区域进行实例级替换和自适应调整，从而在数学推理、代码生成和问答等多个任务上超越现有最强方法MaAS平均3.85个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic工作流优化方法在执行前确定工作流结构，无法利用执行时的无标签质量信号对失败区域进行自适应调整；而直接对整个工作流重新优化计算成本过高。

Method: GRAFT保留全局优化的工作流，仅对每个输入的选定局部区域进行替换。无需参数训练，基于无标签执行质量信号评估区域级替代方案，仅接受能提升局部质量且保持工作流级一致性的替换，从而实现无需整体重优化的实例级自适应。

Result: 在匹配的优化器和执行器设置下，GRAFT平均比最强基线MaAS提升3.85点；仅将执行器替换为更强模型可在不重新优化全局工作流的情况下获得进一步收益。

Conclusion: 优化后的工作流不应被视为静态的优化产物，而是可随推理时反馈和更强执行器演化的自适应执行策略。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic workflow optimization automate workflow design through task-specific workflow search or input-conditioned architecture selection. However, they determine the workflow before execution and cannot adapt failed workflow regions using execution-time label-free quality signals. Naively enabling such inference-time adaptation through whole-workflow re-optimization would be computationally prohibitive. To tackle this challenge, we introduce GRAFT, which preserves a globally optimized workflow while locally replacing only selected regions for each input. Without parameter training, GRAFT evaluates region-level alternatives using label-free execution-quality signals and accepts only replacements that improve local quality while preserving workflow-level consistency, thereby enabling instance-wise adaptation without whole-workflow re-optimization. GRAFT applies without modification across a range of tasks spanning mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-hop and knowledge-intensive question answering. Under matched optimizer and executor settings, it improves over the strongest prior workflow-optimization method, MaAS, by 3.85 points on average. Replacing only the executor with a stronger model yields further gains without re-optimizing the global workflow. This suggests that an optimized workflow is not merely a static optimization artifact, but an adaptable execution policy that can evolve with inference-time feedback and stronger executors.

</details>


### [119] [ScrambleToolBench: Agents Search Exhaustively Even When Their Own Map Points to the Next Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02358)
*Vernon Toh,Navonil Majumder,Zhengyuan Liu,Nancy F. Chen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ScrambleToolBench，一个交互式终端基准测试，用于隔离评估自主智能体在无语义线索和静态环境中通过试错交互推断未知工具行为的能力，并发现当前SOTA语言模型在初始发现方面表现良好，但在面对环境动态变化（如映射漂移）时缺乏适应性推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用基准测试暴露语义工具模式于静态环境中，使智能体依赖先验知识而非自主发现。然而，在开放世界环境中，自主智能体需要在缺乏文档的情况下通过交互推断未知系统的行为。因此需要设计一个能够隔离行为推理能力的基准测试，排除语义线索干扰，并评估智能体在动态变化环境中的适应能力。

Method: 提出ScrambleToolBench干扰终端基准测试：(1) 移除语义线索，使智能体必须通过试错交互发现隐藏工具行为；(2) 引入连续任务课程（continuous task curriculum）; (3) 设计动态挑战，包括映射漂移（mapping drift）、随机动作失败和时态执行窗口，以测试智能体能否随着环境变化修正和调整假设。

Result: 评估SOTA语言模型发现：成功的初始发现并不能转化为稳健的适应性。面对映射漂移等结构性变化时，智能体无法使用循环追踪等演绎策略，表现出信念惯性（belief inertia）或退回到穷举搜索。增加测试时推理只会放大昂贵的暴力搜索而非实现演绎恢复。配备持久记忆可以减少复合错误，但仍无法高效推断结构性变化。

Conclusion: 当前智能体推理在动态环境适应方面存在显著差距：从"发现"到"适应"之间的能力鸿沟尚未被填补，这为未来自主智能体在开放世界中稳健运行的推理研究指出了关键方向。

Abstract: To operate robustly in open-world environments, autonomous agents should be able to infer the behavior of unfamiliar systems through interaction alone, even in the absence of documentation. However, existing tool-use benchmarks expose semantic tool schemas in static environments, allowing agents to rely on prior knowledge rather than autonomous discovery. To address this limitation, we introduce ScrambleToolBench, an interactive terminal benchmark designed to isolate behavioral reasoning. By removing semantic cues and enforcing a continuous task curriculum, the benchmark requires agents to uncover hidden tool behaviors entirely through trial-and-error interaction. The benchmark further introduces dynamic challenges, including mapping drift, stochastic action failures, and temporal execution windows, to evaluate whether agents can revise and adapt their hypotheses as the environment changes. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art language models reveals that successful initial discovery does not translate into robust adaptation. When faced with structural changes such as mapping drift, agents fail to use deductive strategies such as cycle tracing, and instead exhibit belief inertia or fall back to exhaustive search. Increasing test-time reasoning only amplifies this expensive brute-force search rather than enabling deductive recovery. While equipping agents with persistent memory reduces compounding errors, they remain unable to efficiently infer structural changes, highlighting a gap in current agent reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [Fast and Accurate Quotation Attribution in Literary Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02359)
*Gaspard Michel,Hugo Attali,Elena V. Epure*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于编码器的联合评分（joint scoring）方法，在共享的大上下文窗口中同时解决多个引语归属任务，在PDNC语料库上达到94.5%的准确率（SOTA），同时比标准方法快20倍、比LLM方法快1000倍以上，并发布了ModernBookNLP工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学文本中引语归属（quotation attribution）仍是一个未解决的挑战。现有标准方法独立预测每个引语的说话者，准确率有限；而LLM方法性能虽强但计算成本过高，无法用于大规模文学分析。因此需要一种兼顾准确性和效率的方法。

Method: 提出encoder-based的联合评分（joint scoring）方法：在共享的大上下文窗口中同时解决多个引语归属任务，利用预训练编码器中已存在的长距离照应（anaphora）解析信号，提高对困难归属样例的处理能力。

Result: 在PDNC（包含22部英文小说、35,000多条人工标注引语）上达到94.5%的整体归属准确率，达到SOTA；处理速度比标准方法快20倍，比LLM方法快1000倍以上（在A100 GPU上）。

Conclusion: 联合评分方法通过在大上下文窗口中联合处理多个引语归属，兼顾了高准确率和高效率。研究表明预训练编码器中已包含长距离照应解析信号，该信号对困难归属案例至关重要。作者开源了ModernBookNLP工具以便推广应用。

Abstract: Attributing quotations to their speakers in literary texts remains an open challenge. Standard methods, which independently predict a speaker mention for each quotation, are efficient but still limited in accuracy. In contrast, large language model (LLM) approaches achieve strong performance, but their computational cost limits their use in large-scale literary analysis. We propose an encoder-based efficient formulation that resolves multiple quotation attributions within a shared, large context window. Using our new formulation, \textit{joint scoring}, we report state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on the Project Dialogism Novel Corpus (PDNC), comprising more than 35,000 manually annotated quotations from 22 English novels. Our best model reaches 94.5\% overall attribution accuracy while processing novels $20\times$ faster than comparable standard methods and more than $1000\times$ faster than LLM-based approaches on an A100 GPU. An analysis of models' representations suggests that joint scoring improves on challenging attribution examples by preserving long-range anaphora resolution signal, an information that we found already present in pretrained encoders. To facilitate adoption, we release ModernBookNLP, a modified fork of BookNLP that replaces its quotation attribution model with our best system available at https://github.com/gasmichel/ModernBookNLP_QA/.

</details>


### [121] [PredAct-Bench: Benchmarking Tool-Augmented Dialogue under Controlled Tool Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02372)
*Abdulrahman AlRabah,Xiaocheng Yang,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Abdussalam Alawini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PREDACTBENCH，一个用于评估对话智能体与统计不完美工具配对的基准测试，涵盖噪声工具和人类决策者信任不确定的真实场景，并引入RAIR和RSR两个新的信任校准指标。在13个SOTA LLM和人类研究中发现，当前模型无法在工具产生错误建议时为用户提供足够的可见性以防止过度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设工具输出完全准确，忽略了实际部署中系统必须面对噪声工具和信任不确定的人类决策者这一现实。在教育、医疗、金融等高风险领域，工具输出的不确定性可能导致错误决策，因此需要一个更贴近实际的评估基准。

Method: 构建PRACTBENCH基准：(1) 设计AI辅助人类决策的任务格式，AI使用噪声预测器引导用户；(2) 引入情节级别的Relative AI-Reliance (RAIR)和Relative Self-reliance (RSR)指标，扩展信任校准框架到多轮对话；(3) 在两个教育数据集（OULAD和PREDACT-CS）上评估13个闭源和开源LLM，并开展人类研究。

Result: 评估13个SOTA LLM后发现，当工具产生噪声时，模型本应为教师提供可见性以避免过度依赖错误建议或幻觉，但当前模型未能做到这一点。人类研究涉及教师和助教，进一步验证了这一问题。

Conclusion: PREDACTBENCH为构建更好的人机协作LLM决策支持系统提供了基准，特别是在帮助教师应对噪声工具预测方面具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in task-oriented dialogue systems that support multi-step decision-making in high-stakes domains such as education, healthcare, and finance. However, existing benchmarks typically assume perfectly accurate tool outputs, overlooking the reality that deployed systems must operate with noisy tools and human decision-makers whose trust in the agent is itself uncertain. Such conditions are common in practice, for example, a clinician using a diagnostic prediction tool or an advisor relying on a model that forecasts student outcomes from historical records. We introduce PREDACTBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating dialogue agents paired with statistically imperfect tools, using education as a measurable testbed where ground truth outcomes and clear intervention decisions are available. First, we build a benchmark for AI-assisted human decision-making, where the AI uses noisy predictors to help guide a user. Second, we introduce episode-level Relative AI-Reliance (RAIR) and Relative self-reliance (RSR) metrics, extending prior trust calibration framework to multi-turn dialogue. Third, we evaluate 13 state-of-the-art closed and open source LLMs on two educational datasets, OULAD (real assessment trajectories from the UK Open University) and PREDACT-CS (60 courses with real final grade outcomes and synthetically generated weekly score trajectories), alongside a human study with instructors and teaching assistants. We find that when tools are noisy, SOTA models are supposed to provide visibility to teachers so that they do not over-rely on wrong suggestions or hallucinations, but current models fail to do that. We offer PREDACTBENCH to help build better LLMs as AI decision support systems to help teachers.

</details>


### [122] [Training-Free versus Training-Based Intent Classification in LLMs: Accuracy, Robustness, and Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02415)
*Nan Chen,Zhouhao Yang,Soufiane Hayou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性比较了基于大语言模型的意图分类方法，包括两类免训练方法（基于内部表示统计）和两类训练方法（MLP分类器和线性探针），发现训练方法在困难分类任务上更优，而免训练方法在混合意图和对抗性提示下更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图分类对于将用户提示路由到专用模型以提升准确性和计算效率至关重要。然而，现有研究缺乏对训练免费vs训练式意图分类方法的系统性比较，需要明确不同方法在简单与困难任务上的性能差异及鲁棒性表现。

Method: 本文采用两种轻量级免训练方法（基于内部表示统计）与MLP分类器和线性探针两种训练方法进行对比实验，在数学vs编码vs自然语言等简单基准任务以及Java vs Python等困难分类任务上进行全面评估，同时考察混合意图和对抗性提示场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验发现：1) 免训练和训练方法在简单基准任务（数学vs编码vs自然语言）上均能达到饱和性能；2) 在困难分类任务（如Java vs Python）上训练方法具有明显优势；3) 免训练方法在混合意图和对抗性提示下通常更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 免训练与训练方法各有适用场景：简单任务上两者差距不大，困难任务应优先选择训练方法，而面对混合意图或对抗性输入时免训练方法更具鲁棒性，可根据实际应用场景选择合适的意图分类策略。

Abstract: Intent classification in Large Language Models (LLMs) involves categorizing user prompts into predefined classes. For instance, given a user prompt, the system must determine whether it primarily concerns mathematics, coding, or general text processing. Such classification enables routing prompts to specialized models optimized for specific domains, improving both accuracy and computational efficiency. In this work, we conduct a systematic study comparing training-free vs training-based approaches for intent classification. For this purpose, we consider two lightweight, training-free methods based on statistics of internal representations and compare them against MLP classifiers and linear probes. Our comprehensive empirical evaluation reveals that 1) Both training-free and training-based methods saturate easy benchmarks (mathematics vs. coding vs. natural language), 2) Training-based classifiers have an advantage on harder classification tasks (e.g. Java vs Python), and 3) Training-free methods are generally more robust to mixed-intent and adversarial prompts.

</details>


### [123] [CTRAG: An In-Context Retrieval-based Framework for Automated Compliance Checking using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02472)
*Muhammad Roman,Karen Rafferty,Barry Devereux*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CTRAG，一种面向自动合规检查的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，通过自适应分块、动态检索配置和上下文学习策略，实现了高精度的文档信息合规验证，在大型四大的真实部署中F1分数达78%，召回率达85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管合规验证对金融报告、数据隐私和网络安全等受控环境中的企业至关重要，但手动合规测试耗时且易产生不一致，尤其在间接依赖第三方服务（如云提供商）时合规检查更加复杂，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: CTRAG采用检索增强生成框架，核心策略包括：从监管文本中提取控制问题，与无结构公司文档进行交叉引用；使用自适应分块技术优化文档切分；采用动态检索配置提升检索相关性；结合上下文学习增强合规评估的精确性。

Result: 在最终部署配置下CTRAG达到78%的F1分数和85%的召回率，显著减少漏检的不合规案例，并降低了人工审查工作量。该方案已在四大专业服务公司中开发部署了概念验证（POC），应用于真实案例并与手动合规报告交叉验证。

Conclusion: CTRAG有望简化合规工作流程、降低风险，并在复杂高风险的监管环境中增强监管信任，为自动化合规检查提供了有效的实用方案。

Abstract: Trust is fundamental in modern regulatory ecosystems, and compliance checking plays a critical role in fostering that trust. Regulatory compliance verification is essential for businesses operating in highly controlled environments, as it ensures alignment with sector-specific guidelines across domains such as financial reporting, data privacy, and cybersecurity. Manual compliance testing, however, is often time-intensive and prone to inconsistencies, particularly when compliance depends indirectly on third-party services such as cloud providers, where vendors rely on external providers to meet regulatory standards. In this paper, we present CTRAG, a novel Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline designed for automated compliance checking. CTRAG employs advanced strategies, including adaptive chunking, dynamic retrieval configurations, and in-context learning, to improve the precision and relevance of compliance assessments. By extracting control questions from regulatory texts and cross-referencing them with unstructured company documentation, CTRAG achieves highly accurate, document-informed compliance verification, even in cases of indirect compliance through third-party services. Empirical evaluations demonstrate significant improvements, with CTRAG achieving an F1-score of 78% and a recall of 85% in the final deployed configuration, ensuring minimal missed non-compliance cases while reducing manual reviewer effort in a real-world deployment. To validate CTRAG value, we developed and deployed a POC within a Big Four professional services firm, applying it to real-world cases and cross-checking results against manual compliance reports. These findings highlight CTRAG potential to streamline compliance workflows, mitigate risks, and enhance regulatory trust in complex, high-stakes environments.

</details>


### [124] [Cultural Awareness is Represented but Not Decoded: Tracing Mythological Knowledge across 18 Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02486)
*Iaroslav Chelombitko,Ekaterina Chelombitko,Mika Hämäläinen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大语言模型在跨文化实体（如神话人物）识别上的文化偏置问题，发现模型残差流中已清晰地编码了文化信息，但解码器在输出时会将文化特异的token坍缩到主导文化（如希腊/北欧神话）的对应物上，失败发生在"读出"阶段而非"表征"阶段，且该读出门控依赖于提示语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管开源LLM能可靠地识别主流文化（如Zeus、Jupiter、Thor）中的实体，但对非主流文化（芬兰、斯拉夫、埃及、中国神话）中的对应实体识别一致性很差。作者想定位这种"文化默认"（cultural default）是在模型内部何处产生的，以理解多文化公平性问题的根源。

Method: 作者在Thompson母题实体的平行跨文化子集上，使用线性探针（linear probing）、logit透镜（logit lens）、激活修补（activation patching）和输出提取（output extraction）四种技术，对8个架构家族共18个开源LLM进行系统性仪表化分析。同时设计了提示语言变量（目标文化母语 vs 英语），并发布了逐实体的（探针、输出）分解框架、引用锚定的跨文化真值数据集、语言条件读出门控的within- vs cross-mode相关性检验等工具。

Result: 研究发现：残差流能清晰区分不同文化（远高于名字字符串基线），但解码器会将文化特异token坍缩到主导传统的token上。文化失败发生在读出阶段而非表征阶段。此外，提示语言（英语vs目标文化母语）的失败模式在语言内聚类但在跨语言间解耦，说明解码器的读出行为受提示语言门控。

Conclusion: LLM的文化偏置根因不在内部表征层（模型"知道"但不"表达"），而在于解码器的读出阶段，并且读出门控与提示语言强相关。这为改善模型跨文化输出提供了解释性依据：干预解码器层而非表征层是更有希望的改进方向。作者同时提供了完整的评估框架和18个模型的逐实体预测数据。

Abstract: Open-source LLMs reliably name Zeus, Jupiter, and Thor, but recover their counterparts in less-represented traditions like Finnish, Slavic, Egyptian, or Chinese mythology far less consistently. We ask where inside the model this cultural default is produced. On a parallel cross-cultural substrate of Thompson-motif entities, we instrument 18 open-source LLMs from 8 architecture families with linear probing, logit lens, activation patching, and output extraction. The residual stream cleanly distinguishes cultures, well above a name-string baseline, yet the decoder collapses culturally-specific tokens onto dominant-tradition ones. The failure is at readout, not at representation. Asking the same question in the target culture's native language versus English produces failures that cluster within language but decouple across language: the decoder is gated on prompt language. We release a per-entity (probe, output) decomposition framework, a citation-anchored cross-cultural ground truth, a within- versus cross-mode correlation test for language-conditioned readout, and per-entity predictions for all 18 models.

</details>


### [125] [LiveMem: Maintaining Memory State Continuity in Long-Running LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02515)
*Zhichen Liu,Ruihan Sun,Hengjie Yang,Zipeng Wu,Zhaohan Chen,Xiaofan Zhang,Yang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LiveMem是一种内在记忆方法，通过固定容量的记忆状态实现"上下文更替下的状态连续性"，使长时运行的LLM助手/智能体在活动上下文窗口之外仍能保留并利用历史信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的助手和智能体会消费交互流，最终超出上下文限制。现有的上下文保留、摘要和检索方法只能访问选定的历史，无法在活动上下文变化时提供覆盖完整生命周期的持久状态。作者将这一缺失的推理能力形式化为"状态连续性"，并指出需要一种与活动上下文寿命无关的固定容量记忆机制。

Method: LiveMem在预训练的全注意力LLM上增加一个记忆状态，该状态在完整生命周期内保留历史信息，而主注意力路径则保持一个有界的KV窗口。该方法联合使用了三个关键组件：1) 上下文更替与记忆状态维护机制；2) 面向记忆的后训练（memory-oriented post-training）；3) 状态感知的推理服务（state-aware serving）。这三者共同确保记忆状态在原始令牌被释放后仍然承载有效信息。

Result: 在评估系统中，LiveMem在整体性能上达到领先水平。在LongMemEval上的实验表明，即使支持性证据已从当前上下文中移除，LiveMem仍能基于记忆状态回答问题；证据距离分析显示有用信息能持续存在于活动窗口之外。

Conclusion: LiveMem确立了状态连续性作为持续LLM推理中一个独立且互补的抽象概念，证明了固定容量记忆状态可以在上下文更替下保持完整生命周期的信息承载能力。

Abstract: Long-running assistants and agents consume interaction streams that eventually outgrow the context. Existing context retention, summarization, and retrieval preserve access to selected history, but do not provide a persistent state over the full lifecycle when working context changes. We formulate this missing inference capability as \emph{state continuity under context turnover}: carrying computation forward through a fixed-capacity memory state whose lifetime is independent of the active context. We introduce an intrinsic memory method, \textbf{LiveMem}, which augments a pretrained full-attention LLM with a memory state that preserves the historical information over the whole lifecycle while the main attention path retains a bounded KV window. Context turnover and memory state maintaining, memory-oriented post-training, and state-aware serving jointly make this memory state load bearing after its originating tokens are released. Our experiments show that LiveMem achieves leading overall performance among evaluated systems and other intrinsic memory methods. Experiments on LongMemEval show that LiveMem is able to answer the question based on the memory state, even when the supporting evidence has been removed from the current context, and evidence-distance analysis shows that useful information persists beyond the active window. LiveMem thus establishes state continuity as a distinct and complementary abstraction for continual LLM inference.

</details>


### [126] [MedPRESS: A Multi-turn Benchmark for Patient-Pressure-Induced Medical Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02520)
*Saman Sarker Joy,Niloy Farhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPRESS，一个用于衡量LLM在患者施压对话中产生谄媚（sycophancy）行为的多轮基准测试。包含600个医疗场景的五轮对话，评估20个LLM后发现模型在面对反复患者压力时经常转向不安全的一致性回答，且反谄媚提示无法完全消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用静态问题而非施压式患者对话来衡量LLM医疗建议的安全性，无法捕捉模型在动态多轮对话中因患者施压而妥协真实医疗知识的行为。医疗领域安全不能仅依赖静态评估，需要专门针对压力场景的基准测试。

Method: 构建MedPRESS基准，包含600个医疗场景五轮对话，覆盖三类场景（用药与治疗要求、个人健康自我照护、症状分诊与护理抗拒），每轮对话从健康问题逐步升级为个人经历、社会证明、外部证据主张和直接对抗性挑战。对20个LLM（涵盖通用、医疗领域、轻量、大型、开源、专有等）使用结构化评判和安全性指标进行评估。

Result: 模型在反复患者压力下频繁转向不安全的一致性回答，不同模型家族、模型规模和提示类型间存在显著差异。反谄媚提示能提高部分模型的鲁棒性，但无法完全消除不安全的一致行为。

Conclusion: MedPRESS揭示了医疗LLM评估中的关键缺口：仅具备安全的医学知识是不够的，模型还需能在对话压力下维持其知识判断，这为医疗AI安全评估提供了新的基准维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for health-related advice. Existing research measures their safety with static questions rather than pressured patient-facing conversations. We introduce MedPRESS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring patient-pressure-induced sycophancy in LLMs. MedPRESS contains 600 medically grounded five-turn dialogues across three scenario families: medication and treatment demand, personal health self-care, and symptom triage and care resistance. Each dialogue begins with a health query and escalates through personal experience, social proof, external evidence claims, and direct adversarial challenge. We evaluate 20 LLMs across general, medical-domain, lightweight, large, open-weight, and proprietary families using structured judging and safety-focused metrics. Results show that models frequently shift toward unsafe agreement under repeated patient pressure, with substantial variation across model families, model scale, and prompt type. Anti-sycophancy prompting improves robustness for several models, but does not eliminate unsafe agreement. MedPRESS highlights a critical gap in medical LLM evaluation: safe medical knowledge is not enough unless models can maintain it under conversational pressure.

</details>


### [127] [Romanized Arabic Across Dialects: Views, Usage Patterns, and Linguistic Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02555)
*Amr Keleg,Ahmed Amine Ben Abdallah,Taha Yassine,Chadi Helwe,Imane Guellil,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是迄今为止规模最大的以人为中心、跨方言的Arabizi（阿拉伯语拉丁字母转写）感知与实践研究，通过问卷收集五种阿拉伯方言使用者对Arabizi的认识和使用见解，并发布两个资源：字符级对齐数据集（用于研究方言内外的转写变体）和句级平行语料库（用于研究说话者识别风格变异的能力），发现方言内具有系统性规律而方言间存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往NLP研究多将Arabizi视为受技术限制的临时现象，忽略了它在地域和年龄群体中持续且广泛的使用。作者认为需要从阿拉伯语使用者的视角出发，系统性理解Arabizi的感知、使用习惯及其跨方言变异规律，以纠正NLP领域的误区。

Method: （1）与阿拉伯语使用者进行问卷调查，收集其对Arabizi的感知和实际使用习惯；（2）通过调查参与者转写的词构建字符级对齐资源，分析阿尔及利亚、埃及、黎巴嫩、摩洛哥和突尼斯五种方言的方言内与方言间变异；（3）人工构建句级平行语料库，包含阿拉伯字母书写句子及其对应多种Arabizi转写，研究说话者识别风格变异的能力。

Result: 研究发现Arabizi在方言内部具有系统性的转写规律，而在不同方言之间存在系统性的转写差异。作者构建了字符级对齐数据集和句级平行语料库两个资源，并据称这是目前最大规模的跨方言、以人为中心的Arabizi研究。

Conclusion: Arabizi并非临时的技术替代现象，而是一种具有系统化模式的语言实践，其转写规律因方言而异。该研究为NLP领域提供了新的资源和视角，强调应重视Arabizi作为真实语言现象的跨方言多样性。

Abstract: Arabizi refers to Arabic written in Latin script. Although previous studies have shown that the prevalence and usage of Arabizi vary by factors such as region and age group, most NLP research on Arabic texts treats it as a temporary phenomenon resulting from limited technological support for the Arabic script. In this work, we engage with Arabic speakers to collect insights on their perceptions and usage of Arabizi. We further examine writing norms among speakers of different dialects, focusing on Algerian, Egyptian, Lebanese, Moroccan, and Tunisian Arabic. To this end, we release two resources. First, a character-level alignment of Arabic words to study inter- and intra-dialectal variation across these five dialects, based on words transliterated by survey participants, finding systematic intra-dialectal regularity and inter-dialectal variation. Second, to study Arabic speakers' ability to identify this stylistic variation at the sentence-level, we build a manually curated parallel corpus of sentences written in Arabic script alongside multiple Arabizi transliterations, collected from speakers of the same five dialects. Our study presents the largest human-centered, cross-dialectal study of Arabizi's perceptions and practices to date.

</details>


### [128] [AURORA-LM: Autoencoding Unified Representation for Continuous-Latent Diffusion Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02602)
*Jiajun Liang,Yucheng Liao,Yukang Cao,Jiazhe Wei,Ken Li,Wende Tan,Jiankun Zhang,ZY Cui,Jingkang Yang,Liucheng Guo,Shiqi Yang,B. Yang,Caifeng Shan,Ziwei Liu,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AURORA-LM是一个连续潜空间扩散语言模型,通过将可解码文本表示的构建与分布建模解耦,在OpenWebText自由生成和XSum摘要任务上达到了最强性能,1B参数规模下超越更大的公开潜扩散语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本生成仍主要依赖离散token,而图像、视频、音频已在连续潜空间建模。现有连续语言模型要么继承非为联合生成与解码设计的嵌入空间,要么压缩自编码潜变量以简化扩散过程而牺牲token级保真度。作者主张保留高容量、可解码的文本潜表示,让扩散模型直接学习其分布。

Method: AURORA-LM提出分离可解码文本表示的构建与分布建模:(1)基于Query的编码器-解码器将文本组织为高容量、前缀对齐的潜序列;(2)Block-causal Diffusion Transformer通过flow matching学习分布,从左到右生成块,同时并行去噪块内位置;(3)仅限制噪声输入路径而保留完整干净潜变量预测目标,避免减少解码器端容量;(4)根据潜变量宽度校准噪声水平分布;(5)引入自轨迹一致性(self-trajectory consistency)衔接训练时的独立采样噪声与推理时的迭代去噪。

Result: AURORA-LM在OpenWebText自由生成和XSum摘要任务上达到所有评测的连续及扩散语言模型中最强性能。扩展到1B参数(约1500 EFLOPs总计算量)获得进一步提升,在匹配的评测协议下超越更大的公开潜扩散语言模型。所有实验在Ascend NPU上完成。

Conclusion: AURORA-LM证明了不简化文本潜表示、而是设计扩散模型直接学习高容量可解码潜变量分布的有效性,为连续语言建模提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Language remains an outlier in generative modeling: while images, video, and audio are increasingly modeled in continuous latent spaces, text generation still relies predominantly on discrete tokens. Existing continuous language models either inherit embedding spaces not designed for joint generation and decoding, or compress autoencoded latents to ease diffusion, sacrificing token-level fidelity. Instead of simplifying the representation to suit the generative model, we preserve a high-capacity, decodable text latent and design the diffusion model to learn its distribution directly.
  We introduce AURORA-LM, a continuous-latent diffusion language model that separates the construction of a decodable text representation from the modeling of its distribution. A Query-based Encoder-Decoder organizes text into a high-capacity, prefix-aligned latent sequence, and a Block-causal Diffusion Transformer learns its distribution through flow matching, generating blocks left to right while denoising positions within each block in parallel. Because such a latent is harder for diffusion to model, AURORA-LM restricts only the noisy-input pathway while retaining the full clean-latent prediction target, accommodating full-width latents without reducing decoder-facing capacity. We further calibrate the noise-level distribution to the latent width, and introduce self-trajectory consistency to bridge independently sampled training noise and iterative denoising at inference.
  AURORA-LM achieves the strongest performance among evaluated continuous and diffusion-based language models on OpenWebText free generation and XSum summarization. Scaling to 1B parameters with about 1500 EFLOPs of total compute yields further gains, surpassing a larger publicly released latent-diffusion language model under a matched evaluation protocol. All experiments are conducted on Ascend NPUs.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Skillsets on the Chain: A Blockchain-based Zero-Trust Framework for Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00104)
*Yayu Gao,Yong Xiao,Hao Hu,Xubo Li,Zhiwei Liu,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustAgentNet，一种基于双链区块链的零信任框架，通过全局技能链（CoS）和瞬态协作链（CoC）解决代理AI网络中的技能声明一致性问题和安全漏洞，实验证明其在验证精度（100%）和自恢复能力上优于无区块链基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理AI网络系统严重依赖第三方技能集实现和分布式多代理协作，但在"默认信任"的假设下存在严重的声明-能力不一致性和安全漏洞，亟需一种零信任机制来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出双链结构的零信任框架TrustAgentNet：（1）全局技能链（Chain of Skillsets, CoS）通过专门代理执行链下审计并维持轻量级链上密码共识，管理技能集元数据的生命周期；（2）为任务动态建立瞬态协作链（Chain of Collaboration, CoC）实现去信任的多代理分布式协作；（3）提供安全级别、任务性能和资源开销三者间权衡的理论分析。存储上采用账本-IPFS集成设计降低链上成本。

Result: 在硬件原型实验中：相比无区块链的默认信任基线，TrustAgentNet的零信任开销主要由链下推断主导，区块链层通过账本-IPFS存储及链上/链下集成设计仅产生少量账本成本。验证流水线对50个AI模型实现100%准确率（40个诚实技能集全部正确验证，10个对抗性技能集全部被拦截）；对非AI领域（171个ClawHub技能、1478个特征）实现83.91%准确率和0.85 F1分数。对抗实验表明系统能实现技能集自主自恢复以应对各类恶意攻击。

Conclusion: TrustAgentNet有效缓解了代理AI网络中的信任问题，通过双链零信任设计实现了安全性与性能的平衡，在AI和非AI场景均展现出良好的验证能力和抗攻击鲁棒性，为下一代自主代理网络提供了可行的安全基础设施方案。

Abstract: Agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems rely heavily on third-party skillset implementations and distributed multi-agent collaboration, yet they face major claim-to-capability inconsistencies and security vulnerabilities under trust-by-declaration assumptions. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes TrustAgentNet, a dual-tier blockchain-secured zero-trust framework. Specifically, a global Chain of Skillsets (CoS) governs the lifecycle of skillset metadata with protocols empowered by specialized agents to enforce off-chain auditing while maintaining lightweight on-chain cryptographic consensus. Furthermore, transient, task-oriented Chains of Collaboration (CoC) are dynamically established to enable trustless distributed multi-agent collaboration. Theoretical analysis of the three-way trade-off among security level, task performance, and resource overhead is provided and empirically validated. Experimental results on a hardware prototype demonstrate that compared with no-blockchain trust-by-default baselines, the zero-trust overhead of TrustAgentNet is dominated by off-chain inference, while the blockchain layer incurs minor ledger costs via the ledger-IPFS storage and on/off-chain integration design. Crucially, the proposed verification pipeline achieves a flawless 100% accuracy across 50 AI models, correctly validating 40 honest skillsets and intercepting 10 adversarial ones, and generalizes to non-AI domains with an 83.91% accuracy and a 0.85 F1-score across 1478 features from 171 ClawHub skills. Adversarial experiments further show that TrustAgentNet enables autonomous skillset self-recovery against various malicious attacks.

</details>


### [130] [Deep Learning for Cyber Threat Detection and Mitigation in Healthcare-IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00118)
*Mirza Akhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向医疗物联网（H-IoT）的深度学习网络安全防御机制，包括三个真实数据集（两个DDoS二进制分类数据集和一个多类攻击数据集），以及轻量级TCN和Res-TCN模型，并通过量化至TFLite实现在Raspberry Pi 4上的边缘端低延迟、低功耗实时DDoS检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H-IoT系统中的可穿戴设备资源受限，安全故障直接危及患者安全。现有深度学习安全机制往往架构复杂、参数量大，且现有数据集鲜有质量评估，难以适用于H-IoT环境。

Method: 1) 开发多个真实数据集：UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025和UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025（在Cooja和ns-3模拟器中生成）用于DDoS二进制分类，UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025整合生理与网络特征用于SF、MITM和DDoS多类分类；2) 设计TCN和Res-TCN轻量级模型；3) TCN模型采用基于监测频率的检测机制和动态阈值缓解策略；4) 模型量化并转换为TFLite部署于Raspberry Pi 4实现边缘实时检测。

Result: 成功建立了涵盖真实数据集生成、轻量级模型设计和边缘部署的完整H-IoT网络安全防御体系，TCN模型在MQTT和UDP数据集上实现了DDoS的检测与缓解，并在Raspberry Pi 4上实现了低延迟、低功耗的实时检测。

Conclusion: 该研究为资源受限的H-IoT系统提供了从数据到部署的端到端深度学习安全解决方案，验证了轻量级模型结合边缘计算在保障患者安全方面的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is a fundamental requirement for protecting wearable devices used in healthcare Internet of Things (H-IoT) systems. Security failures in these resource-constrained systems directly compromise patient safety. Physiological data and network traffic are frequent targets of cyberattacks in H-IoT environments. To address these risks, deep learning-based cybersecurity mechanisms for H-IoT often involve complex architectures with large parameter counts. Existing datasets are also rarely assessed for quality, limiting their applicability. However, this research addresses these challenges by developing multiple realistic datasets and proposing lightweight deep learning models, namely the Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and Residual TCN (Res-TCN), for H-IoT. It includes two binary classification datasets for Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks and a multiclass dataset representing Selective Forwarding (SF), Man-in-the-Middle (MITM), and DDoS attacks. The datasets UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025 and UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025 are generated in Cooja and ns-3 to capture transmission behaviours and protocol variations. The third dataset, UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025, integrates physiological and network features to represent multiple cyber threats in H-IoT. Building on this, the TCN model is designed to detect and mitigate DDoS attacks over the MQTT and UDP-based datasets. It incorporates a monitoring frequency-based detection mechanism and a dynamic threshold-based mitigation strategy. To enable edge deployment, the model is quantised and converted into TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) for real-time DDoS detection on Raspberry Pi 4, achieving low latency and power-efficient operation in H-IoT. This thesis establishes a deep learning-based cybersecurity defence mechanism encompassing realistic dataset generation, lightweight model design, and edge deployment for securing H-IoT systems.

</details>


### [131] [Enforcing Access Control in Distributed Version Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00132)
*Xin Xu,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Shiran Pan,Liangqin Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Disac是一种为分布式版本控制系统（DVCS）设计的分布式访问控制方案，利用基于属性的加密（ABE）和基于属性的签名（ABS）实现读写权限控制，使每个实体独立执行访问控制并完全掌控自身数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制方案主要针对集中式版本控制系统（CVCS），依赖服务器强制实施访问控制；而分布式版本控制系统（DVCS）中每个实体都存储整个仓库、可从任何实体获取仓库并可自由共享自己的仓库，因此CVCS的访问控制方案不适用于DVCS，需要新的分布式访问控制方案。

Method: 提出Disac方案，利用基于属性的加密（ABE）实现读权限控制，利用基于属性的签名（ABS）实现写权限控制，每个实体独立执行访问控制而不依赖中央服务器。

Result: 通过对Git客户端的分析，证明Disac可以与DVCS（如Git）轻松集成。

Conclusion: Disac提供了一种适用于DVCS的分布式访问控制方案，使每个实体对自身数据拥有完全控制权，并能独立实施访问控制，且易于集成到实际DVCS系统中。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage the changes to various types of data. Unlike the CVCS where all the entities obtain the data from the server and the access control is enforced with the cooperation of the server, each entity in the DVCS stores the entire repository, obtains the repository shared by any entity and is free to share its own repository. Therefore, existing access control schemes for CVCS are not suitable for DVCS. In this paper, we present a distributed access control scheme (Disac) for DVCS. Disac makes each entity have full control over its data, while the access control is enforced at each entity independently. We adopt Attribute-based Encryption (ABE) and Attribute-based Signature (ABS) to achieve the read and write permission control. The analysis of the Git client demonstrates that Disac can be integrated easily.

</details>


### [132] [Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00134)
*Siyuan Li,Zehao Liu,Haoyu Li,Xi Lin,Ning Liu,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoopGuard，一个针对LLM多轮对抗攻击的主动防御框架，通过多智能体协作实现破坏（控制响应节奏）、误导（策略性模糊输出）和适应（取证分析）策略，在EMRA数据集上将ASR平均降低69%，同时将欺骗参与度提升了6倍以上，并增加攻击者令牌消耗198.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对抗攻击防御方法本质上是反应式的，难以应对在多轮交互中不断调整策略的高级对手。随着LLM被集成到复杂应用中，这种防御的局限性日益凸显，需要一种能够主动应对不断演化攻击的新型防御框架。

Method: 提出CoopGuard主动防御框架，采用合作式多智能体架构，各专业智能体执行互补的防御策略：(1) 受控响应节奏策略增加攻击成本；(2) 策略性模糊输出误导攻击者采用无效策略；(3) 基于交互日志的取证分析以识别攻击模式并完善防御。这些智能体由一个自适应协调机制动态调整防御策略以应对逐步升级的威胁。同时构建了EMRA数据集，包含5,200个多轮对抗样本和八种攻击类型。

Result: 在EMRA数据集上多个LLM骨干网络上的实验表明，CoopGuard相比评估的最先进基线将ASR平均降低69%；在抑制有害输出的同时保持欺骗性参与度，平均DR超过最强基线的6倍；攻击者令牌消耗平均增加198.83%。

Conclusion: CoopGuard通过主动式多智能体协作防御，有效应对演化中的多轮对抗攻击，在安全性（降低ASR）和防御博弈策略（维持欺骗参与、消耗攻击者资源）两方面均显著优于现有基线，为LLM对抗防御提供了从被动响应到主动博弈的新范式。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly integrated into complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks has raised significant concerns. However, existing defenses remain reactive in nature. This limitation makes it difficult for them to counter sophisticated threats, as adversaries continuously adjust their strategies across multi-turn interactions. In this paper, we present a proactive defense framework for securing LLMs against evolving multi-turn adversarial attacks that combines disruption, misdirection, and adaptation across successive interaction turns. In particular, it employs a cooperative multi-agent architecture in which specialized agents execute complementary defense strategies. These strategies include controlled response pacing to increase attack costs, strategically ambiguous outputs to mislead adversaries into ineffective strategies, and forensic analysis of interaction logs to identify attack patterns and refine defenses. These agents are coordinated by an adaptive mechanism that dynamically adjusts the defense strategy in response to escalating threats. To facilitate comprehensive evaluation, we present the EMRA dataset designed to simulate evolving strategies across multi-turn attacks, including 5,200 adversarial samples across eight attack types. Experimental results on EMRA across multiple LLM backbones show that the proposed framework reduces ASR by 69% on average relative to evaluated state-of-the-art baselines. Beyond suppressing harmful outputs, it sustains deceptive engagement, achieving an average DR more than six times that of the strongest baselines and increasing attacker-token consumption by 198.83% on average relative to evaluated baselines. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/CoopGuard.

</details>


### [133] [Symbolic Attack Chain Generation from Atomic Red Team Techniques: An Empirical Study of Predicate Representation Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00143)
*Ramya Varunsegar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了谓词表示粒度对自动化攻击链生成的影响，通过对比九类AALM模型与五类简化模型，发现计划有效性和成本对粒度不敏感，81.3%的结果相同，更高粒度主要增强计划内部结构分辨率而非攻击链的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化攻击链生成对现代网络安全至关重要，但手动构建无法随对手行为扩展。虽然基于PDDL的经典AI规划可自动化该过程，但依赖技术到符号谓词的准确翻译。现有最先进的AURORA系统采用九类攻击动作链接模型（AALM），但其特定粒度的必要性尚未得到验证，因此需要研究谓词表示粒度对规划有效性的影响。

Method: 采用流水线方法：LLM负责将攻击技术翻译为符号谓词，Fast Downward引擎执行确定性推理。对比完整的九类AALM方案与从Atomic Red Team（ART）执行证据中经验推导的五类简化方案，基于十六种技术的语料库进行评估。

Result: 在十六种技术语料库上，两种粒度方案产生81.3%的相同结果，计划有效性和成本对粒度变化基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 更高粒度的谓词表示主要增强计划内部结构（即计划正当性的解析度），而非提升生成攻击链本身的可行性，表明实践中可采用更简化的表示粒度以降低复杂度而不损失计划质量。

Abstract: Automated attack chain generation is critical for modern cybersecurity, yet manual construction fails to scale as adversary behaviors expand. While classical AI planning using PDDL offers a formal method to automate this process, it relies on the accurate translation of techniques into symbolic predicates. Current state-of-the-art systems like AURORA employ a nine-category Attack Action Linking Model (AALM), but the necessity of this specific granularity remains unvalidated. This work investigates the impact of predicate representation granularity on plan validity, cost, and fidelity. Utilizing a pipeline where a Large Language Model (LLM) performs translation and the Fast Downward engine performs deterministic reasoning, the study compares the full nine-category AALM against a reduced five-category scheme derived empirically from Atomic Red Team (ART) execution evidence.
