Results from a sixteen-technique corpus demonstrate that plan validity and cost are largely insensitive to granularity, with 81.3% identical outcomes across both schemes. The findings suggest that higher granularity primarily enhances the internal structural resolution of a plan's justification rather than the viability of the generated attack chain itself.

</details>


### [134] [Exposed by Design: A Dynamic Security Assessment of Internet-Facing MCP Servers at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00150)
*Nicolás Padilla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对互联网上暴露的MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器进行动态行为安全评估，通过11个数据源被动发现和自研框架Corvus的34个测试模块主动检测，在414台服务器中发现68个可报告漏洞，并揭示91.8%的服务器缺乏OAuth认证、687个工具实例暴露无控制的shell执行能力以及41.6%的服务器在三天内消失等安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP自2024年11月发布以来快速普及，公共互联网上已检测到超过21,000个服务器实例，但缺乏对实际部署中MCP服务器安全状况的系统性研究，需要首次进行动态行为安全评估来了解其真实威胁面。

Method: 结合11个数据源的被动发现策略（crt.sh、HuggingFace、GitHub、npm、Smithery、PyPI、Censys、FOFA、Shodan、glama.ai、pulsemcp.com）与基于自研框架Corvus的主动动态测试，Corvus实现34个测试模块覆盖10类MCP特定漏洞类别，在2026年7月期间进行四轮测量。

Result: 确认640台生产环境MCP服务器并动态审计414台，发现68个可报告漏洞，涵盖SQL注入、针对云元数据服务的SSRF、提示模板注入及通过游标操纵的路径遍历。91.8%的审计服务器缺乏OAuth认证，687个工具实例暴露未加访问控制的shell执行能力，41.6%的服务器在连续测量轮次间三天内消失，表明部署速度快但缺乏安全审查。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统的安全状况令人担忧，存在广泛的认证缺失和危险工具暴露问题，且服务器生命周期短、缺乏安全审核。作者建立了负责任披露流程，并将Corvus作为开源框架发布以促进MCP安全评估。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has seen rapid adoption since its November 2024 launch, with over 21,000 server instances detectable on the public internet. We present the first dynamic behavioral security assessment of internet-facing MCP servers, combining passive discovery across eleven data sources (crt.sh, HuggingFace, GitHub, npm, Smithery, PyPI, Censys, FOFA, Shodan, glama.ai, and pulsemcp.com) with active dynamic testing using Corvus, a purpose-built framework implementing 34 test modules covering 10 MCP-specific vulnerability classes. Across four measurement runs spanning July 2026, we confirm 640 production MCP servers and dynamically audit 414, uncovering 68 reportable vulnerabilities including SQL injection, SSRF targeting cloud metadata services, prompt template injection, and path traversal via cursor manipulation. We find that 91.8% of dynamically audited servers lack OAuth authentication, 687 tool instances across confirmed servers expose shell execution capabilities without access controls, and 41.6% of confirmed servers disappear within three days between consecutive measurement runs---indicating rapid deployment cycles without security review. We report on our responsible disclosure pipeline and release Corvus as an open-source framework for MCP security evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00202)
*Yashaswi Malla,Sandra Siby*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了基于LLM的Web多智能体系统（MAS）相对于单智能体系统（SAS）新增的结构性攻击面，提出了针对Web-MAS的攻击向量分类法（taxonomy），并构建了测试平台WebMASLab。作者提出了一种MAS特有的"Telephone Loop"攻击，利用跨智能体委派机制制造循环任务，该攻击在SAS中无效但对MAS有效，可在四个前沿模型中攻破三个（平均成功率80%），唯一抵抗的模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6）检出率达92%。研究还发现提示词加固等简单防御手段无法泛化推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Web智能体正从单智能体系统（SAS）向多智能体系统（MAS）演进。虽然MAS通过任务分解和子智能体专业化提升了性能，但这种角色分解引入了SAS中不存在的新结构性攻击面。然而，这一被扩展的攻击面目前缺乏系统的理解和分类，亟需开展针对性的安全研究。

Method: 作者提出了一个针对Web-MAS攻击向量的分类法，用于归类由多智能体参与引入或放大的漏洞。同时构建了WebMASLab测试平台，在保持用户任务、工具面和浏览器环境固定的条件下，对比单智能体和多智能体架构的安全性。实验在三种条件下（基线、提示词加固、启用推理）评估了三种对抗场景，并提出了全新的MAS特有攻击——Telephone Loop攻击，该攻击利用跨智能体委派机制制造循环任务链。

Result: Telephone Loop攻击对SAS无效但对MAS有效：在四个前沿模型中（Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.4），基线攻击成功率平均达80%，而第四个模型Claude Sonnet 4.6以92%的检出率成功抵御该攻击。其余模型在基线下的检出率为0%，提示词加固仅使一个模型达到33%的检出率。此外，提示词加固对不同模型效果差异巨大：一个模型的攻击成功率从100%降至8%，但对其他模型仅带来适度降低，说明简单防御手段不具备泛化能力。

Conclusion: 从SAS转向MAS不仅改变性能表现，也从根本上改变了Web智能体的安全态势。角色专业化在带来性能优化的同时，也可能引入新的架构级安全风险。现有简单防御措施（如提示词加固）无法在所有模型上泛化，需要进一步研究与设计针对MAS架构特性的专门防御方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based web agents are increasingly evolving from single-agent systems (SAS) to multi-agent systems (MAS). While MAS can lead to improved task performance by decomposing complex tasks across specialized sub-agents, such role decomposition introduces new structural attack surfaces that are absent in SAS. This expanded attack surface remains poorly understood and inadequately categorized.
  To address this, we propose a taxonomy to categorize attack vectors specific to web-based MAS, accounting for vulnerabilities introduced or amplified by the involvement of multiple agents. We further present a test-bed WebMASLab to analyze web agent security against a fully external, web-only adversary. To isolate the effect of architecture, we keep the user task, tool surface, and browser substrate fixed, and compare single- and multi-agent setups. We evaluate three adversarial scenarios, across three conditions (baseline, prompt-hardened, and reasoning-enabled), including a novel MAS-specific Telephone Loop attack that exploits cross-agent delegation to create cyclical task loops. The attack is inert against SAS but compromises MAS when powered by three of the four frontier models evaluated (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.4), averaging 80% across them at baseline. Only the fourth model, Claude Sonnet 4.6, resists the attack with a 92% detection rate. For the rest, the detection is 0% at baseline, reaching 33% with prompt-hardening for one model. We also show that obvious defenses do not generalize; prompt-hardening collapses one model's ASR from 100% to 8% while providing only modest reduction to the others. Our findings demonstrate that the transition from single- to multi-agent web systems changes the security landscape. Role specialization may not only lead to performance optimization but also introduce new architectural risks that require further study and defenses.

</details>


### [136] [Reflection, Education, Consistency: Towards Best Ethics Practices At Security And Privacy Conferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00282)
*Florian Hantke,Rafael Mrowczynski,Til Dralle,Ben Stock*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过回顾四大安全与隐私会议的历史伦理政策，并对20位资深与初级社区成员进行半结构化访谈，探讨了伦理审查程序的目标、现状与挑战，发现社区伦理教育缺乏是一大障碍，并提出社区层面的协调伦理指导以及开放伦理wiki作为第一步解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全与隐私社区中研究伦理是争议性且情绪化的话题，虽然顶级会议已引入强制性伦理章节等干预措施，但缺乏对伦理程序和干预措施的实证验证，也没有明确沟通的目标，因此需要建立共享理解和为会议层面的决策提供依据。

Method: 回顾四大顶级会议（top-four conferences）的历史伦理政策，并对20位资深和初级社区成员（包括部分现任或前任程序委员会及研究伦理委员会主席）进行深入半结构化访谈，探讨伦理程序的理由与目标、现有伦理干预措施，以及团队提出的新方法。

Result: 社区正朝着已识别的目标（提高伦理意识和防止发表不道德工作）演变，但社区内部缺乏伦理教育是一大障碍，伦理教育往往被推给大学。受访者还提到伦理政策复杂性、不同会议间缺乏一致性以及政策年际变动等挑战。多名参与者警告过度监管可能导致反弹，鼓励仅为合规（如使用LLM撰写伦理章节）而非深入思考。

Conclusion: 建议协调社区范围的伦理指导工作，并首次引入开放伦理wiki作为初步行动。

Abstract: Research ethics is a controversial and emotionally charged topic in the security and privacy community, sparking discussions at conferences and on social media. In recent years, some of the leading conferences have introduced interventions such as mandatory ethics sections, with mixed reactions within the community. Program committee chairs and steering committees increasingly emphasize ethics, yet there is limited empirical validation on ethics procedures and interventions, as well as no explicitly communicated goals.
  To support a shared understanding in our community and guide informed decisions at the conference level, we examined past ethics policies at the top-four conferences and conducted in-depth semi-structured interviews with senior and junior (n=20) community members, including some (former) chairs of program and research ethics committees. In these, we explored reasons for and goals of ethics procedures and discussed existing ethics interventions as well as possible new approaches proposed by our team.
  While the community appears to be evolving toward the identified goals, i.e., raising ethical awareness and preventing the publication of unethical work, we identified the lack of ethical education within the community itself as a major obstacle to further progress on this matter. Ethical education is often passed to the universities. Interviewees also mentioned challenges with the complexity of ethics policies and the lack of consistency across different conferences, as well as policies changing year-to-year. Several participants warned that over-regulation may lead to backlash, encouraging mere compliance, such as people turning to LLMs for ethics sections rather than undertaking in-depth consideration. In response, we suggest a coordinated, community-wide ethics steering effort and take a first step by introducing an open ethics wiki.

</details>


### [137] [Trimming: Decoupling Multiplicative Depth from Modulus Chains in RNS-CKKS via Rational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00375)
*John Chiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Trimming机制，通过引入辅助的、由更小NTT友好模数因子组成的修剪模数链，在RNS-CKKS中实现有理数级别的精细粒度管理，从而将乘法深度从模数链结构中解耦出来，突破了传统整数级模数链对乘法深度的根本性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Grafting工作已将缩放因子从模数中解耦，实现了更灵活的精度管理，但RNS-CKKS的乘法深度仍然从根本上受限于模数链的结构。这构成了同态加密架构中的一个关键瓶颈，需要一种新的机制来解耦乘法深度与模数链。

Method: 提出Trimming机制，核心思想是引入一条由更小NTT友好模数因子组成的辅助修剪模数链，实现部分模数转换而非直接丢弃整个模数因子。通过用细粒度的模数因子细化替代传统的离散级别降低，Trimming提供了超越传统整数模数链表示的有理数级别抽象。

Result: 该方法保持与现有RNS-CKKS算术运算的兼容性，同时实现更灵活的深度管理和自适应模数转换。论文指出该框架将通过具体实现和实验评估进一步验证其在实际同态加密应用中的性能表现和计算开销。

Conclusion: Trimming与Grafting互补，Grafting解决精度瓶颈，Trimming解决深度瓶颈，共同推动实现完全解耦的RNS-CKKS架构。

Abstract: Recent work on Grafting decouples scale factors from ciphertext moduli, enabling more flexible precision management in RNS-CKKS. However, the multiplicative depth remains fundamentally constrained by the modulus chain structure.
  In this paper, we propose \emph{Trimming}, a novel fine-grained level management mechanism that decouples multiplicative depth from modulus chains in RNS-CKKS via rational levels. The key idea is to introduce an auxiliary trimming modulus chain composed of smaller NTT-friendly modulus factors, which enables partial modulus transitions instead of directly discarding an entire modulus factor. By replacing conventional discrete level reductions with fine-grained modulus factor refinement, Trimming provides a rational-level abstraction beyond the traditional integer-based modulus chain representation.
  Our approach preserves the compatibility with existing RNS-CKKS arithmetic while enabling more flexible depth management and adaptive modulus transitions. Similar to Grafting, which addresses the precision bottleneck, Trimming targets the depth bottleneck in RNS-CKKS and contributes toward a fully decoupled RNS-CKKS architecture.
  The proposed framework will be further validated through concrete implementation and experimental evaluation to investigate its practical performance and computational overhead in real-world homomorphic encryption applications.

</details>


### [138] [TI-StegoAlign: Channel-Guided Post-Training for Generative Text Steganography under Tokenization Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00382)
*Jiuan Zhou,Yuhao Xue,Yu Cheng,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TI-StegoAlign，一种通道引导的后训练框架，用于生成式文本隐写术，解决发送端与接收端token边界不同步导致的恢复率高估问题，实现100%接收端比特准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式文本隐写方法在发送端token上评估恢复率，但接收端只观察表面文本；去令牌化和重新令牌化会改变token边界、使编码状态失步，导致评估高估接收端恢复性能。现有补救方法依赖推理时过滤或验证，仅修正单个输出，未将生成策略适配到接收端通道。

Method: 提出TI-StegoAlign后训练框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 比特一致监督目标（BCSO）在发送端嵌入位置扩大局部编码裕度；2) 通道条件偏好优化（CCPO）使用接收端真实恢复、文本质量和反隐写分析反馈来对齐完整隐写文本。仅更新LoRA参数，通信时无需令牌化特定修正。

Result: 实验结果显示100%的接收端比特准确率；相比最强基线，归一化困惑度偏差降低21.6%，反隐写分析性能相对提升6.3%。

Conclusion: TI-StegoAlign通过通道引导的后训练框架有效解决发送端与接收端token状态失步问题，在不依赖推理时修正的情况下实现了接收端完美比特恢复，同时保持文本质量和隐写安全性。

Abstract: Generative text steganography enables LLM agents to exchange secret information through task-relevant messages. Yet most methods evaluate recovery on sender-side tokens, whereas the receiver observes only surface text. Detokenization and receiver-side retokenization can alter token boundaries, desynchronize coding states, and cause such evaluation to overestimate receiver-side recovery. Existing remedies rely on inference-time filtering or verification, correcting individual outputs without adapting the generation policy to the receiver-side channel. To address these limitations, we propose TI-StegoAlign, a channel-guided post-training framework. The Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective (BCSO) enlarges local coding margins at realized sender-side embedding positions. Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization (CCPO) then aligns complete stegotexts using receiver-realistic recovery, text quality, and anti-steganalysis feedback. TI-StegoAlign updates only LoRA parameters and requires no tokenization-specific correction during communication. Experimental results show 100% receiver bit accuracy. Compared with the strongest baselines, TI-StegoAlign achieves a 21.6% reduction in normalized perplexity deviation and a 6.3% relative improvement in anti-steganalysis performance.

</details>


### [139] [Auditable Release Control for Pedagogical Leakage in LLM Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00515)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了LLM教师中的"教学泄漏"(pedagogical leakage)问题——模型在授权之前就泄露答案或关键推理。作者提出了一种"授权感知的完全中介边界"(authorization-aware complete-mediation boundary)，通过选择器、策略门控、渲染器三重架构和单一发布函数实现可审计的披露控制。实验显示严格中介可将泄漏标志从181降至0，但以降低有用性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型辅导系统虽然可以给出正确且有帮助的回答，但它们可能在未被授权的情况下泄露答案或决定性推理过程，这是一种依赖状态和动作的失败模式。现有方法缺乏对这种泄露的正式定义和系统化控制机制，需要一种可审计、可归属的发布边界来防止未经授权的信息泄露。

Method: 提出三元架构：选择器(selector)发出五种披露契约之一，策略门控(policy gate)保护特权模式，渲染器(renderer)生成语言。核心是单一发布函数(felease function)，应用可检查的检查项、可选累积验证和动作特定回退；可重放轨迹区分选择、生成、验证和执行四类失败。此外还引入匹配组件归因(matched component attribution)暴露安全-效用前沿。

Result: 在599个固定Gemini 3.5提案上，严格中介使三模型小组多数投票的泄漏标志从181降至0(配对问题簇差异-30.22分，95%CI[-35.00,-25.72])，但替换了581个响应且降低了有用性。仅检查器触发回退产生11个多数标志；添加语义验证器产生14个，无可靠边际增益。全局A1脚手架实现0多数标志和54个任意评委标志，在自动安全性和效用上优于拟合Q。外部时间戳复制实验(40个未见问题簇、480个攻击序列)中，高保证发布将多数标志从42降至8(-7.08分，95%CI[-13.13,-2.29])，仍有7个失败持续存在，1个被引入，平均有用性下降0.192。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可审计的发布边界和在声明契约下的失败归属机制，而非宣称实现通用语义安全或学习增益。严格的授权感知中介可以大幅减少教学泄漏，但存在安全-效用权衡，且完全消除泄露仍具挑战性。

Abstract: Large language model tutors can be correct and helpful yet disclose an answer or decisive reasoning before that disclosure is authorized. We formalize this state- and action-dependent failure as pedagogical leakage and introduce an authorization-aware complete-mediation boundary. A selector emits one of five disclosure contracts, trusted policy gates privileged modes, and a renderer proposes language. A single release function applies inspectable checks, optional cumulative verification, and action-specific fallback; replayable traces separate selection, generation, verification, and enforcement failures.
  Matched component attribution exposes a safety-utility frontier. On 599 fixed Gemini 3.5 proposals, strict mediation reduces blinded three-model panel-majority leakage flags from 181 to 0 (paired problem-cluster difference -30.22 points, 95% CI [-35.00,-25.72]), while replacing 581 responses and lowering helpfulness. Checker-triggered fallback alone yields 11 majority flags; adding the semantic verifier yields 14 and no reliable marginal gain. A global A1 scaffold yields 0 majority and 54 any-judge flags, outperforming fitted Q on automatic safety and utility. In an externally timestamped replication over 40 unseen problem clusters and 480 attack sequences, high-assurance release reduces majority flags from 42 to 8 (-7.08 points, 95% CI [-13.13,-2.29]); seven failures persist, one is introduced, and mean helpfulness falls by .192. These results establish an auditable release boundary and failure attribution under declared contracts, not universal semantic safety or learning gains.

</details>


### [140] [Exploring Usability and Legal Practice: Insights from German Judicial Users of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00541)
*Tobias Hoppmann,Leona Lassak,M. Angela Sasse,Zinaida Benenson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过调查德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州101名司法从业人员，将数字取证视为刑事案件诉讼程序中的社会技术系统，结果表明存在对数字取证工作流整合、跨领域沟通和利益相关者期望对齐的强烈需求，并揭示了数字取证可用性改进的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然先前研究主要关注数字取证工具和方法，但其更广泛的程序性和组织性背景受到的关注有限。数字取证已日益成为现代刑事诉讼不可或缺的部分，但因数据量增长、技术演进以及技术与法律利益相关者之间的复杂互动，其有效整合仍面临挑战。本文旨在从社会技术系统角度填补这一研究空白。

Method: 将数字取证概念化为刑事诉讼程序中的社会技术系统，基于可用性研究和以人为本的安全视角，对德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州司法部门的101名从业者（包括检察官、法官和数字取证专家）进行调查研究。

Result: 调查结果显示：对数字取证更好地整合进工作流程有强烈需求；跨领域沟通需要增强；利益相关者的期望需要更紧密对齐。同时发现了数字取证可用性改进的巨大潜力，如加强跨学科合作、更便捷快速地获取证据数据和结果、改进利益相关者的培训和教育工作。

Conclusion: 数字取证在刑事诉讼中的有效应用不仅依赖技术和工具本身，更需要从社会技术系统视角出发，关注程序流程整合、跨领域沟通、利益相关者协调以及可用性提升等组织层面的因素。

Abstract: Digital forensics has become an integral part of modern criminal proceedings, yet its effective integration remains challenging because of increasing data volumes, evolving technologies, and complex interactions between technical and legal stakeholders. Although prior work has focused primarily on digital forensic tools and methods, its broader procedural and organizational context has received limited attention. Building on emerging perspectives inspired by usability research and human-centered security, we conceptualize digital forensics as part of a socio-technical system within criminal proceedings. We consequently investigate this perspective through a survey of 101 practitioners from the judiciary of the German federal state of North Rhine-Westphalia, including public prosecutors, judges, and digital forensic experts. The results indicate a strong demand for improved integration of digital forensics into workflows, enhanced cross-domain communication, and a closer alignment of stakeholder expectations. They also uncover great potential for the improvement of digital forensics usability, e.g., through stronger interdisciplinary cooperation, easier and faster access to evidential data and results, or improvement of stakeholder training and education.

</details>


### [141] [Robust Watermarks Meet Backdoored Models: Evading Diffusion Semantic Watermarks via Stealthy Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00543)
*Jinyuan Liu,Tianshuo Cong,Pei Li,Tianrui Wang,Xinlei He,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostVAE，通过在潜在扩散模型（LDMs）的VAE编码器中植入隐蔽后门，实现对语义水印检测系统的可靠规避攻击。GhostVAE通过功率谱正则化构建通用触发器，并采用参数对齐目标训练带有后门的VAE编码器，在保持正常图像水印检测性能的同时，可高效绕过水印检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义水印被视为保护LDM生成图像的有效安全措施，但其检测流水线依赖神经网络，这引入了一个关键但尚未被充分探索的后门攻击面。现有研究忽视了攻击者可能通过篡改水印检测管道中的神经网络组件来规避检测的可能性。

Method: GhostVAE采用两阶段方法：(1) 通过功率谱正则化构造通用触发器，以增强触发器的鲁棒性；(2) 使用参数对齐目标训练带有后门的VAE编码器。该方法在保持原始水印检测性能的同时，通过植入的触发器实现水印规避。

Result: 在三种先进的语义水印方案和三种广泛采用的LDM上，GhostVAE在良性图像上保持94.4%的平均真阳性率，同时在触发器激活下达到94.6%的平均攻击成功率。此外，对17种代表性防御方法进行了全面分析，证明GhostVAE在输入空间、参数空间和潜在空间均保持隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 该工作从根本上削弱了语义水印系统的可信度，强调安全的语义水印部署需要端到端的安全考量，特别是针对神经网络组件。这项研究揭示了一个严重的安全漏洞，即水印检测所依赖的神经网络本身可能成为攻击目标。

Abstract: Although semantic watermarking is considered a promising safeguard for images generated by Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs), the reliance of the watermark detection pipeline on neural networks introduces a critical yet underexplored backdoor attack surface. To systematically study this vulnerability, we propose GhostVAE to plant a stealthy backdoor into the encoder of Variational Autoencoder (VAE), enabling reliable evasion of watermark detection. GhostVAE operates in two stages: it first constructs a universal trigger via power spectrum regularization to improve the trigger robustness, and then trains a backdoored VAE encoder with a parameter-aligned objective. Through extensive evaluations across three state-of-the-art semantic watermarking schemes and three widely adopted LDMs, we show that GhostVAE preserves watermark detection performance on benign images (achieving an average true positive rate of 94.4%), while simultaneously enabling highly effective evasion under trigger activation (achieving an average attack success rate of 94.6%). Moreover, we comprehensively analyze seventeen representative defenses and demonstrate that GhostVAE remains stealthy across the input space, parameter space, and latent space. Our work fundamentally undermines the trustworthiness of semantic watermarking systems and highlights that secure deployment of semantic watermarks requires end-to-end security considerations, particularly for neural network components.

</details>


### [142] [A False Average: Chain-of-Thought Monitors Collapse Where They Are the Only Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00583)
*Shikhar Shiromani,Leo Richter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，通过仅重写智能体的推理过程（使其看起来像善意的工程实践），同时逐字保留所有命令和输出，可以破坏基于思维链（CoT）的监控。这种免梯度的单次攻击将监控器的捕获率从约95%降至不足11%，并且攻击可跨监控器家族和智能体模型迁移，还能在真实智能体上复现。只有轨迹外信息才能大幅恢复检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控本意是捕捉那些行为表面上干净、仅在推理中暴露的奖励黑客行为（reward hacking）。作者想揭示这一范式本身的脆弱性：当对手控制推理内容时，是否可以通过只改写推理文本、保留实际行为不变来规避监控。

Method: 攻击者仅重写智能体的推理文本，使其看起来像正常的工程思维，同时逐字节复制所有命令和输出（保证恶意行为完全不变），从而实现一次免梯度（gradient-free）的单一改写攻击。作者在多个监控器家族和智能体模型上进行评估，并引入校准监控器、仅轨迹防御和激活探针等方法进行深入分析。

Result: 持有监控器在攻击后对该子集的捕获率从约95%暴跌至11%以下。监控器的总体准确率是虚假平均值——被行为本身暴露的黑客案例主导，掩盖了推理重写导致的近乎完全崩溃。攻击跨监控器家族和智能体模型迁移，并能在真实智能体上复现；针对校准监控器，规避行为集中在最强智能体。仅轨迹防御只能部分恢复，即使针对攻击预训练也不行；只有轨迹外信息能大幅改善。激活探针可分离监控器漏判的黑客，但因果对照表明这是探测器而非监控器"暗中知晓"的证据。

Conclusion: CoT监控在对手能控制推理的情况下具有根本性脆弱性，因为重写后的推理对事件保持真实、仅对意图撒谎，使仅轨迹防御难以奏效。核心防线必须依赖轨迹外信息。即使监控器内部表示中存在可分离信号，也不能证明其具备隐蔽的知识，需要因果方法验证。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is meant to catch the reward hacks that look clean in the actions and betray themselves only in the reasoning. We show that this is exactly where an adversary who controls the reasoning can defeat it. Rewriting only an agent's reasoning to read as good-faith engineering, while copying every command and output verbatim so the exploit is unchanged, drops a held-out monitor's catch rate on that subset from about 95% to under 11% in one gradient-free shot. A monitor's aggregate accuracy is a false average: dominated by hacks the actions give away, it hides the near-total collapse this rewrite produces on the subset where CoT monitoring is the only signal. The attack transfers across monitor families and agent models, reproduces with live agents, though against a calibrated monitor evasion concentrates in the strongest agent. Trace-only defenses recover it only partially, even one primed on the attack, because the rewrite stays truthful about what happened and lies only about intent; only information from outside the trace helps substantially. A probe on an open-weight surrogate monitor's activations separates the hacks its verdict misses, but a causal control shows this is a detector, not evidence the monitor secretly knows.

</details>


### [143] [Domain Decoupling Attack: Exploiting the Validation Gap Between Protective DNS and Shared Edge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00643)
*Weizhe Wang,Minhong Dong,Jinhao Li,Yao Zhang,Hao Liu,Qiang Hu,Tao Luo,Guangquan Xu,Bin Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出域名解耦攻击（DDA），利用DNS授权验证漏洞，通过解析允许域名获得共享边缘IP的权限，然后在SNI和Host字段中一致地呈现隐藏域名，从而绕过CDN和共享主机环境的访问控制。大规模测量显示95.8%的域名暴露于此类风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CDN的逃避技术依赖SNI-Host不一致、域名所有权验证不足或供应商特定路由重写，在现代CDN环境中适用性受限。研究者发现DNS授权存在验证缺口：来自允许域名的权限作用于共享IP，可被复用到达其他租户，需要在CDN和非CDN共享主机环境中揭示这一安全风险。

Method: 识别DNS授权验证中的逻辑漏洞，提出域解耦攻击（DDA）：先将允许域名解析到共享边缘IP以获得合法权限，随后在TLS SNI和HTTP Host字段中一致地呈现隐藏域名连接到同一地址。在六大洲对1,069,048个域名进行大规模测量，并进行实验室实验验证DNS绑定访问控制的结构性局限。

Result: 共产生18,025,068次成功探测；总体暴露率95.8%，CDN域名暴露率99.26%，非CDN域名暴露率92.75%，非CDN跨租户IP暴露率97.7%。实验室实验证实共享地址上DNS绑定访问控制在结构上存在缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明DNS派生的IP授权所面临的安全风险，支持CDN和非CDN共享托管环境中访问控制机制的评估与改进。

Abstract: Network attackers often conceal malicious communication within legitimate Internet traffic. Existing CDN-based evasion techniques rely on SNI--Host inconsistency, insufficient domain ownership verification, or provider-specific routing rewrites, which limit their applicability in modern CDN environments. We identify a validation gap in DNS-based authorization, where permission derived from an allowed domain applies to a shared IP and can be reused to reach another tenant in both CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments. This paper presents the Domain Decoupling Attack (DDA), which resolves an allowed domain to obtain permission for a shared edge IP and subsequently connects to the same address while presenting the hidden domain consistently in both TLS SNI and HTTP Host. Measurements of 1,069,048 domains across six continents produce 18,025,068 successful probes and identify exposure rates of 95.8% overall, 99.26% for CDN domains, 92.75% for non-CDN domains, and 97.7% for non-CDN cross-tenant IPs, while laboratory experiments reveal a structural limitation of DNS-bound access control on shared addresses. These results clarify the security risks of DNS-derived IP authorization and support the evaluation and improvement of access-control mechanisms in CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments.

</details>


### [144] [Improving the Security of Containerized Workloads using Transparency and Traceability Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00660)
*Nikos Fotiou,Lefteris Georgiadis,Ignacio Lacalle,George C. Polyzos,Vasilios A. Siris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可验证的容器镜像分发架构，通过透明性服务生成一次性签名密钥并记录签名事件于追加式透明注册表中，在准入时通过策略即代码评估加密可验证的包含证明和身份属性，确保只有合规的制品被部署。作者在GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners上实现了概念验证，并评估了其在现实威胁模型下对常见供应链攻击的缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器化工作负载通常通过CI/CD流水线构建并存储在注册表中，跨云和边缘等异构基础设施执行。单个被攻陷的构建步骤或凭据可导致恶意制品的规模化分发，因此需要完整性、透明性和可强制执行的部署时检查机制。

Method: 提出一种架构：(1) 透明性服务生成绑定到已认证身份的一次性签名密钥；(2) 在追加式透明注册表中记录签名事件；(3) 返回加密可验证的包含证明；(4) 证明和身份属性附加到镜像元数据；(5) 在准入时由策略即代码评估，仅允许合规制品部署。实现概念验证并集成GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners。

Result: 实现了一个与GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners集成的概念验证系统，评估了该流水线在现实威胁模型下对常见供应链攻击的缓解能力。

Conclusion: 该架构通过透明注册表、一次性密钥绑定和策略准入控制，有效增强了容器镜像分发供应链的安全性，为CI/CD环境中的部署验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Containerized workloads are commonly built via CI/CD pipelines, stored in registries, and executed across heterogeneous infrastructures, including cloud and edge environments. A single compromised build step or credential can turn routine automation into large-scale distribution of malicious artifacts, motivating integrity, transparency, and enforceable deployment-time checks. In this paper, we present an architecture for verifiable container image distribution that addresses key-management challenges and enables policy-enforced admission-time verification. A transparency service generates one-time signing keys bound to authenticated identities, records signing events in an append-only transparency registry, and returns cryptographically verifiable proofs of inclusion. These proofs and identity attributes are attached to image metadata and evaluated by policy-as-code at admission time, so only compliant artifacts are deployed. We implement a proof-of-concept integrated with GitHub Actions and GitLab Runners and evaluate how the resulting pipeline mitigates common supply-chain attacks under a realistic threat model.

</details>


### [145] [From Chasing Ghosts to Missed Attacks: Perspectives and Perceptions of SOC Practitioners on LLM Integration, Risks, and Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00672)
*Jonas Thurner,Nadine Jost,Stefan Albert Horstmann,Fabian Ising,Lea Groeber,Alena Naiakshina,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对25位有LLM使用经验的安全运营中心（SOC）从业者进行半结构化访谈，识别出15个LLM用例（归为6个功能类别），发现LLM擅长自动化重复性低层任务（如报告自动化），但在高影响任务（如事件分析）上仍不可行，主要受限于技术深度、上下文意识和组织特定知识不足。从业者虽表达了对LLM的强采纳意愿（受竞争压力驱动），但指出限制更多在于SOC组织就绪度和人类过度依赖因素，而非模型本身。文章最终提出了人本、操作安全的LLM辅助安全运营设计及集成要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC分析师需在时间压力下处理海量安全事件并准确检测、评估网络攻击。尽管LLM在安全运营中显示出潜在价值，但其在实际SOC工作流中的适用性仍缺乏深入理解，本研究旨在填补这一实证空白，探索LLM在真实SOC环境中的负责任集成方式。

Method: 采用质性研究方法：对25名具有LLM使用经验的SOC从业者进行半结构化访谈，并结合交互式场景以预期挑战、识别机会，从而获取对LLM工具在SOC工作流中整合的实证洞察。

Result: 识别出15个LLM用例，归入6个功能类别。LLM在自动化重复性低层任务（如报告自动化）中获好评，但从业者评估高影响任务（如事件分析）尚不可行，指出技术深度、上下文意识及组织特定知识方面的不足。限制根因更多在于SOC就绪度与人类过度依赖，而非模型本身。从业者仍表现出强烈采纳意愿，受竞争压力驱动。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了跨SOC角色与组织的LLM使用实证、从业者驱动分析，并据此推导出面向人本、操作安全的LLM辅助安全运营的具体设计与集成要求。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) process large volumes of security events, requiring analysts to accurately detect and assess ongoing cyberattacks under time pressure. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest potential benefits for security operations, yet their practical suitability for real-world SOC workflows remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we conducted 25 semi-structured interviews with SOC practitioners who had prior experience with LLMs, complemented by interactive scenarios to anticipate challenges and identify opportunities for the responsible integration of LLM-based tools into SOC workflows. We identified 15 LLM use cases grouped into six functional categories. While LLMs are valued for automating repetitive, low-level tasks such as report automation, practitioners rate high-impact tasks such as incident analysis as not yet feasible, reporting limitations in technical depth, context awareness, and organization-specific knowledge. They locate these limitations less in the models than in the readiness of their SOCs and human factors driving over-reliance. Despite concerns, practitioners express a strong willingness to adopt LLMs, describing competitive pressure that leaves few alternatives. This work contributes an empirical, practitioner-driven analysis of LLM use across SOC roles and organizations and derives concrete design and integration requirements for human-centered, operationally safe LLM-assisted security operations.

</details>


### [146] [Supporting Cybersecurity Risk Management for Medical Devices via the SECUMAN Ontology and Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00698)
*Martin Diller,Anne Esslinger,Piotr Gorczyca,Evi Hartig,Lia Kacholdt,Hannes Strass*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SECUMAN本体和SHACL形状，用于表示和分析医疗器械的网络安全风险管理文档。该本体基于OWL构建正式词汇表，通过SHACL约束检查文档结构完整性和一致性，并与VDE Spec 90025及RISKMAN本体对齐，将安全导向方法扩展到网络安全风险文档相关概念，支持自动化一通过验证、可追溯性及安全与网络安全风险管理文档的整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着连网医疗设备日益普及，网络安全风险直接威胁患者安全，但现有风险管理文件多以半结构化自然语言维护，导致一致性检查、认证审查和复用困难，亟需形式化的表示方法。

Method: 构建基于OWL的SECUMAN本体，形式化建模网络安全风险的上下文、评估、控制措施和残余风险评价，并利用SHACL约束检查文档结构完整性和模型符合性，同时与VDE Spec 90025和RISKMAN本体及形状对齐，扩展威胁场景、保护目标、攻击者画像、暴露级别、资产和安全设计论证等网络安全相关概念。

Result: SECUMAN本体和形状能够对医疗器械网络安全风险管理文档进行形式化表示和自动验证，支持结构完整性检查、可追溯性以及网络安全与安全风险管理文档的集成。

Conclusion: SECUMAN为医疗器械网络安全风险管理文档提供了一种基于本体的形式化建模与自动验证方案，有助于提升认证审查效率和文档一致性，促进网络安全与安全风险管理的整合。

Abstract: We propose the SECUMAN ontology and shapes for representing and analysing cybersecurity risk-management documentation for medical devices. Cybersecurity risks are increasingly relevant for connected medical devices and may have direct consequences for patient safety. Current risk-management files are often maintained as semi-structured natural language text, which makes consistency checking, certification review, and reuse difficult. SECUMAN provides a formal OWL-based vocabulary for modelling security-risk context, assessment, control measures, and residual-risk evaluation, and uses SHACL constraints to check structural completeness and conformity with the intended documentation model. The ontology is aligned with VDE Spec 90025 and the related RISKMAN ontology and shapes, while extending their safety-oriented approach to concepts relevant to cybersecurity risk documentation such as threat scenarios, protection goals, attacker profiles, exposure levels, assets, and secure design arguments. SECUMAN is intended to support automated first-pass validation, traceability, and integration of cybersecurity and safety risk-management documentation.

</details>


### [147] [Adversarial Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines: Unveiling Structural Vulnerabilities in Agentic AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00718)
*Faisal Haque Bappy,Tahrim Hossain,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Raiful Hasan,Kamrul Hasan,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了多智能体LLM流水线因缺乏边界验证（boundary verification）而存在结构性安全漏洞，这些漏洞源于跨智能体边界传递数据时缺少显式验证机制，导致内容注入、智能体冒充、计划偏离和记忆投毒等攻击面。通过GAIA和SWE-Bench基准的注释生产轨迹以及受控多智能体实验（GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Kimi K2.5），证明攻击成功率与流水线结构而非模型能力相关，表明对抗性漏洞本质上是架构属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要聚焦单智能体场景，忽视了多智能体LLM流水线中独有的安全缺口：一旦智能体接受对抗性内容，该内容会作为可信输入在整个流水线中传播。作者认为这一漏洞源于缺失边界验证这一安全原语——即数据跨越智能体边界时应执行的内容、身份、执行意图和状态完整性的显式验证。这一出发点旨在揭示流水线中隐式信任假设的不可防御性。

Method: 首先利用GAIA和SWE-Bench基准的注释生产轨迹，证明这些漏洞在良性部署中实际存在且能逃避现有评估框架；然后在受控多智能体环境中操作化这些失效模式，并在完全相同的流水线配置下对GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Kimi K2.5三个模型进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，攻击成功率与流水线结构对齐而非模型能力对齐，即无论底层模型能力如何，相同流水线构型展现出一致的弱点模式。这表明对抗性漏洞本质上是架构属性，而非模型缺陷。

Conclusion: 多智能体流水线的安全脆弱性是架构性而非模型性的，因此防御策略应从单点模型加固转向流水线层面的防御，特别是引入显式的边界验证机制以恢复跨智能体的数据可信度。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines orchestrate multiple specialized language model agents into structured workflows where intermediate outputs are passed across agents to solve complex tasks. This design introduces a security gap absent in single-agent settings: once an agent accepts adversarial content, it is propagated as trusted input throughout the pipeline. We argue that this vulnerability stems from the absence of boundary verification, a security primitive that enforces explicit validation of data as it crosses inter-agent boundaries, including content, identity, execution intent, and state integrity. Without such verification, modern pipelines embed implicit trust assumptions that are not adversarially robust, giving rise to structurally distinct attack surfaces (e.g., content injection, agent impersonation, plan deviation, and memory poisoning). Leveraging annotated production traces from the GAIA and SWE-Bench benchmark, we show that these vulnerabilities arise in benign deployments and largely evade existing evaluation frameworks. We further operationalize these failure modes within a controlled multi-agent setting and evaluate them across GPT-5-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Kimi K2.5 under identical pipeline configurations. The results reveal that attack success aligns with pipeline structure rather than model capability, indicating that adversarial vulnerability is fundamentally an architectural property and motivating a shift toward pipeline-level defenses.

</details>


### [148] [Safety Invariants for Agents Orchestrating Irreversible State Transitions: A Four-Dimensional Formalism Evaluated on Public Ledgers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00783)
*Zhaoming Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对自主智能体在外部系统上产生不可逆副作用（如转账、写入持久化存储、控制硬件）时的安全问题，提出"执行保真度"（execution fidelity）的形式化保障：在给定的故障模型下，会话在账本上的实际效果要么为零，要么与向用户呈现的状态转移完全一致且恰好一次。文章从执行保真度推导出七个安全不变量，并在N=60的对抗性测试套件上验证，同时已在生产中部署，支撑了108次跨8条链的真实写操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（如ReAct、Reflexion、MCP）主要优化基准任务的成功率，忽视不可逆副作用的安全性。随着自主智能体越来越多地被要求对外部系统产生不可逆影响（资金转移、持久化存储写入、硬件驱动），需要一个形式化框架来保证这些副作用的安全性，而非仅依赖经验性列举的安全规则。

Method: 将公开账本上的价值移动形式化为四维空间中的状态转移（由钱包、链、地址、协议索引）。声明并证明"执行保真度"定理，该定理在包含规划器映射错误、模糊结果、重试、至少一次投递及委托非人类调用者的故障模型下成立。从保真度条件推导出七个安全不变量，而非从经验枚举。构建了N=60的对抗性测试套件进行经验验证。

Result: 在对抗性测试中，该系统在两种写激进型底层模型上将通过率比naive-ReAct基线提升约74个百分点，但在写谨慎型模型上仅提升约3个百分点。系统已部署，108次跨8条链的生产写操作支撑了故障分类法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：执行保真度将"渲染的转移是否符合用户意图"这一无界问题压缩为对有限对象的单一谓词判断，使预览成为充分的安全控制手段。单模型评估智能体安全栈几乎不可证伪——需要在写激进与写谨慎模型上都进行测试。尽管评估场景是公开账本，该形式化框架和不变量适用于任何作用于不可逆外部状态的概率性智能体。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly asked to produce irreversible effects on external systems - transferring funds, writing to durable storage, actuating hardware. Existing agent frameworks (ReAct, Reflexion, MCP) optimize task success on benchmarks and give little attention to the safety of irreversible side-effects. We formalize one such setting, movement of value across public ledgers, as state transitions in a four-dimensional space indexed by (wallet, chain, address, protocol), and use that formalism to state and prove a guarantee we call execution fidelity: under a fault model admitting planner mis-mapping, ambiguous outcomes, retries, at-least-once delivery, and delegated non-human callers, a session's realized effect on the ledger is either nothing at all or exactly the transition that was rendered to the user, exactly once. The theorem deliberately does not claim that the rendered transition matches the user's intent - no runtime layer can decide that - but it confines that unbounded question to a single predicate over a finite object, which is what makes a preview a sufficient control rather than a formality. Seven safety invariants, derived from the fidelity condition rather than enumerated from experience, discharge the guarantee. Empirically, on a controlled N=60 adversarial suite the stack lifts pass rate by ~74 percentage points over a naive-ReAct baseline on two write-aggressive backing models, but by only ~3 points on a write-cautious one - evidence that single-model evaluations of agent safety stacks are close to unfalsifiable. The system is deployed; 108 production write operations across 8 chains back the failure taxonomy. Although the evaluation setting is public ledgers, the formalism and invariants apply to any probabilistic agent acting on irreversible external state.

</details>


### [149] [Hardware-rooted attestation for AI-agent evidence: composing IETF RATS with action evidence packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00801)
*Anton Sokolov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将软件层的"动作证据包"(AEP)与硬件信任根(RATS/Veraison)结合，构成复合证明方案，用以验证AI代理动作的真实性与模型部署版本的可信性。通过在软件TPM上进行的实验，展示了将AEP哈希与验证者nonce绑定到平台签名quote的可行性，并验证了Attested/Contested/Expired三种平台评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件层AEP只能证明"代理声称做了什么"，但当验证者需要确认"模型特定版本是否在未修改硬件上真正产生该输出"时，AEP无法回答。需要一个硬件信任根来证明平台引导与运行时状态与认可的参考一致，即RATS认证。

Method: 提出复合认证方案：将硬件证据（在RATS下评估）与软件AEP绑定。在软件TPM（swtpm仿真器）上实现输出绑定协议——将AEP结果哈希和新鲜nonce折叠进由认证密钥签名的quote中，模型工件测量值存入平台寄存器。使用最小化的RATS验证器替代程序解析三种平台结果。

Result: 实验验证了三种结果按设计工作：良好且新鲜的quote判定为Attested；模型测量被替换时判定为Contested；重放过期quote判定为Expired；绑定到有效quote上的伪造AEP结果被拒绝。但该结果是在仿真硬件上的可行性演示，并非硬件根保证。

Conclusion: 软件级认证（AEP）必要但不充分，需以硬件信任根（RATS/Veraison）补充。复合认证方案在仿真TPM上证明了概念可行性，后续需在真实硬件上进行验证以获得硬件级保证。

Abstract: An action evidence package (AEP) is a signed, append-only record of what an AI agent did, who or what authorised the action, and what the outcome was. It is a software-layer artefact: it tells a verifier the story of an action as the agent's own runtime reports it. This note argues that software attestation of this kind is necessary but not sufficient. When a verifier's question shifts from "what does the agent claim it did?" to "did the specific model version the operator claims to have deployed actually produce this output, on unmodified hardware?", the AEP alone cannot answer. The missing element is a hardware root of trust: an attestation that the measured boot and runtime state of the platform match an endorsed reference. The IETF Remote Attestation Procedures (RATS) architecture (RFC 9334) and Veraison, an open-source RATS Verifier implementation (Confidential Computing Consortium / Linux Foundation), supply exactly this. We propose a composite attestation: hardware Evidence appraised under RATS, bound to a software AEP. We map a small verifier vocabulary (Authorised / Unauthorised / Indeterminate / Attested / Contested / Expired) onto RATS appraisal outcomes, and demonstrate feasibility with a small executed experiment: on a software Trusted Platform Module (TPM; the swtpm emulator), an output-binding protocol folds the hash of an AEP outcome and a fresh appraiser nonce into an attestation-key-signed quote, with a model-artefact measurement carried in a platform register. A minimal RATS-Verifier stand-in resolves the three platform outcomes as designed -- Attested for a good, fresh quote; Contested when the model measurement is swapped; Expired when a stale quote is replayed -- and rejects a forged AEP outcome bound to a valid quote. The result is a feasibility demonstration on emulated hardware, not a hardware-rooted guarantee.

</details>


### [150] [XR-PRISM: Data-Driven Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric for Extended Reality in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00826)
*Nafisa Anjum,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文回顾了2017至2024年间65篇关于XR安全和隐私的同行评审研究，构建了包含设备、网络、用户和云四层的威胁分类体系及防御目录，并提出XR-PRISM评估框架——一种六因素加权隐私与风险影响评分指标，为医疗XR部署提供可量化的风险评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实（XR）技术在医疗保健领域广泛应用（如沉浸式训练、远程会诊、患者康复），但其广泛的感知能力和复杂的数据管线带来了独特的安全、隐私和安全隐患。现有研究缺乏一个统一的定量框架来评估和优先排序这些风险，亟需建立一个系统化的风险评估方法。

Method: 作者系统回顾了2017至2024年间发表的65篇关于XR安全和隐私的同行评审研究，综合合成一个四层威胁分类体系（设备层、网络层、用户层、云层）以及相应的防御目录。在此基础上，引入XR-PRISM——一个六因素加权的隐私与风险影响评分指标，整合威胁可能性、系统脆弱性、攻击面、安全影响、隐私影响和控制有效性为单一可操作的风险评分。

Result: 分析发现超过70%的已识别对策缺乏标准化的风险评估，而只有不到15%的已记录攻击需要高水平的专业知识来执行。XR-PRISM为研究人员和从业者提供了透明、数据驱动的风险比较、优先级排序和缓解方法。

Conclusion: XR-PRISM为医疗XR部署的安全和隐私风险管理提供了统一的、可量化的评估工具，弥补了现有研究缺乏标准化定量评估框架的空白，有助于推动医疗XR技术的安全落地。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) technologies are transforming healthcare through immersive training, remote consultation, and patient rehabilitation. However, their extensive sensing capabilities and complex data pipelines introduce distinct security, privacy, and safety risks. Existing research lacks a unified quantitative framework for assessing and prioritizing these risks. We review 65 peer-reviewed studies on XR security and privacy published from 2017 to 2024, synthesizing a four-layer threat taxonomy consisting of Device, Network, User, and Cloud layers, along with a corresponding catalog of defenses. Building on this analysis, we introduce XR-PRISM, a six-factor weighted Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric that integrates threat likelihood, system vulnerability, attack surface, safety impact, privacy impact, and control effectiveness into a single actionable risk score. Our analysis shows that more than 70% of the identified countermeasures lack standardized risk evaluation, while fewer than 15% of the documented attacks require a high level of expertise to execute. XR-PRISM provides researchers and practitioners with a transparent, data-driven method for comparing, prioritizing, and mitigating security and privacy risks in healthcare XR deployments.

</details>


### [151] [Explainable Hybrid Feature Selection for Intrusion Detection in Internet of Medical Things Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00869)
*Amira Berrezzek,Hayet Djellali,Giulio Mallardi,Lamia Mahnane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向IoMT网络的入侵检测系统，通过Pearson相关滤波、基于模型的特征重要性与SHAP归因相结合的混合特征选择策略，将特征数量从40个缩减至最少5个（压缩率达88%），在保持准确率和F1-score仅下降几个百分点的情况下，训练出轻量且可解释的Random Forest和LightGBM分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT（医疗物联网）网络面临严峻的安全防护挑战：设备异构、计算资源有限、流量需实时分析。传统入侵检测系统在此类受限环境下难以高效部署，需要在不牺牲检测性能的前提下大幅降低特征维度，以实现实时、可解释且资源友好的入侵检测。

Method: 采用两阶段特征选择流程：（1）Pearson相关系数滤波，去除冗余属性；（2）混合策略，将基于模型的特征重要性与SHAP归因相结合，选出紧凑的特征子集。随后在所选特征上训练Random Forest和LightGBM分类器，并用SHAP和LIME对保留特征的决策贡献进行解释。

Result: 在CIC-IoMT 2024和CIC-IDS 2017两个数据集上，特征空间被压缩最高达88%（从40个特征降至最少5个），同时准确率和F1-score与使用全部特征的模型相比仅相差几个百分点。

Conclusion: 紧凑且可解释的入侵检测器是资源受限医疗网络上切实可行的部署方案，在保持检测性能的同时满足了IoMT场景对轻量化和可解释性的需求。

Abstract: Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) networks are hard to protect: devices are heterogeneous, computing resources are scarce, and traffic must be analyzed in real time. We present an intrusion detection system that addresses these constraints through feature selection. A Pearson correlation filter first removes redundant attributes; a hybrid strategy then combines model-based feature importance with SHAP attribution to pick a compact subset, on which we train Random Forest and LightGBM classifiers. SHAP and LIME explain what each retained feature contributes to the decisions. On CIC-IoMT 2024 and CIC-IDS 2017, the method cuts the feature space by up to 88% - from 40 to as few as 5 features - and accuracy and F1-score stay within a few points of models trained on all features. Compact, interpretable detectors of this kind are practical candidates for deployment on resource-limited medical networks.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-LLM Consensus Framework for Evaluating Banking-Sector NIDS Dataset Coverage of MITRE ATT&CK Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00895)
*Sanjida Khanom,Sadia Afrin Khan,Adrita Rahman Tory,Md. Ahsan Habib,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向银行业的NIDS评估方法论，通过将MITRE ATT&CK知识库中的对手行为映射到现有NIDS基准数据集，并施加NIST SP 800-94定义的现实传感器约束，系统性评估5个基准数据集对银行关键攻击行为的覆盖能力。结果显示UNSW-NB15以82.2%的加权覆盖率表现最佳（但仅18.4%为直接技术级证据），而CIC-DDoS2019对核心银行行为存在89.9%的盲区，揭示了现有基准与真实银行环境之间的显著验证鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球银行网络的系统性关键性使其成为高级持续性威胁（APT）的高优先级目标，但现有NIDS模型在标准基准上的高准确率无法转化为真实银行环境的有效防护，因为通用数据集缺乏SWIFT、ATM等金融部门特有的入侵模式，存在实验性能与操作有效性之间的验证鸿沟。

Method: 采用部门感知的评估方法论：将MITRE ATT&CK知识库中记录的对手行为映射至NIDS基准数据集，同时遵循NIST SP 800-94的现实传感器限制约束；利用四个SOTA大语言模型构建多LLM共识引擎，评估210种银行特定对手技术，推导出68种网络可观测行为用于系统性覆盖分析。

Result: 对五个基准数据集的分析表明：UNSW-NB15以82.2%的加权覆盖率得分最高（但其中仅18.4%为直接技术级证据），CIC-DDoS2019对核心银行行为存在89.9%的盲区，其他数据集覆盖率参差不齐。

Conclusion: 研究建立了可复现的部门感知NIDS评估基础，凸显了开发银行原生数据集的紧迫需求，为银行业网络安全评估提供了更具操作有效性的评价框架。

Abstract: The systemic criticality of global banking networks has ren-dered them high-priority targets for advanced persistent threats, neces-sitating Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS) whose operational effectiveness must extend beyond statistical accuracy. However, a signif-icant validation gap persists between experimental NIDS performance and real-world effectiveness: NIDS models that achieve high accuracy on standard benchmarks often fail in operational banking environments because generic datasets lack sector-specific patterns, such as SWIFT and ATM-related intrusions, that characterize real financial threats. To address this, the paper investigates a sector-aware evaluation method-ology that systematically assesses how well existing NIDS benchmark datasets cover the attack behaviors most relevant to banking infrastruc-ture. The methodology maps documented adversary behaviors from the MITRE ATT&CK knowledge base to NIDS benchmarks while enforcing the realistic sensor limitations defined by NIST SP 800-94. Leveraging a multi-LLM consensus engine with four state-of-the-art models, we evalu-ated 210 banking-specific adversary techniques to derive a baseline of 68 network-observable behaviors for systematic coverage analysis. Results across five benchmark datasets demonstrate that UNSW-NB15 achieves the highest utility with an 82.2% weighted coverage score (though only 18.4% reflects direct, technique-level evidence), while CIC-DDoS2019 re-veals an 89.9% blind spot for core banking behaviors. These findings es-tablish a reproducible foundation for sector-aware NIDS evaluation and highlight the urgent need for banking-native datasets.

</details>


### [153] [A Decade of Healthcare Cyber Threats: Empirical Analysis, Evidence-Based Prioritisation, and AI Threat Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00901)
*Sadia Mehrin Rahi,Ayesha Siddika,Istiyak Hasan Maruf,Adrita Rahman Tory,Muhammad Aminur Rahaman,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于1,214条威胁记录（来自MITRE ATT&CK、CISA KEV和NIST NVD三个权威数据源）对2017至2024年间44个针对医疗保健领域的威胁实体进行了系统性分析，发现攻击者行为向隐蔽导向战术显著转变（如防御规避持续占主导、持久化和初始访问降至零），并揭示了现有检测指南与攻击者精力分布的结构性错位及可利用的共同瓶颈点，最终识别出42个高优先级检测机会并映射至AI集成临床系统的新兴威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗系统面临着持续且不断演变的网络威胁，但此前缺乏基于实证、多源数据对攻击者战术和技术随时间变化进行系统性刻画的研究。

Method: 整合MITRE ATT&CK行为框架、CISA已知被利用漏洞目录和NIST漏洞数据库三个权威来源的1,214条威胁记录，对2017至2024年间44个已验证的医疗保健定向威胁实体进行行为演变、检测覆盖对齐度、收敛分析和优先级识别。

Result: 防御规避一直是主导战术（占15-20%），持久化从11.2%降至零、初始访问从9.0%降至零；检测指南与攻击者精力分布结构性错位；679个已确认被利用漏洞收敛于单一主导行为技术，形成可寻址的共同瓶颈点；识别出42个高优先级技术并映射至AI集成临床系统的新兴威胁。

Conclusion: 医疗保健网络安全需要重新调整检测优先级，关注攻击者实际投入精力最多的隐蔽型战术，并将检测能力前置至AI集成临床系统等新兴威胁场景。

Abstract: Healthcare systems face persistent and evolving cyber threats, yet how adversarial tactics and techniques have shifted over time has not been systematically characterised using empirical, multi-source data. This paper analyses 1,214 threat records drawn from three authoritative sources: the MITRE ATT&CK behavioural framework, the CISA Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalogue, and the NIST vulnerability database, covering 44 validated healthcare-targeting threat entities from 2017 to 2024. We show that attacker behaviour has shifted measurably toward stealth-oriented tactics: defense evasion remained the dominant tactic throughout the observation period, consistently accounting for 15-20% of observed technique use from 2017 to 2024, while persistence declined from 11.2% to zero and initial access from 9.0% to zero over the same period. We further demonstrate that existing detection guidance is structurally misaligned with where attacker effort is concentrated, with the least-covered techniques receiving the most adversarial attention. A convergence analysis links 679 confirmed exploited vulnerabilities to a single dominant behavioural technique, identifying a common addressable chokepoint across the vulnerability and behavioural surfaces. Finally, we identify 42 high-priority techniques representing immediate detection opportunities and show that this set of techniques maps directly to emerging threats against AI-integrated clinical systems.

</details>


### [154] [Neuro-Symbolic Participation Governance for Verifiable AI Agents in Open Digital Twin Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00937)
*Juan Li,Wei Cai,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号去中心化治理框架，用于数字孪生生态系统中可验证的自治AI智能体。该框架通过多层语义代理配置、正式领域本体和基于区块链智能合约的凭证验证，在无敏感数据暴露的情况下确保身份、能力和策略合规，并已通过医疗决策支持原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在高风险决策中缺乏对身份、能力和策略合规的稳健验证，尤其在跨机构的去中心化环境中，难以支持可信的人机协作和有意义的人工监督。

Method: 提出神经符号去中心化治理框架：通过多层语义配置文件将概率神经推理与确定性制度治理桥接；能力基于正式领域本体进行接地，实现机器可解释、策略感知和上下文敏感参与；组织权威机构颁发凭证，并通过区块链智能合约验证，确保可审计且不泄露敏感数据。

Result: 在包含诊所、数字孪生和可穿戴设备提供方代理的决策支持原型演示中，该框架有效阻止了未授权交互，强制执行机构策略，且开销可控。

Conclusion: 神经符号去中心化治理为跨机构边界的安全生产人机协作提供了可扩展且值得信赖的路径。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents, increasingly empowered by large language models, are becoming important components of human-machine systems for high-stakes decision support in digital twin ecosystems. However, existing multi-agent systems often lack robust verification for identity, capability, and policy compliance, especially in decentralized environments spanning multiple institutions. This paper proposes a neuro-symbolic decentralized governance framework for verifiable agents in collaborative digital twin environments. By representing agents through multi-layer semantic profiles, the framework bridges probabilistic neural reasoning with deterministic institutional governance, thereby supporting trustworthy human-AI collaboration and meaningful human oversight. Capabilities are grounded in formal domain ontologies to enable machine-interpretable, policy-aware, and context-sensitive participation. These credentials, issued by organizational authorities, are validated via blockchain-based smart contracts, ensuring auditable participation without exposing sensitive data. We demonstrate the framework using a decision-support prototype with clinic, digital twin, and wearable provider agents effectively prevents unauthorized interaction and enforces institutional policies with manageable overhead. Our findings suggest that neuro-symbolic decentralized governance provides a scalable and trustworthy pathway for safe human-machine collaboration across institutional boundaries.

</details>


### [155] [NoisePQC++: A Unified NIST-Compliant PQC and Hybrid-PQC Implementation of the Noise Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00954)
*Nadeem Ahmed,Aryya Gangopadhyay,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NoisePQC++，一个统一的C++23实现，将NIST标准的ML-KEM后量子密钥封装机制集成到Noise Protocol Framework中，支持纯PQC、混合ECDH+PQC握手以及所有57种经典Noise握手模式的变体，旨在应对量子计算机对传统公钥密码学的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机对经典公钥密码学构成严重威胁，而广泛使用的Noise Protocol Framework（如WireGuard和WhatsApp所采用）仍依赖易受量子攻击的ECDH密钥交换方案，亟需演进为支持后量子密码原语的安全通信协议。

Method: 设计并实现NoisePQC++：一个基于C++23的统一实现，将NIST标准化的ML-KEM算法与经典ECDH集成，支持纯后量子（PQC）握手、混合ECDH+PQC握手，并完整覆盖57种经典Noise握手模式、13种后量子Noise握手及其混合变体。

Result: 评估显示在正常网络条件下开销极低，在不利网络条件下开销可接受，同时显著增强了对抗量子攻击者的安全性；相比现有工作，协议覆盖范围更广，实现支持更完整，灵活性更高。

Conclusion: NIST标准化的后量子及混合Noise握手是实用的，为未来部署提供了可信的基础。

Abstract: The threat of quantum computers to classical public-key cryptography has created an urgent need to evolve secure communication protocols with post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) primitives. The Noise Protocol Framework, widely used in systems such as WireGuard and WhatsApp, traditionally relies on the Elliptic Curve Diffie-Hellman (ECDH) public-key exchange scheme, which is vulnerable to quantum threats. In this paper, we present NoisePQC++, a unified C++23 implementation of the Noise Protocol framework augmented with post-quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanisms and Hybrid Forward Secrecy. Our design integrates the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) standardized ML-KEM algorithm alongside classical ECDH, enabling full PQC, hybrid ECDH+PQC handshakes, and unified support for all 57 classical Noise handshake pattern variants, 13 post-quantum Noise handshakes, and their hybrid variants. Compared with prior work, NoisePQC++ offers broader protocol coverage, more complete implementation support, and greater flexibility. Our evaluation shows minimal overhead under normal network conditions and acceptable overhead in adverse cases, while significantly improving resistance against quantum adversaries. These results indicate that NIST-standardized post-quantum and hybrid Noise handshakes are practical and provide a credible basis for future deployment.

</details>


### [156] [What Syntax Cannot See: The Dynamic Syntactic Invariance Principle and Several Instances of the Same Hidden Assumption, and a Contradiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00958)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一方法：将已接受结果中隐含的静态假设显式化为变量，并证明在放宽该假设后结果依然成立（或产生新的结论）。其核心是"动态句法不变性原理"，并应用于密码学（滚动密钥）、狭义相对论、物理定律的形式理论、以及SAT问题等多个领域，最终推导出P≠NP的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图寻找跨领域共通的深层结构：一个在完整描述层面真实存在的区分，在受限描述层面可能不可见。该方法的核心动机是揭示看似无关领域背后共同的逻辑骨架，而非强行套用某个模型。

Method: 方法分三步：(1) 识别已知结果中未被质疑的静态假设；(2) 将该假设转化为变量；(3) 证明当该假设被撤销后结论如何变化。形式的工具是"动态句法不变性原理"——一个静态不可达性结果在系统规则随时间演化时仍然成立，前提是满足一个必要条件。

Result: 密码学方面：滚动密钥方案在结构性更新下跨会话保持保密性，强化了先前仅静态的保证；在各种异质领域（相对论、物理定律的形式理论、可计算输出与噪声不可区分）中均独立验证了该结构；在SAT问题上推导出一个矛盾，由此得出P≠NP，但作者对证明本身（而非答案）有所保留。

Conclusion: 该方法在不同领域反复生效，表明其背后的"层级区分不可见性"是一个普遍的认知/逻辑结构，而非人为设定的框架。对P≠NP的结论，作者认为它由标准理论已接受的事实推出而非预先假定，但对证明细节持有保留态度。

Abstract: This paper develops a single method, find what an accepted result silently assumed, make it a variable, and prove what follows once it is dropped, and shows it keeps working across domains with nothing in common. Its formal core is the Dynamic Syntactic Invariance Principle: a known static inaccessibility result survives when a system's rules evolve in time, under one sharp necessary condition. A direct cryptographic payoff follows: the secrecy of a rolling-key scheme persists across sessions under a structural update, strengthening a static guarantee from earlier work. The same move is then carried into settings far from each other, special relativity, the reach of a formal theory of physical law, and computable output that is fully meaningful yet indistinguishable from noise to every observer, each established on its own terms, so the recurring structure beneath them is found, not imposed: a distinction real at a full level of description can be invisible at a restricted one. Applied to \textsc{SAT}, this yields a contradiction from which coherence forces $\mathsf{P}\neq\mathsf{NP}$, derived from what standard theory already admits rather than assumed,offered with one reservation, about the proof, not the answer.

</details>


### [157] [An AI Approach to Verified Production Cryptographic Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00965)
*Chuyue Sun,Su Fong,Zhiyi Kuang,Yizheng Jiao,Nina Narodytska,Haoze Wu,David L. Dill,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CryptoProver是一个AI驱动系统，能从高层API契约自动综合内部规范并生成Verus验证的证明，在不修改可执行代码的情况下独立验证了curve25519-dalek和RustCrypto的chacha20实现。该系统在11.4小时内以466.99美元的API成本完成验证，并采用信任优先设计原则防止规格弱化和引用证明检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学代码是关键基础设施，必须正确无误，但正式验证生产级密码学库仍然非常困难。现有基于语言模型的证明系统只能解决已给出规范和前提的孤立证明义务，无法解决生产库验证问题。

Method: CryptoProver采用AI驱动方法，从高层API契约出发合成内部规范（internal specifications）并生成Verus检查的证明。它使用固定的可信库（包括域规范、算术事实、公理和vstd），遵循信任优先设计原则：通过机械门控（mechanical gates）拒绝规格弱化、发明公理和跨模块破坏，并通过隔离机制阻止引用先前证明检索（包括git历史）。

Result: CryptoProver构建了一个独立的curve25519-dalek新证明，并验证了RustCrypto先前未验证的chacha20实现（符合RFC 8439规范）。这些密码学组件支撑着Signal（约2.18亿次全球下载）和Shadowsocks等部署系统。相比人工主导的curve25519-dalek验证（由5名主要贡献者历时8个月公开开发），CryptoProver在11.4小时内以466.99美元记录API成本完成综合。

Conclusion: CryptoProver证明AI系统可以有效验证生产级密码学库，通过从高层API契约合成内部规范而非解决孤立证明义务，能将数月的专家人工验证工作压缩到数小时和数百美元成本，同时通过机械信任门控保证证明的完整性与独立性。

Abstract: Cryptographic code is critical infrastructure that must be correct, yet formally verifying production libraries remains difficult. Existing language-model proof systems solve isolated obligations with specifications and premises already given, leaving production-library verification unresolved.
  We present CryptoProver, an AI-based system that synthesizes internal specifications and Verus-checked proofs from high-level API contracts. Without changing executable code, CryptoProver constructs a new independent proof of curve25519-dalek and verifies RustCrypto's previously unverified chacha20 implementation against an RFC 8439 specification. These cryptographic lineages underpin deployed systems including Signal and Shadowsocks; Signal has an estimated 218M global downloads. The independent, human-led curve25519-dalek verification was developed publicly over eight months by five main contributors. Given the API contracts and a fixed trusted library of field specifications, arithmetic facts, axioms, and vstd, CryptoProver synthesizes the internal specifications and proofs in 11.4 hours with USD 466.99 in recorded API cost. CryptoProver follows a trust-first design principle: mechanical gates reject specification weakening, invented axioms, and cross-module breakage, while isolation blocks reference proof retrieval, including from git history.

</details>


### [158] [Inverting the Hidden: Unveiling Multimodal Privacy Leakage in Collaborative LVLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01020)
*Shuaifan Jin,Zhibo Wang,Qiyuan Wang,Yiting Han,Yajie Zhou,Yuanfan Zhang,Jiahui Hu,Xiaoyi Pang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RASR，一种粗到细的多模态重建攻击方法，从深度LVLM隐藏状态中恢复图像和文本隐私信息，在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct和LLaVA-1.5-7B上实现图像重建MSE降低约50%，文本恢复准确率高达99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同推理将LVLM计算在边缘设备与云端之间划分，虽然原始输入不直接上传，但中间隐藏状态的传输暴露了潜在的隐私攻击面。然而，深层LVLM隐藏状态在视觉内容被投影到语言嵌入空间后是否仍保留可恢复的私有信息尚不明确，需要对此进行理论分析和实证验证。

Method: 首先在理论上分析LVLM隐藏状态的可恢复性，证明在正则假设和正语义-干扰边际条件下，隐私相关的视觉语义仍可识别且稳定恢复。在此基础上提出RASR攻击方法：通过模态特定的逆路径（遵循各自前向处理流程的逆向过程）获得初始图像和文本重建，然后利用隐藏状态一致性对两类重建进行细化优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct和LLaVA-1.5-7B上对五个数据集进行评估，RASR将图像重建MSE较最强基线降低约50%，文本恢复的token准确率高达99%。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示即使从深层LVLM隐藏状态中，隐私敏感的视觉和文本信息仍可被恢复，这暴露了协同推理范式中存在的严重隐私风险。

Abstract: Collaborative inference deploys Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by partitioning computation between edge devices and the cloud. While withholding raw inputs supposedly ensures privacy, transmitting intermediate hidden states exposes a critical attack surface. However, it remains unclear whether deep-layer LVLM hidden states retain recoverable private information, given that visual content has been projected into the language embedding space. To address this concern, we theoretically analyze LVLM hidden-state recoverability and show that, under regularity assumptions and a positive semantic--nuisance margin, privacy-relevant visual semantics remain identifiable and stably recoverable. Motivated by this analysis, we propose RASR, a novel coarse-to-fine multimodal reconstruction attack. RASR obtains initial image and text reconstructions through modality-specific inverse paths that follow their respective forward processing pipelines in reverse, and then uses hidden-state consistency to refine both reconstructions. Evaluations on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and LLaVA-1.5-7B across five datasets demonstrate that RASR reduces image reconstruction MSE by \(\sim\)50\% compared to the strongest baselines, while achieving up to 99\% token accuracy for text recovery. These results show that privacy-sensitive visual and textual information can be recovered even from deep-layer LVLM hidden states, exposing the privacy risks of collaborative inference.

</details>


### [159] [CallScreenBench: Benchmarking On-Device Models as Phone Secretaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01033)
*Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CallScreenBench基准，用于评估设备端小语言模型作为"电话秘书"接听未知来电的能力。不同于传统任务导向型基准，该场景无明确任务、无合作用户，关键在于代理能否在开场回合就判断来电者意图（可能是对手），并做出让机主认可的处理方式。评估涵盖五个质量维度，但不合成为一个总分。研究发现模型质量随能力提升而提升，但分流（triage）能力并非如此；通过脚本化退化代理作为地板校正后，模型间分流表现差异从15对中的11对降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多评估有明确任务和合作用户的任务完成型代理，但真实场景中的电话秘书需要处理无任务、可能敌对且仅在开场回合可判断的不明来电。作者希望建立一种更贴近真实使用场景的评估框架，关注机主认可度而非任务成功率。

Method: 构建CallScreenBench五个质量维度评估框架，每个维度配有对应的对销度量（counter-metric）且不对各维度做平均合成。采用无工具（toolless）、无凭证的代理profile，在6个0.6-4B参数、4-bit量化设备端模型上进行实验。引入脚本化退化代理（例如挂断并回显来电者）作为性能地板，通过地板校正重新检验模型间分流性能差异。

Result: 五个质量维度上，模型质量随能力增长而提升，但分流性能不随能力增长。表面上的分流能力随能力提升是度量伪影。经脚本化退化代理地板校正后，在预注册操作点上，15对模型中有11对的分流性能差异归零。退化代理甚至在"消息保真度"维度获得满分。论文报告了哪些自身指标被这些地板击穿，并明确不设通过/失败阈值。

Conclusion: CallScreenBench揭示了一个重要发现：在电话秘书场景中，模型能力的提升并不必然带来分流（triage）等关键判断能力的提升，看似相关实则源于测量伪影。该基准拒绝综合单一分数，以多维度+对销度量的方式提供更稳健的评估，并提倡使用退化地板来校准指标可靠性。

Abstract: Language models small enough to run on a handset, quantized to a few bits, are increasingly capable of acting for their user, making on-device task automation newly plausible. One such task is answering the phone. A phone secretary takes an unknown inbound call on its owner's behalf. Unlike the agents evaluated by most benchmarks, it has no task to complete and no cooperative user: the caller holds the goal, may be an adversary, and must be judged from the opening turn with no oracle. What matters is not task success, but whether the owner would endorse how their proxy handled the call.
  We present CallScreenBench, which scores this setting on five quality dimensions. Each dimension is printed beside the counter-metric that bills it and is never averaged into a single number. We also report a guardedness profile for a toolless proxy that holds no credentials and calls no tools. Across six on-device models (0.6-4B parameters, 4-bit quantization), quality scales with capability, but triage does not. The appearance that it does is an artifact of measurement. Scripted degenerate agents supply the missing floors: after correcting for them, the number of model pairs whose triage performance separates falls from 11 of 15 to zero at the preregistered operating point. An agent that simply hangs up and echoes the caller also scores perfect message fidelity. We report which of our own metrics these floors defeat and declare no pass/fail threshold.

</details>


### [160] [Decoy Images Amplify Caption-Mediated Defenses Against Encoded Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01043)
*Haoyu Zhang,Xiangchen Guan,Shibo Zheng,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一种反直觉的效应：在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，将编码的越狱提示与无关的"诱饵"图像配对，可以显著降低攻击成功率（ASR）。跨五个前沿VLM、两类编码攻击和三种黑盒防御验证，基于字幕的防御（ECSO）在纯文本编码输入上几乎不改变ASR，但附加无内容诱饵后ASR最多下降73个百分点。作者提出两种假设：字幕介导的防御会分支于图像存在性，以及图像侧固有安全机制对图像内容起作用。无条件附加诱饵不可部署（良性拒答率升至20-79%），但基于轻量级编码输入检测器的门控附加可将良性拒答率恢复到文本基线，同时保留安全增益。自适应攻击下效应退化但仍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VLM中图像输入与现有黑盒防御之间的意外交互效应，揭示编码越狱提示与诱饵图像配对时攻击成功率显著下降的反直觉现象，并系统性地控制实验以排除干扰因素（图片内容、厂商过滤、符号混淆等），最终提出基于检测器门控的安全增益保留方案。

Method: 在五个前沿VLM、两类编码攻击家族和三种黑盒防御下进行系统实验；使用精确McNemar检验评估显著性；通过三类控制实验（空白画布和自然照片诱饵、开源权重无审核层VLM、非符号化基于语义的编码器）来限定解释空间；提出两种假设（字幕介导防御分支于图像存在性、图像侧固有安全机制）；并设计自适应攻击评估效应的稳健性。

Result: 附加诱饵图像可使ECSO在编码输入上的ASR降低最多73个百分点；空白画布和自然照片诱饵在所有模型上复现效应，说明是图像存在性而非内容起作用；三种开源权重VLM（无审核层）上也复现，排除厂商过滤；非符号化编码器也复现，排除符号混淆特异性。但无条件附加诱饵使良性拒答率升至20-79%（+10至+67pp），而门控策略可将拒答率恢复到文本基线并保留安全增益；自适应攻击下效应退化但仍成立。

Conclusion: 该效应本质是防御管线交互的观测结果，而非稳健的防御机制。图像的存在性（而非内容）触发了字幕介导防御的分支行为和图像侧安全机制，从而抑制编码攻击。可部署方案是将诱饵附加门控在轻量级编码输入检测器上，此时检测器召回率成为关键约束。此发现揭示了黑盒防御中"管线条件分支"作为隐藏变量的重要性，对VLM安全评估方法论具有启示意义。

Abstract: We report a counter-intuitive interaction between image inputs and existing black-box defenses on Vision--Language Models (VLMs): pairing an encoded jailbreak prompt with an unrelated decoy image can sharply lower attack success rate (ASR). The operative change is in the defense pipeline, not in the image. Across five frontier VLMs, two encoded-attack families, and three black-box defenses, a caption-mediated defense (ECSO) that leaves ASR essentially unchanged on text-only encoded input drops it by up to $73$pp once a content-free decoy is attached; every non-saturated contrast is significant under exact McNemar tests. We advance two hypotheses for this pattern, supported by indirect evidence rather than pipeline introspection, since a black-box threat model precludes inspecting vendor internals: caption-mediated defenses branch on image presence, and intrinsic image-side safety engages on image-resident content. Three controls constrain the explanation. Blank-canvas and natural-photograph decoys reproduce the effect on every model, implicating image presence rather than content; the effect replicates on three open-weight VLMs served with no moderation layer, so it is not a vendor-filtering artifact; and a non-symbolic, meaning-based encoder reproduces it, so it is not specific to symbolic obfuscation. Attaching a decoy unconditionally is not deployable --- it raises benign refusal to $20$--$79\%$, an inflation of $+10$ to $+67$pp --- but gating attachment on a lightweight encoded-input detector returns benign refusal to the text baseline while preserving the safety gain wherever the detector fires, making detector recall the binding constraint. Under adaptive attacks that target the caption-mediated re-check, the effect degrades but holds. We frame this as an observation about pipeline interaction, not as a robust defense.

</details>


### [161] [SoK: Intent-Oriented Systematization of Multi-Turn LLM Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01117)
*Siyuan Li,Aodu Wulianghai,Zehao Liu,Xi Lin,Qinghua Mao,Haoyu Li,Xiang Chen,Siyuan Liang,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四部分、面向意图的多轮越狱（multi-turn jailbreak）分类体系，并通过受控消融实验发现，攻击有效性取决于恶意意图在多轮对话中的组织方式，而非上下文长度或查询次数；同时，意图的组织方式决定了其可被检测的层级（从单轮、会话到跨会话），说明单轮安全机制在结构上存在不足，需要与恶意意图可观察层级相匹配的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将多轮越狱视为零散的提示词模式集合，缺乏对攻击者如何在交互过程中组织和推进恶意意图的系统性分析，因此需要一套结构化的意图导向分类体系来深入理解攻击机制。

Method: 提出了一个四部分的、面向意图的分类体系（taxonomy），用于按对抗性意图结构组织多轮越狱攻击；通过受控消融实验（controlled ablations）逐一检验上下文长度、查询数量以及意图组织方式等因素对攻击有效性的影响。

Result: 发现攻击有效性主要由意图在多轮之间的组织方式决定，而非上下文长度或查询数量；并进一步证明意图的组织方式决定了其可被检测的层级，将所需检测面从单轮层级推移到会话层级再到跨会话层级。

Conclusion: 单轮局部（turn-local）安全机制在结构上不足以防御多轮越狱；单一时间点（single-point）的评估会忽略意图的组织方式，因此需要设计对齐到恶意意图可观察层级的评估协议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive settings, where user intent commonly unfolds through multi-turn dialogue. Multi-turn jailbreaks exploit this pattern by advancing a harmful intent across turns, so that no single message exposes the full objective. However, existing work treats these attacks as a loose collection of prompt patterns and does not analyze how the adversary organizes and advances harmful intent across an interaction. We develop a four-part, intent-oriented taxonomy that organizes multi-turn jailbreaks by adversarial intent structure. Through controlled ablations, we find that effectiveness is driven by how deliberately intent is organized across turns rather than by context length or query count. We further show that the way intent is organized determines the level at which it becomes detectable, pushing the required detection surface outward from the turn level to the session level to the cross-session level. These findings indicate that turn-local safety mechanisms are structurally insufficient and that single-point evaluation overlooks how intent is organized, motivating evaluation protocols aligned to the level at which harmful intent becomes observable. The code is available at: https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/INTACT.

</details>


### [162] [Latency-Optimal Adaptive Split Inference for Privacy-Preserving Cloud-Edge-End Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01148)
*Yi Li,Peng Zhang,Man Ho Au*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向隐私保护的云边端协同自适应拆分推理框架，端设备作为可信锚点执行明文模型前缀并使用全同态加密（FHE）加密中间激活，边缘与云端仅在密文上执行模型片段，通过拆分点选择优化端到端延迟，在CIFAR-10和PathMNIST上实现了相对全云FHE约12.9倍、相对块级方案约3.9倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网端设备计算资源有限，难以在本地完整执行卷积神经网络以支持隐私敏感的批量推理；而完全云端的FHE推理延迟极高。需要在保护隐私的同时，通过云边端协同拆分推理来优化延迟。

Method: 提出延迟最优的自适应拆分推理框架：端设备充当信任锚点，执行明文模型前缀，使用FHE加密拆分激活并本地持有密钥；边缘和云端仅在FHE密文上执行各自分配的模型片段。将协同加密推理建模为端侧拆分点和边侧终止点的拆分对选择问题，规划器联合建模明文前缀执行、加密、通信、边缘FHE执行和云端FHE完成，支持卷积级和块级两种拆分粒度。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和PathMNIST数据集上，卷积级协同方案相对全云FHE获得约12.9倍的端到端加速，相对块级方案获得约3.9倍加速，同时保持对应的明文模型精度。计入通信后，CIFAR-10和PathMNIST上的平摊延迟分别为1033.279秒/样本和1023.429秒/样本。

Conclusion: 所提的卷积级自适应拆分推理方案在显著降低端到端延迟的同时保持模型精度，验证了端侧可信执行+FHE密文协同推理框架在隐私保护条件下的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) end devices are increasingly expected to support privacy-sensitive batch inference, yet their limited computational resources often make full local execution of convolutional neural networks impractical. This paper presents a latency-optimal adaptive split inference framework for privacy-preserving cloud-edge-end collaboration. The end device acts as the trust anchor, executes the plaintext model prefix, encrypts the split activation using fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), and keeps the secret key locally, while the edge and cloud execute assigned model segments only on FHE ciphertexts. We formulate collaborative encrypted inference as a split-pair selection problem over an end-side split point and an edge-side termination point. The proposed planner jointly models plaintext prefix execution, encryption, communication, edge-side FHE execution, and cloud-side FHE completion, and supports both convolution-level and block-level split granularities. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and PathMNIST show that the proposed convolution-level collaborative scheme achieves amortized end-to-end speedups of approximately 12.9 times over full-cloud FHE and 3.9 times over the block-level alternative, while preserving the corresponding plaintext-model accuracy. Including modeled communication, the amortized latencies are 1033.279 s/sample on CIFAR-10 and 1023.429 s/sample on PathMNIST.

</details>


### [163] [A Unified Benchmark for Privacy-preserving Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01192)
*Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Rafael Pires,Mathis Randl,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种私有向量搜索加密方案（SAP、EMVP、BNTM、Tiptoe）在完全相同的工作负载、硬件和度量标准下进行了统一的实验比较，并与明文基线对照，构建了隐私、性能和召回率之间的帕累托前沿，为实践者提供了可防御的部署选择依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量搜索（支撑语义搜索、推荐系统和RAG）存在隐私泄露问题：服务端同时看到查询向量和语料库。多种加密方案（SAP、EMVP、BNTM、Tiptoe）虽已提出，但各自在独立语料、威胁模型、参数选择、硬件和度量约定下评估，数据无法直接比较，实践者无法做出有依据的部署决策。

Method: 在统一平台上对Plaintext基线及四种加密后端进行等量工作负载、相同硬件和一致度量定义的横向对照实验，评估各项方案在隐私保护强度、性能开销和召回率三个维度的表现，并额外考察GPU加速对各方案的影响，所有实验工件公开可复现。

Result: 各方案构成隐私-性能-召回率的帕累托前沿而非统一惩罚：SAP性能与明文持平；EMVP以4倍CPU吞吐量代价实现密码学不可区分性；BNTM在EMVP基础上增加恶意服务器可验证性，但中位延迟进一步增加22倍；Tiptoe隐藏聚类选择自身，但每查询开销为明文的190倍。GPU加速对Plaintext和SAP有效，对EMVP和BNTM无效。

Conclusion: 不存在单一最优方案，实践者应依据自身威胁模型和性能预算在帕累托前沿上选择：要求最高性能选SAP，需要不可区分性选EMVP，需要恶意服务器安全性选BNTM，需要隐藏访问模式选Tiptoe。研究填补了该领域缺乏可比较基准的空白。

Abstract: Vector search powers semantic search, recommendation systems, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). By design, the service answering a query sees both the query embedding and, usually, the corpus against which it is matched. This is a privacy breach for both the user issuing the query and the owner of the corpus. A family of cryptographic schemes (e.g., SAP, EMVP, BNTM, Tip-toe) addresses that leak. However, as each scheme is published and evaluated on its own corpus, threat model, parameter choices, hardware, and metric conventions, the numbers cannot be compared directly. Consequently, a practitioner asking which one to deploy today has no defensible way to choose. We close that gap with a uniform experimental comparison, including a Plaintext baseline and four cryptographic backends running over the same workload, hardware, and metric definitions. Under that ruler, the schemes spread across a Pareto frontier in privacy, performance, and recall rather than imposing a flat penalty on performance. We find that the performance of SAP matches Plaintext, EMVP delivers cryptographic indistinguishability at a 4x throughput cost on CPU, BNTM adds malicious-server verifiability at a further 22x median-latency cost, and Tiptoe hides the cluster choice itself, but incurs a 190x per-query cost compared to Plaintext. GPU acceleration pays off for Plaintext and SAP but not for EMVP or BNTM. All our experiment artifacts are publicly available for reproducibility

</details>


### [164] [The Boy Who Cried Wolf: Adversarial Misclassification of Safe Inputs as Unsafe in Multimodal Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01373)
*Shuo Shi,Rui Yin,Naen Xu,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Zhaoxiang Wang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"不安全诱导攻击"（Unsafe Induction Attacks），通过向良性图像注入微小扰动，诱使多模态安全守卫模型错误拒绝合法用户请求，造成服务可用性下降和信任侵蚀。作者提出不安全语义蒸馏（USD）方法，将对抗扰动与不安全内容的分布表示对齐，在四个最先进的守卫模型上实现了84%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗研究主要关注通过越狱攻击产生假阴性（错误放行不安全内容），但反向威胁——在良性输入上诱导假阳性（错误拒绝合法请求）——尚未被探索。这种"狼来了"效应会降低服务可用性并侵蚀用户信任，构成部署安全过滤器时的一个可用性失效模式。

Method: 提出不安全语义蒸馏（USD）方法，核心思路是将对抗扰动与不安全内容的分布表示对齐（而非与具体的提示词实例对齐），从而在多样的用户提示下仍能有效触发守卫模型的拒绝行为。该扰动是几乎不可感知的，施加于安全图像上。

Result: 在四个最先进的多模态守卫模型上，结合现实用户模拟场景评估，USD实现了84%的攻击成功率，优于现有方法，暴露了当前多模态安全架构的根本性漏洞。

Conclusion: 多模态安全守卫模型存在可利用的假阳性漏洞，攻击者可通过看似无害的扰动图像使安全过滤器拒绝合法请求，从而削弱服务可用性。这提示当前多模态安全架构需要重新审视其鲁棒性设计。

Abstract: Multimodal guard models have emerged as critical safety components for screening content in vision-language systems. While adversarial research has extensively studied jailbreaking attacks that produce false negatives, the inverse threat of inducing false positives on benign inputs remains unexplored. We introduce Unsafe Induction Attacks, where adversaries distribute imperceptibly perturbed safe images that trigger guard models to reject legitimate user requests, causing a "Boy Who Cried Wolf" effect that degrades service availability and erodes trust. This reveals an availability failure mode in deployed safety filters. To realize this threat under diverse user prompts, we propose Unsafe Semantic Distillation (USD), which aligns adversarial perturbations with distributional representations of unsafe content rather than prompt-specific instances. Evaluated on four state-of-the-art guard models across realistic user simulation scenarios, USD achieves 84% attack success rates, outperforming existing methods and exposing fundamental vulnerabilities in current multimodal safety architectures.

</details>


### [165] [AdaptoNet: Modular Foundation-Adaptive Neural Networks for Cyber-Physical Attack Detection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01375)
*Anissa Elias,Jennifer Rogers,Hui Lin,Yan,Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptoNet，一种模块化神经网络架构，用于在数据拒绝（data denial）攻击下仍能有效检测电网异常。AdaptoNet将冻结的基础模块（在完整数据上训练）与可训练的适应模块配对，通过二元测量可用性向量进行条件控制，使其能够区分被拒绝的数据与异常数据，无需重新训练基础模块。在四种IEEE测试系统中，即使攻击者阻断最多20%的测量数据，AdaptoNet将F1分数从低于12%恢复至81%以上，性能提升约七倍，接近完整测量下89%-99%的基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网作为典型的网络物理系统（CPS），面临结合物理破坏与数据破坏的网络攻击。现有的标准数据驱动检测方法在数据拒绝攻击面前几乎失效（检测性能下降超过86%），亟需一种能够适应测量数据部分缺失场景的新型检测方法。

Method: AdaptoNet采用模块化神经网络设计：1) 冻结的基础模块在完整数据上预训练，保留对正常模式的核心表征能力；2) 可训练的适应模块通过二元测量可用性向量进行条件控制，动态调整模型行为以适应当前测量数据的可用状态；3) 两个模块协作使模型能区分被拒绝的数据与真正异常的数据，无需对基础模块进行重训练。

Result: 在IEEE 30、39、57和118母线四个测试系统上，针对区域内阻断最多20%测量数据的攻击场景，AdaptoNet将F1分数从低于12%恢复到81%以上，实现了约七倍的性能提升，接近完整测量情况下89%-99%的基线水平。

Conclusion: AdaptoNet通过冻结-适应模块架构有效应对了数据拒绝攻击对电网异常检测的威胁，表明在数据部分缺失条件下，通过条件控制机制可以显著恢复检测性能，为网络物理系统的鲁棒异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cyber attacks on the power grid combine physical disruptions with compromised data to destabilize cyber-physical systems. We demonstrate that data denial attacks, where adversaries block measurements in a targeted region while triggering a line outage, reduce detection performance by more than 86\%, rendering standard data-driven methods ineffective. We propose AdaptoNet, a modular neural network that adapts to measurement availability through conditional controls. AdaptoNet pairs a frozen foundational module trained on complete data with a trainable adaptive module, conditioned on a binary measurement-availability vector, enabling the model to distinguish between denied and anomalous data without retraining the foundational module. Evaluated across four IEEE test systems (30-, 39-, 57-, and 118-bus) under in-region attacks blocking up to 20% of measurements, AdaptoNet recovers F1 from below 12\% to above 81\%, an approximate sevenfold improvement approaching the 89%-99% baseline with complete measurements.

</details>


### [166] [Why Formal Monitors Fail: Attack Distribution Entropy as a Coverage Bound for LTL-Based LLM Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01388)
*Ruiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为LLM智能体运行时安全监控器在不同模型架构上存在的攻击覆盖率差异提供了理论解释：证明固定不变式FSA监控器的召回率上界由攻击分布的集中度（熵）决定，并通过八个前沿LLM架构的实证验证，提出了部署前的熵测试用于预测监控器覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LTL和FSA的运行时安全监控器在不同模型架构上的攻击覆盖率差异巨大（68-75% vs 接近零），但现有能力指标、训练数据或提示设计都无法解释这一现象，缺乏理论依据。

Method: 1) 理论证明：固定不变式FSA监控器的召回率受攻击分布集中度（k个最频繁触发-补全模式的比例）约束；2) 熵-覆盖率界限推导；3) 在八个前沿LLM架构上实证验证，计算Shannon熵与覆盖率的相关性；4) 提出部署前熵测试，利用小样本预测监控器覆盖率。

Result: GPT类与DeepSeek后端产生高度集中的攻击（熵约0.24 bits，单一模式覆盖96%），解释68-75%的召回率；Gemini变体产生高熵分布（熵约2.81 bits，7个聚类各不超过7%），解释近零召回率（6-13%）。熵解释76%的覆盖率方差（Pearson r = -0.87, p = 0.005），leave-one-out验证稳定。

Conclusion: 攻击分布的熵是预测FSA监控器覆盖率的关键变量，熵测试可在部署前辅助架构感知的监控器选择；该界限和测试与架构无关，适用于任何基于FSA的运行时监控器。

Abstract: Runtime safety monitors based on Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) and finite automata (FSA) are increasingly deployed to intercept unsafe tool-call sequences in LLM agents. Yet the same monitor achieves 68-75% attack coverage on some model architectures and near-zero on others, with no explanation from capability scores, training data, or prompt design. We provide the missing theory. We prove that the recall of any fixed-invariant FSA monitor is bounded above by the concentration of the attack distribution: the fraction of attacks covered by the k most frequent trigger-completion patterns. When attacks concentrate (low Shannon entropy), a small fixed invariant set achieves high recall; when they disperse across many structurally distinct patterns (high entropy), no fixed invariant set of tractable size can, regardless of how the invariants were derived. We validate this entropy-coverage bound across eight frontier LLM architectures. GPT-class and DeepSeek backends yield highly concentrated attacks (H ~ 0.24 bits; one pattern covers 96%), explaining 68-75% recall; Gemini variants yield high-entropy distributions (H ~ 2.81 bits; 7 clusters each <= 7%), explaining near-zero recall (6-13%), invariant to architecture-matched retraining. Entropy accounts for 76% of variance in coverage (Pearson r = -0.87, p = 0.005, 95% CI [-0.98, -0.78]), holding under leave-one-out (r in [-0.91, -0.82]). We introduce a pre-deployment entropy test that predicts monitor coverage from a small attack sample, enabling architecture-aware monitor selection before deployment. The bound and test are architecture-agnostic and apply to any FSA-based runtime monitor over discrete action sequences.

</details>


### [167] [Pivot: Proactive and Verifiable Threshold Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions From Isogeny Group Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01390)
*Abhinav Sharma,Vikas Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIVOT，一个基于有效同源群作用的无中心阈值VOPRF框架，支持秘密共享份额的定期刷新而不改变主密钥、公钥或已有OPRF输出，可抵御移动敌手长期累积(t)个份额恢复主密钥的攻击，并提供可公开验证的诚实性保证和委员会重共享功能。


<details>
