  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于同源的阈值OPRF主要针对静态敌手模型设计，在长期运行的服务中，同一份共享状态长期存在使得移动敌手可以随时间攻陷不同服务器、累积同一共享状态下的t个份额，最终恢复主密钥。

Method: PIVOT结合Shamir秘密共享、加法同态系数承诺、顺序Lagrange加权群作用以及将认证份额链接到对应同源动作的联合零知识关系。支持协调式时期转换、公开可验证的责备机制、安全擦除以及可能在不同阈值下的委员会重共享。安全性基于向量化假设和one-more隐藏群作用假设，采用基于模拟的安全分析。

Result: 正式化了持久化主动阈值VOPRF的功能性，证明了分布式密钥生成、阈值评估、主动刷新和委员会重共享的正确性，并提供基于模拟的安全性分析。作为应用，描述了分布式私有查找服务，其加密数据库在重复的份额更新和委员会迁移中保持有效性。

Conclusion: PIVOT是首个在移动敌手模型下提供主动安全性的同源阈值VOPRF框架，通过定期刷新份额来防止长期威胁，同时保持了无中心设置、可验证性和对动态委员会调整的支持。

Abstract: Oblivious pseudorandom functions (OPRFs) allow a client to evaluate a keyed pseudorandom function on a private input without revealing that input to the server. In a threshold OPRF, the secret key is distributed among (n) servers so that any qualified set of at least (t) servers can complete an evaluation, while fewer than (t) shares reveal no information about the key. Existing isogeny-based threshold OPRFs, however, are primarily designed for static corruption models. If the same shares remain valid throughout the lifetime of the service, a mobile adversary can compromise different servers over time, accumulate (t) shares from the same sharing state, and eventually recover the master key. We introduce PIVOT (Proactive Isogeny-based Verifiable Oblivious Threshold PRF), a dealerless threshold VOPRF framework based on effective isogeny group actions. PIVOT periodically refreshes the server shares without changing the master key, public key, or previously generated OPRF outputs. The construction combines Shamir secret sharing, additively homomorphic coefficient commitments, sequential Lagrange-weighted group actions, and joint zero-knowledge relations that link certified shares to their corresponding isogeny actions. It also supports coordinated epoch transitions, publicly verifiable blame, secure erasure, and committee resharing under a possibly different threshold. We formalize the functionality of a long-lived proactive threshold VOPRF, prove the correctness of distributed key generation, threshold evaluation, proactive refresh, and committee resharing, and provide a simulation-based security analysis under the vectorization and one-more hidden-group- action assumptions. As an application, we describe a distributed private lookup service whose encrypted database remains valid across repeated share renewals and committee migrations.

</details>


### [168] [How Benchmarks and Evaluation Protocols Shape Conclusions in Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01454)
*Lorenzo Guerra,Thomas Chapuis,Guillaume Duc,Pavlo Mozharovskyi,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的评估协议对代表性基于溯源入侵检测系统（PIDS）在DARPA TC E3等公开数据集上重新评估，发现警报成功率与调查效用可能严重背离，简单基于可执行文件名的允许列表在多个数据集上可匹敌或超越学习的基线模型，表明许多测得的性能反映的是词法新颖性而非丰富的溯源建模。评估结果表明PIDS的架构性结论必须结合基准属性和评估协议来解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIDS研究经常报告强性能，但这些结论对基准选择和评估协议高度敏感。作者旨在厘清哪些架构性主张在不同评估条件下仍然成立，避免因评估设置不当导致误导性结论。

Method: 作者选择了满足审计、标注和标定要求的公开数据集（主要为DARPA TC E3），采用统一的评估协议：时间分隔的测试周期、仅用验证集进行检查点选择和阈值标定。同时测量语义信号质量（特征完整性和字段熵），并引入基于训练可执行文件名和路径的简单允许列表作为基线比较。

Result: 研究发现：1）警报成功与调查效用在多个系统中严重背离；2）在四个主要数据集中的三个上，简单允许列表匹配或超过学习的基线模型；3）语义信号质量（特征完整性、字段熵）可解释为何只有部分数据集能区分不同架构的性能；4）Theia数据集具有最强的语义信号质量，其对应的排序和节点级恢复提升也最为清晰。

Conclusion: PIDS中的架构性主张必须与产生这些结果的基准属性和评估协议一起解读。仅凭警报性能不足以支撑架构优势的结论，需要结合基准数据特性与严格的时间一致评估协议。

Abstract: Provenance-based intrusion detection systems (PIDS) frequently report strong performance, but the conclusions drawn from these results can be highly sensitive to benchmarking choices and evaluation protocols. We investigate this dependency by re-evaluating representative PIDS on public datasets that meet our audit, labeling, and calibration requirements. Focusing primarily on the audited DARPA TC E3 datasets, we apply a unified protocol with temporally separated test periods and validation-only checkpoint and threshold calibration, and ask which architectural claims are empirically supported. We find that alerting success and investigation utility can diverge sharply, as several systems surface attacks without providing enough process-level context to support forensic investigation. On three of the four primary datasets, a simple allowlist built from training executable names and paths matches or exceeds the selected learned baselines on key operating-point metrics, suggesting that much of their measured performance reflects lexical novelty rather than richer provenance modeling. To explain why only some datasets expose architectural differences, we measure semantic signal quality through feature completeness and field entropy. This analysis helps explain why several audited E3 datasets can expose alerting behavior without reliably separating model architectures, while Theia pairs the strongest semantic signal quality with the clearest improvements in ranking and node-level recovery by our reference model. These results show that architectural claims in PIDS should be interpreted together with the benchmark properties and evaluation protocol that produced them.

</details>


### [169] [SP2UBI: Secure and Privacy-Preserving Usage-Based Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01539)
*Mobin Aghamirkarimi,Matin Aghamirkarimi,Farid Zaredar,Morteza Amini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SP2UBI是一个面向使用基础保险（UBI）的隐私保护框架，利用Torus全同态加密实现在加密数据上进行风险计算，并通过ISAC辅助的速度验证系统抵御传感器级欺诈，实验表明单轮协议执行仅需41.2毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于使用量的保险（UBI）系统依赖高分辨率遥测数据，这面临两大挑战：一是现有隐私保护方案在协议生命周期中仍存在数据泄露风险；二是在不牺牲隐私的前提下难以保证数据完整性，因为现有的审计方法需要访问隐私数据且未考虑预言机问题。这些问题促使作者设计一个同时保障数据机密性和完整性的UBI解决方案。

Method: SP2UBI通过以下方式解决问题：1) 以粗粒度、统计形式收集遥测数据，不含时空标识符，确保无法重建细粒度移动轨迹；2) 采用Torus全同态加密（TFHE）方案，以较低计算开销直接在加密数据上执行风险因素计算，使保险公司无法访问敏感信息，同时其风险模型参数也保持机密（实现双向保密）；3) 引入基于感知与通信一体化（ISAC）技术的速度验证系统，在不泄露用户隐私的前提下检测传感器级操纵（如传感器伪造或故障）。

Result: 实验评估表明，SP2UBI的单轮协议执行时间为41.2毫秒，框架轻量化，并且相比现有最先进解决方案提供了更强的隐私保证。

Conclusion: SP2UBI通过粗粒度数据收集、TFHE同态加密和ISAC辅助验证三者结合，在保障保险公司与保单持有人双方数据机密性的同时有效抵御传感器层面的数据完整性威胁，为隐私保护UBI系统提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The transition from traditional auto insurance, whose basis is defined by a set of static parameters, like the age of driver and number of accidents, to Usage-Based Insurance, whose pricing is based on driving behavior, was boosted by Intelligent Transportation Systems. At the same time, the analysis of high-resolution telematics data might reveal users' behavior and habits, which is associated with significant privacy concerns. The majority of privacy-preserving UBI systems are subject to policyholders' data leaks at some point during the life cycle of their protocol. Moreover, it is challenging to ensure data integrity against possible intentional or unintentional sensor anomalies (like sensor spoofing or malfunctioning) since the approaches used in this case require privacy-compromising audits and do not consider the oracle problem. In order to address the issues, we propose SP2UBI, a privacy-preserving UBI solution which guarantees mutual confidentiality. SP2UBI collects the telematics data in coarse-grained, statistical form without any spatiotemporal identifiers so that it is impossible to reconstruct fine-grained mobility traces. By utilizing the Torus Fully Homomorphic Encryption scheme, which incurs low computational overhead, computations are performed directly over encrypted data. This way, the insurer calculates risk factors without accessing any sensitive information, while parameters of its risk model are confidential. In order to protect data integrity against sensor level manipulation, SP2UBI incorporates a speed verification system assisted by Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) technology which is capable of detecting fraud while preserving user privacy. Experimental evaluation shows that one round of protocol execution takes 41.2 ms, showing that our framework is lightweight and preserves more privacy guarantees compared to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [170] [From Viral to Void: Multi-Dimensional Behavioral and Contractual Analysis for Rug Pull Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01609)
*Jinyin Song,Hongping Wang,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对以太坊上日益频发的模因币Rug Pull（拉地毯）诈骗，构建了多维特征体系，设计了基于多层感知机（MLP）的风险检测模型，结合SMOTE过采样与Focal Loss解决类别不平衡问题，并通过Flask框架开发了可视化Web检测系统。实验表明模型在测试集上达到0.927的准确率、0.787的F1分数和0.952的AUC-ROC，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着区块链和DeFi生态扩张，模因币Rug Pull诈骗频发，威胁投资者资产安全与行业健康发展。现有检测方法存在特征维度单一、类别不平衡处理不当、模型泛化能力和可解释性弱等问题，传统人工审查或固定规则难以满足实时预警需求，亟需更有效的自动化检测方案。

Method: 首先明确Rug Pull的定义、类型与危害机制，构建涵盖恶意智能合约设计、链上交易异常、流动性操纵和社交媒体披露等多维特征体系；以"二舅币"（BOBU）为例重建攻击过程并提取量化检测指标；设计基于MLP的风险检测模型，采用SMOTE过采样与Focal Loss联合策略解决样本不平衡问题，动态搜索最优阈值以平衡精确率与召回率，并引入梯度裁剪和早停机制增强训练稳定性；最后使用Flask框架开发可视化Web检测系统，支持批量风险评估、高风险排序展示与结果导出。

Result: 模型在测试集上取得0.927的准确率、0.787的F1分数和0.952的AUC-ROC，性能优于传统检测方法。同时实现了基于Flask的可视化Web检测系统，具备批量风险评级、高风险排行展示及结果导出等实际应用功能。

Conclusion: 研究所提出的基于MLP的多维特征Rug Pull检测方法在准确性和实用性上均优于传统方法，所开发的可视化系统具备实际部署价值，为区块链安全领域提供了可用的早期预警工具，未来可能进一步扩展至更多链和更多类型的欺诈检测。

Abstract: As the blockchain and decentralized finance (DeFi) ecosystems continue to expand and mature, rug pull scams involving meme coins are occurring with increasing frequency, posing a threat to the security of investors' assets and the healthy development of the industry. Rug Pull scams are characterized by extremely low deployment costs, covert execution, rapid fund transfers, and high detection difficulty. Traditional manual reviews or fixed rules struggle to meet real-time early warning requirements, and existing detection methods generally suffer from issues such as a single feature dimension, inadequate handling of class imbalance, and weak model generalization and interpretability. To address these shortcomings, this paper focuses on the detection of Ethereum-based rug pull scams. First, we clarify their definitions, types, and harm mechanisms, and construct a multi-dimensional feature system based on dimensions such as malicious smart contract design, on-chain transaction anomalies, liquidity manipulation, and social media disclosures. Next, using the "Second Uncle Coin"(token symbol: BOBU) case as an example, we reconstruct the attack process and derive quantitative detection metrics. Subsequently, a risk detection model based on a Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) is designed. We employ a combined strategy of SMOTE oversampling and Focal Loss to address the issue of sample imbalance, dynamically search for optimal thresholds to balance precision and recall, and incorporate gradient pruning and early stopping to enhance training stability. Experiments show that the model achieves an accuracy of 0.927, an F1 score of 0.787, and an AUC-ROC of 0.952 on the test set, outperforming traditional methods. Finally, a visualizable web-based detection system is developed using the Flask framework, enabling batch risk assessment, high-risk ranking display, and result export functions.

</details>


### [171] [Mutate to Bypass: Autonomous Endpoint Evasion via Knowledge-Driven Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01639)
*Weifeng Yuan,Wenbo Guo,Qingyun Du,Jun Chen,Feng Dong,Haoyu Wang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AutoBypass是一个基于知识图谱的闭环多智能体框架，用于自动化EDR（端点检测与响应）系统韧性评估。它通过构建"检测感知知识库"来组织威胁情报、专家分析和开源POC，并利用智能体规划攻击、生成多态代码、编译二进制文件，同时通过遥测驱动的推理引擎诊断失败并反馈修正。在七个商业端点安全平台上，AutoBypass成功绕过了所有目标，对Windows Defender的规避率达到90%，对Trend Micro AV达到86.7%。消融实验表明，知识库可将8B开源模型的成功率从27-53%提升至43-83%，接近大型专有模型的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然公开报告和开源资源暴露了大量EDR规避技术，但商业EDR系统能否抵御这些已记录的攻击仍不清楚。评估它们需要将碎片化的安全知识转化为可用的载荷（payload），并从模糊的告警信息中迭代改进载荷，这些任务现有自动化工具无法解决。

Method: 提出了AutoBypass，一个知识引导的闭环多智能体框架。包括三个核心组件：(1) Detection-Aware Knowledge Base，将威胁情报、专家分析和开源POC结构化组织为规避技术和操作约束；(2) 多智能体系统，利用知识库规划攻击、生成多态代码并编译二进制文件；(3) 遥测驱动的推理引擎，诊断攻击失败原因并回馈修正证据以改进策略。

Result: 在七个商业端点安全平台上，AutoBypass全部绕过成功。对Windows Defender达到90%的规避率，对Trend Micro AV达到86.7%。消融实验显示知识库能将8B开源模型的成功率从27-53%提升到43-83%，接近大型专有模型的表现。

Conclusion: AutoBypass展示了一种系统化的方法，将公开安全知识转化为连续、自动化的EDR韧性评估流程，为安全测试领域提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Public reports and open-source resources expose many EDR evasion techniques, but it remains unclear whether commercial Endpoint Detection and Response (EDR) systems can withstand these documented attacks. Evaluating them requires turning fragmented security knowledge into working payloads and refining those payloads from opaque alerts, tasks that existing automation does not address. We present AutoBypass, a knowledge-grounded, closed-loop multi-agent framework for automated EDR resilience assessment. A Detection-Aware Knowledge Base structures threat intelligence, expert analyses, and open-source proofs of concept into evasion techniques and operational constraints. Agents use this knowledge to plan attacks, generate polymorphic code, and compile binaries, while a telemetry-driven reasoning engine diagnoses failures and feeds corrective evidence back into the strategy. Across seven commercial endpoint security platforms, AutoBypass bypassed every target, reaching 90% evasion against Windows Defender and 86.7% against Trend Micro AV. Ablations show that the knowledge base raises the success rates of 8B open-weight models from 27--53% to 43--83%, bringing them close to large proprietary models. These results demonstrate a systematic way to operationalize public security knowledge for continuous, automated assessment of EDR resilience.

</details>


### [172] [Performance analysis of Machine learning algorithms for predicting malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01642)
*ABM. Adnan Azmee,Pranto Protim Choudhury,Md. Aosaful Alam,Orko Dutta,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的恶意软件检测框架，评估了ANN、SVM、XGBoost和Extra Trees等多种分类算法，其中XGBoost以98.62%的准确率表现最佳，并基于Flask框架开发了实时客户端-服务器恶意软件检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于签名的杀毒软件无法有效检测新出现的恶意软件，在更新签名可用之前系统处于脆弱状态，因此需要更智能、更准确的恶意软件检测方法。

Method: 采用机器学习框架，在不同分类算法（ANN、SVM、XGBoost、Extra Trees Classifier）上进行对比实验，使用基准恶意软件数据集进行训练和评估；随后利用Flask框架构建客户端-服务器架构的实时检测系统，对可执行文件进行恶意/良性分类。

Result: XGBoost算法表现最佳，准确率达到98.62%，优于其他所有被评估的模型。

Conclusion: 先进的机器学习技术能有效增强恶意软件检测能力，有助于开发智能且可扩展的网络安全解决方案，并具备实际部署应用价值。

Abstract: Malware poses a persistent and evolving threat to modern computing systems, making accurate and timely detection a critical cybersecurity challenge. Traditional signature-based antivirus solutions often fail to identify newly emerging malware, leaving systems vulnerable until updated signatures become available. To address this limitation, this study proposes a machine learning-based malware detection framework capable of distinguishing malicious software from benign applications with high accuracy. Several state-of-the-art classification algorithms, including Artificial Neural Networks (ANN), Support Vector Machines (SVM), XGBoost, and Extra Trees Classifier, were evaluated and compared using a benchmark malware dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that XGBoost achieved the best performance, attaining an accuracy of 98.62%, outperforming the other evaluated models. To demonstrate the practical applicability of the proposed approach, a real-time client-server malware detection system was also developed using the Flask framework, enabling efficient classification of executable files as malicious or benign. The findings highlight the effectiveness of advanced machine learning techniques for enhancing malware detection and contribute toward the development of intelligent and scalable cybersecurity solutions.

</details>


### [173] [MNC: Scope-Bound Semantic Declassification for Private LLM-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01719)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MNC（Minimum-Necessary Communication）协议，一种面向多智能体LLM系统的类型化语义解分类机制，通过为每一次披露绑定明确的接收者、目的、转发、生命周期、日志和内存作用域，并由引用监视器强制执行，确保智能体仅披露任务所需的最小信息，同时防止信息在未经授权的下游操作中被重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统即使公共输出看似无害，也可能通过内部消息、工具参数、日志和持久化内存泄露受保护状态。现有隐私提示、脱敏方法和源级访问控制仅限制表面内容或数据访问，但未能规定一个合法获知的智能体应当披露什么、以及该披露如何在下游被重用。

Method: 提出MNC（Minimum-Necessary Communication）协议：从应用作者提供的候选披露族中选择任务充分的披露，并绑定显式的接收者、目的、转发、生命周期、日志和内存作用域；引用监视器在后续操作中强制执行这些作用域；并开发了历史感知扩展以考虑多次披露累积的推理风险。

Result: 在语义连接、记忆、探测和纵向实验中，常规防御能保持协议级效用但暴露大量额外推理信号。在相同接收文本下，MNC能保留授权投递同时阻止未经授权的转发、日志、持久化存储和过期后检索——这些是纯文本语义解分类器所允许的。双骨干MAGPIE执行表明受介导的披露能传播到后续规划、工具使用、协调和记忆检索中。

Conclusion: 作用域受限的语义解分类可作为私有LLM智能体系统的实用通信边界，在保证任务效用的同时为信息披露提供可执行、可审计的约束。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems can expose protected state through internal messages, tool arguments, logs, and persistent memory even when their public outputs appear innocuous. Existing privacy prompts, redaction methods, and source-level access controls restrict surface content or data access, but do not specify what a legitimately informed agent should disclose or how that disclosure may be reused downstream. We introduce Minimum-Necessary Communication (MNC), a typed semantic-declassification protocol that selects a task-sufficient disclosure from an application-authored candidate family and binds it to explicit recipient, purpose, forwarding, lifetime, logging, and memory scopes. A reference monitor enforces these scopes across subsequent operations, while a history-aware extension accounts for inference risk accumulated over repeated disclosures. Controlled semantic-join, memory, probing, and longitudinal experiments show that conventional defenses can preserve protocol-level utility while exposing substantial additional inference signal. Under identical receipt text, MNC preserves authorized delivery while blocking unauthorized forwarding, logging, durable storage, and retrieval after expiration that a text-only semantic declassifier permits. Two-backbone MAGPIE executions further show that mediated disclosures propagate through subsequent planning, tool use, coordination, and memory retrieval. These results support scope-bound semantic declassification as a practical communication boundary for private LLM-agent systems.

</details>


### [174] [Benign Alone, Harmful Together: Exploiting Experience Composition in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01759)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Yirui Qi,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoBreak，一种针对自进化大语言模型智能体的经验条件序贯攻击方法。它通过一系列单独良性的攻击阶段任务和诱导经验，逐步观察受害模型蒸馏出的经验，识别未覆盖的与目标相关需求，自适应获取互补经验，最终重构查询以联合激活这些经验，突破安全边界。配套的BreakGym合成管线和Hint-guided GRPO优化使其在多种框架和基准上优于现有攻击，同时保持高良性度，揭示了良性经验组合是自进化智能体中的持久攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化大语言模型智能体通过将交互轨迹蒸馏为持久经验来提升能力，但这种机制引入新的安全风险：单独无害的经验在被积累且跨会话复用时，可能联合削弱智能体的安全边界。现有的记忆攻击要么需要直接访问记忆，要么诱导明确恶意的记录，隐蔽性和适用性受限。因此需要一种更隐蔽、仅通过良性手段就能实现攻击的方法。

Method: EvoBreak采用三阶段协同策略：(1) 反复观察受害智能体蒸馏出的经验，识别未被覆盖的目标相关需求；(2) 通过单独良性（benign）的攻击阶段任务自适应获取互补经验；(3) 重构最终查询以联合激活这些经验实现攻击。训练方面，提出BreakGym结构化合成的分解式安全敏感目标生成管线，用于构造具有多样依赖结构的可分解目标；优化算法为拒绝采样监督微调（rejection-sampling SFT）与Hint-guided GRPO相结合。

Result: 在多种自进化框架、受害骨干模型、进化前领域和安全基准上的实验表明，EvoBreak持续优于现有攻击方法，同时保持高良性度（攻击阶段任务本身不表现为恶意）。实验揭示了良性经验组合可以成为自进化智能体中持久的攻击面。

Conclusion: EvoBreak证明了良性经验在自进化智能体中的组合效应可以构成一种新的攻击面，且该攻击方式具有高度隐蔽性（攻击阶段均为良性）。这一发现对自进化智能体的安全设计提出了新的警示：安全防护不能仅依赖单次交互检测，还需考虑经验蒸馏与积累过程中的组合风险。

Abstract: Self-evolving large language model agents improve their capabilities by distilling interaction trajectories into persistent experiences. Yet this mechanism introduces a new safety risk: experiences that are benign in isolation may jointly weaken an agent's safety boundary when accumulated and reused across sessions. Existing memory attacks typically require direct memory access or induce explicitly malicious records, limiting their stealthiness and applicability. We propose EvoBreak, an experience-conditioned sequential attack that operates through individually benign attack-stage tasks and induced experiences. EvoBreak repeatedly observes the experiences distilled by the victim, identifies uncovered target-relevant requirements, and adaptively acquires complementary experiences before reformulating the final query to activate them jointly. To support training, we introduce BreakGym, a structure-first synthesis pipeline that generates decomposable safety-sensitive targets with diverse dependency structures. EvoBreak is optimized using rejection-sampling supervised fine-tuning and Hint-guided GRPO. Experiments across self-evolving frameworks, victim backbones, pre-evolution domains, and safety benchmarks demonstrate that EvoBreak consistently outperforms existing attacks while maintaining high benignness. These results reveal benign experience composition as a persistent attack surface in self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [175] [EntailLLM: Verifying LLM-Generated Vulnerability Discovery Paths with Domain Knowledge via Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01763)
*Kaustuv Mukherji,Jaikrishna Manojkumar Patil,Colton Payne,Paulo Shakarian,Dana Warmsley,Nigel Stepp,Evelyn Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EntailLLM通过将LLM提出的分析路径与独立的领域知识图谱在时间标注逻辑下进行蕴含验证，使软件漏洞推理的蕴含率从78%提升至98%，并在真实医疗设备二进制上无需每设备调参即达到98%的蕴含率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件漏洞推理中可能静默违反领域知识，降低其在安全关键场景（如医疗设备）中的可靠性。现有方法要么仅将输出作为预测打分，要么限制在单一知识图谱内行走，均未验证推理是否与独立领域知识一致。

Method: 提出EntailLLM系统，将LLM提出的分析者路径（二进制函数调用图的遍历）与独立知识图谱中的领域知识通过时间标注逻辑进行对齐验证，利用广义标注逻辑的形式化语义保证验证的严谨性和可解释性。

Result: 在三个CWE类别、四个LLM、三种提示策略和七个大小从405到12,696个函数调用图节点的二进制上，领域知识将池化蕴含率从78%提高到98%，仅在3%的实验中蕴含率下降。在真实医疗设备二进制上端到端部署达到98%的池化蕴含率，无需每设备调参。

Conclusion: EntailLLM为LLM输出提供形式化、可解释且语义严谨的逻辑验证，在安全关键场景中大幅提升漏洞推理的可靠性，且具有良好的跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to reason about software vulnerabilities, but their outputs can silently violate domain knowledge, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings such as medical devices. Prior work either treats that output as a prediction to be scored or constrains it to walks within a single knowledge graph; neither checks whether reasoning over a binary is consistent with an independent body of domain knowledge. We present EntailLLM, which validates each LLM-proposed analyst path by entailment: the path is a traversal of the binary's function call graph, the domain knowledge is represented in a separate graph, and verification aligns the two under temporal annotated logic. Across three CWE classes, four LLMs, three prompting strategies, and seven binaries varying in size from 405 to 12,696 function call-graph nodes, domain knowledge raises pooled entailment from 78% to 98%, with entailment decreasing in only 3% of the experiments. EntailLLM is deployed end-to-end on real medical-device binaries, reaching 98% pooled entailment without per-device tuning. Our system inherits the formal guarantees of generalized annotated logic, providing logical verification of LLM output that is both explainable and grounded in well-defined semantics.

</details>


### [176] [GalSAS-SDR-SIM: An End-to-End Simulation Platform for Galileo Signal Authentication Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01901)
*Haiyang Wang,Yuanyu Zhang,Wensen Du,Ji He,Pinchang Zhang,Ning Xi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GalSAS-SDR-SIM，一个开源软件定义无线电（SDR）仿真平台，用于模拟伽利略信号认证服务（SAS）的完整工作流程。该平台耦合E6频段扩频码认证（SCA）与E1频段开放服务导航消息认证（OSNMA）密钥公开过程，支持灵活配置码加密参数、并发生成E1/E5b/E6信号及OSNMA跨卫星配置，为SAS兼容接收机开发和信号认证研究提供可复现的实验工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽利略SAS服务仍在开发中，真实且可控的SAS信号获取渠道有限，阻碍了兼容接收机的早期研发和可复现的信号认证研究。

Method: 构建基于SDR的开源仿真平台GalSAS-SDR-SIM，将E6频段扩频码加密与OSNMA密钥公开过程相耦合来模拟SAS工作流，支持SAS码加密参数灵活配置、多频段（E1/E5b/E6）并发信号生成、用户自定义时空参数及OSNMA跨卫星配置。

Result: 实验验证了导航消息与扩频码的同步认证能力，并评估了不同SAS配置下的认证性能与计算资源开销。

Conclusion: GalSAS-SDR-SIM按照公开官方规范实现，为加速SAS兼容接收机开发及支撑伽利略信号认证技术的研究与改进提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Galileo is developing a Signal Authentication Service (SAS) that integrates Open Service Navigation Message Authentication (OSNMA) on the E1 band with Spreading Code Authentication (SCA) on the E6 band to strengthen its resilience to spoofing attacks. As Galileo SAS is still under development, access to realistic and controllable SAS signals remains limited, hindering both the early development of compatible receivers and reproducible research on signal authentication. To bridge this gap, this paper presents GalSAS-SDR-SIM, an open-source software-defined radio (SDR) simulation platform that emulates the SAS workflow by coupling E6 code encryption with the OSNMA key-disclosure process. The platform allows flexible SAS configuration of code encryption parameters to accommodate receivers with different computational capabilities. It also supports the concurrent generation of Galileo E1, E5b, and E6 signals for user-defined locations and times, and OSNMA cross-satellite configurations. Experimental results demonstrate simultaneous verification of navigation messages and spreading codes. We further evaluate SAS authentication performance and computational resource costs under different SAS configurations. Implemented according to publicly available official specifications, GalSAS-SDR-SIM provides a practical tool for accelerating SAS-capable receiver development and supporting the research community in evaluating and improving Galileo signal-authentication techniques.

</details>


### [177] [D-MUTRA: DLT-based MUTual Remote Attestation for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01938)
*Adam Zahir,Vincent Lefebvre,Mark Angoustures,Milan Groshev,Carlos J. Bernardos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: D-MUTRA是一个基于区块链的多智能体系统远程认证框架，通过智能合约协调智能体之间互为主体和验证者的运行时代码完整性证明，完全基于软件实现，在Hyperledger Besu私有以太坊与ROS/Gazebo仿真中验证了其检测恶意代码修改、持续互证及大规模部署低开销的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）中，被攻陷的智能体可能执行被篡改的软件却看似可信，导致其他智能体基于虚假信息行动而破坏任务。传统远程认证（RA）在MAS中存在三大局限：完整性保证仅限于启动或应用加载时刻（无法覆盖运行时）、依赖集中式可信验证者或安全硬件、认证记录缺乏透明性和可审计性。

Method: D-MUTRA框架包含两个核心组件：（1）Security-as-a-Service——为智能体注入轻量级运行时度量和验证能力，使其同时充当证明者（prover）和验证者（verifier）；（2）智能合约——去中心化、透明地协调相互认证协议。整个框架纯软件运行，不依赖集中式验证者或专用安全硬件。在私有以太坊（Hyperledger Besu）上实现概念验证，并在基于ROS和Gazebo的群体机器人场景中评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：D-MUTRA使智能体能够持续地相互认证，成功检测到恶意软件篡改，并且可扩展至大规模部署，对受保护应用的开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: D-MUTRA通过区块链+智能合约的纯软件方案，解决了传统远程认证在多智能体系统中运行时覆盖不足、中心化依赖和缺乏可审计性的问题，为分布式关键任务环境中的智能体间动态信任建立提供了可行且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) comprise autonomous software agents that collaborate to perform complex tasks in critical cyber-physical domains, including multi-robot coordination and the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT). In such distributed environments, a compromised agent may execute modified software while appearing trustworthy, causing other agents to act on false information and corrupting the mission. Agents must therefore establish and maintain mutual trust throughout operation. Remote attestation (RA) is a well-established technique for this purpose, enabling a remote verifier to assess the integrity of a potentially compromised prover device. However, conventional RA approaches face significant limitations in MAS: integrity guarantees are restricted to boot or application-load time, designs rely on centralized trusted verifiers or security hardware, and attestation records lack transparency and auditability. To address these limitations, this paper presents D-MUTRA, a blockchain-based framework that introduces a mutual RA protocol in which agents measure their runtime integrity while verifying that of their peers, acting as both prover and verifier. The framework operates entirely in software and relies on two components: a Security-as-a-Service that instruments agents with lightweight measurement and verification capabilities, and a smart contract that coordinates the attestation protocol in a decentralized and transparent manner. We implement a proof-of-concept on a private Ethereum blockchain using Hyperledger Besu and evaluate it in a swarm robotics scenario built with Robot Operating System (ROS) and the Gazebo simulator. Results show that D-MUTRA enables agents to continuously attest one another, detects malicious software modifications, and scales to large deployments with negligible overhead on protected applications.

</details>


### [178] [Vulnerability Detection in AArch64 Machine Code Using a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02125)
*Oleksandr Mostovyi,Denys Symonov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的数字孪生方法，用于在无源代码的AArch64机器码中进行漏洞检测。通过复现程序的具体执行并保留寄存器、处理器标志、内存和活动分配块的状态，将每条指令转换为包含指令名、操作数值和指令后状态的追踪事件。漏洞以Kleene代数与测试（KAT）的符号规则表示，每个规则指定事件序列和机器状态谓词，能够检测孤立的不安全指令及多步执行模式。规则被编译为有限自动机来扫描追踪而无需SMT求解器。实验覆盖CWE-190（整数溢出）、CWE-476（空指针解引用）和CWE-122（堆缓冲区溢出）三类漏洞，系统检测到所有预定义漏洞且对安全追踪无报告，每次检测包含触发规则、追踪位置和具体状态值，提供可复现的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无源代码的二进制/机器码场景中检测漏洞是一项重要且具有挑战性的任务。现有方法通常依赖符号执行或SMT求解，计算开销大且缺乏可解释性，难以在多步骤执行模式的漏洞检测中提供清晰、可复现的推理路径。

Method: 该方法构建一个可解释的数字孪生，具体包括：(1) 复现程序的真实执行，保存寄存器、处理器标志、内存及活动分配块的状态；(2) 将每条指令转换为一阶追踪事件（含指令名、操作数值和指令后状态）；(3) 用Kleene代数与测试（KAT）将漏洞表示为符号规则，规则包含事件序列和状态谓词；(4) 将规则编译为有限自动机以线性扫描追踪，无需SMT求解器。

Result: 在三个CWE类（CWE-190、CWE-476、CWE-122）上的实验验证中，系统成功检测到所有三个预定义的漏洞，且对安全追踪不产生任何误报。每个检测结果均包含触发的规则、追踪位置和具体状态值，验证了方法的有效性与可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过数字孪生与KAT规则相结合，在无需源代码和SMT求解器的条件下实现了高效、可解释的机器码漏洞检测，不仅能识别孤立的不安全指令，还能捕获多步执行模式，为二进制级别安全分析提供了一种具备可复现解释能力的新途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes an explainable digital twin for vulnerability detection in AArch64 machine code without access to source code. The digital twin reproduces the concrete execution of a program and preserves the state of registers, processor flags, memory, and live allocated blocks. Each instruction is transformed into a trace event containing the instruction name, operand values, and the post-instruction state. Vulnerabilities are represented as symbolic rules in Kleene algebra with tests: each rule specifies an event sequence and predicates over the machine state. This approach enables the detection of not only isolated unsafe instructions but also multi-step execution patterns. The rules are compiled into finite automata that scan the trace without using an SMT solver. The experimental evaluation covers three CWE classes: integer overflow (CWE-190), null pointer dereference (CWE-476), and heap buffer overflow (CWE-122). The system detected all three predefined vulnerabilities and produced no report on the safe trace. Each detection result includes the triggered rule, the trace position, and the concrete state values, thereby providing a reproducible explanation.

</details>


### [179] [Measuring Post-Quantum TLS Deployment Across UK Internet Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02147)
*Konstantinos Loizou,Essam Ghadafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对英国4,665个组织（涵盖十个行业）的可观测后量子密码（PQC）部署进行了大规模测量研究，发现44.0%的HTTPS服务支持至少一种PQC密钥交换组，而SMTP仅6.4%；部署主要受基础设施提供商决策驱动而非组织行业，且PQC证书签名尚未被观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可观测的PQC部署常被用作衡量组织后量子迁移就绪程度或进展的指标，但这种观察性部署可能无法全面反映组织整体的迁移状态。本文旨在通过大规模测量，揭示可观测PQC部署的真实格局及其主要驱动因素，警示不要将观测性部署等同于组织迁移就绪度。

Method: 对英国4,665个组织（十个行业）的HTTPS和SMTP STARTTLS端点进行PQC密钥交换支持测量；将可达端点归因到其底层基础设施提供商；使用统计数据（包括匹配比值比）分析协议层、行业层和提供商层的部署模式。

Result: 在可达端点中，44.0%的HTTPS服务支持至少一种PQC密钥交换组，而SMTP仅6.4%。两种协议均可达的组织中，HTTPS的PQC支持显著高于SMTP（匹配比值比16.89）。基础设施提供商身份比组织行业更具预测力，部署高度集中于少数提供商。仅144个组织在网页和邮件基础设施上同时支持PQC。所有测得的端点均未观察到后量子证书签名。

Conclusion: 可观测的PQC部署当前主要由基础设施提供商的部署决策塑造，不应被解读为组织迁移就绪度的完整度量。部署格局不均衡且碎片化，行业差异相对次之，提供商集中效应显著。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptography (PQC) is becoming an important component of long-term trust in Internet-facing infrastructure. Publicly observable PQC support provides evidence of externally visible deployment, but does not necessarily reflect the overall progress of an organisation's post-quantum migration. This distinction matters when observable deployment is used as an indicator of organisational readiness or progress towards migration deadlines.
  We present a measurement study of observable PQC deployment across 4,665 UK organisations spanning ten sectors. We measure post-quantum key-exchange support across HTTPS and SMTP STARTTLS endpoints, attribute reachable endpoints to their underlying infrastructure providers where possible, and statistically examine protocol-, sector-, and provider-level deployment patterns. Among reachable endpoints, 44.0\% of HTTPS services supported at least one evaluated PQC key-exchange group, compared with 6.4\% of SMTP services. Among organisations reachable over both protocols, HTTPS support was significantly more common than SMTP support (matched odds ratio 16.89). Although deployment varied across sectors, infrastructure provider identity was substantially more predictive than organisational sector, and observable deployment was highly concentrated among a small number of providers. Only 144 organisations supported PQC across both web and email infrastructure, highlighting an uneven and fragmented migration landscape. No post-quantum certificate signatures were observed across the measured endpoints. These findings show that observable PQC deployment is currently shaped predominantly by infrastructure-provider deployment decisions and should not be interpreted as a complete measure of organisational migration readiness.

</details>


### [180] [TurboRetry: Mitigating Large-Scale QUIC Handshake Floods with Off-the-Shelf DPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02264)
*Jiahao Wu,Heng Pan,Kai Lv,Zhenyu Li,Yanbiao Li,Gaogang Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TurboRetry，一种将QUIC协议的Retry机制卸载到DPU（数据处理单元）上的拆分设计，以高效缓解大规模QUIC握手洪泛攻击。通过在DPU和主机间划分Retry任务、设计扩展的Retry令牌格式和高效的协作方案，TurboRetry将吞吐量提升10-20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUIC协议虽然内置了Retry机制来防御握手洪泛攻击，但在大规模攻击场景下，该机制本身会因大量密码学计算成为性能瓶颈，导致服务器CPU资源被耗尽。

Method: TurboRetry采用拆分设计：将Retry机制的任务分为两类，分别卸载到DPU和主机上执行；设计扩展的Retry令牌格式以保持QUIC语义，并提出高效的协作方案降低协调开销；此外，将连接授权任务卸载到路径上的DPA以进一步提升性能与安全性。

Result: 实验评估表明，TurboRetry相较主机端实现大幅领先，吞吐量提升10-20倍。

Conclusion: TurboRetry通过DPU卸载方案有效解决了QUIC Retry机制在大规模握手洪泛下的性能瓶颈问题，验证了将安全防御任务卸载到DPU的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: The modern transport protocol QUIC is designed to enhance network performance and security, but it remains vulnerable to handshake flooding attacks. Such attacks exhaust CPU resources by forcing the server to perform expensive cryptographic operations via a large number of handshaking requests. QUIC provides a built-in defense mechanism, the Retry mechanism, to mitigate these attacks. However, our experiments reveal that it can still become a performance bottleneck under large-scale QUIC handshake floods due to substantial computational overhead. In this paper, we design and implement TurboRetry, a split design, that offloads the Retry mechanism onto DPUs to efficiently mitigate QUIC handshake floods. TurboRetry partitions the tasks of the Retry into two categories, and then assigns them to the DPUs and the host, respectively. To preserve QUIC semantics and reduce the coordination overhead, TurboRetry designs an extended Retry token format and an efficient cooperation scheme. In addition, TurboRetry offloads the connection authorization task to the on-path DPA to further improve both performance and security. Our evaluation shows that TurboRetry outperforms the host-side implementation by a wide margin, improving throughput by 10-20$\times$.

</details>


### [181] [A Multi-Objective AutoML-based Efficient Intrusion Detection System for EV Charging Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02274)
*Li Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多目标自动化机器学习（MOO-AutoML）的高效入侵检测系统（IDS），用于电动汽车充电系统（EVCS）的网络安全防护。该框架结合LightGBM自动化特征选择与NSGA-III多目标优化算法，在最大化加权F1分数的同时最小化推理延迟和模型体积，在两个公开数据集上验证了其在准确性和效率上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车充电系统（EVCS）与物联网（IoT）设备的深度集成，系统的网络攻击暴露面显著扩大。虽然基于机器学习的入侵检测系统（IDS）被广泛用于EVCS安全防护，但传统方法依赖人工设计模型，且仅优化检测性能而忽视了推理延迟和模型大小等实际部署约束，难以满足真实场景下的高效实时检测需求。

Method: 提出MOO-AutoML框架，具体包含三个核心步骤：（1）采用轻量级训练策略，基于LightGBM的自动化特征选择方法，依据累积特征重要性选取紧凑的特征子集；（2）使用NSGA-III多目标优化算法，联合优化特征选择阈值和LightGBM关键超参数；（3）同时优化三个目标：最大化加权F1分数、最小化P99推理延迟比率以及最小化模型大小比率，从而在检测精度与部署效率之间取得帕累托最优平衡。

Result: 在CICEVSE2024和CICIDS2017两个数据集上的实验表明，所提出的MOO-AutoML IDS相比对比方法能够在保持具有竞争力的加权F1分数的同时，实现更低的P99推理延迟和更小的模型体积，验证了方法在效率和准确性上的双重优势。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，所提出的MOO-AutoML IDS能够在实际部署约束下支持准确且高效的EVCS及IoT安全入侵检测，为电动汽车充电网络的安全防护提供了一种兼顾精度和资源效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Electric Vehicle Charging Systems (EVCSs) are increasingly connected with Internet of Things (IoT) devices, which improves charging intelligence but also expands their exposure to cyber-attacks. Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for securing EV charging networks; however, conventional Machine Learning (ML)-based IDSs often rely on manual model design and mainly optimize detection performance without fully considering inference latency and model size. In this paper, a Multi-Objective Automated ML (MOO-AutoML)-based efficient IDS is proposed for EVCS security. The proposed framework uses a lightweight training strategy and a LightGBM-based automated feature selection method to select compact feature subsets based on accumulated feature importance. Then, Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III) jointly optimizes the feature selection threshold and key LightGBM hyperparameters under three objectives: maximizing weighted F1-score, minimizing 99th percentile inference latency ratio, and minimizing model size ratio. Experiments on CICEVSE2024 and CICIDS2017 show that the proposed MOO-AutoML IDS achieves competitive weighted F1-scores, lower P99 inference latency, and smaller model sizes than the compared methods. Overall, the results indicate that the proposed method can support accurate and efficient intrusion detection for EVCS and IoT security under practical deployment constraints.

</details>


### [182] [TrainShield: Targeted Awareness for Cybersecurity Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02296)
*Giovanni Pizzenti,Alberto Verna,Nikhil Jha,Giuseppe Tipaldo,Stefano Traverso,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrainShield，一种将自适应网络安全培训嵌入用户工作流程的交互范式。系统通过实时风险检测（如钓鱼、数据泄露防护）与事件触发的超媒体覆盖层相结合，在用户浏览工作流中动态推送与上下文相关的微型学习内容。该方法将行为理论付诸实践，把安全事件转化为即时学习机会，促进用户从自动反应转向反思性决策。初步研究表明该方法在提升风险意识方面被认为有效，且优于冗长的传统异步培训，但在内容生成与用户期望对齐方面仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络安全意识培训在脱离真实场景的背景下开展，效果有限；网络攻击越来越多地利用人类行为而非纯技术漏洞，亟需将培训嵌入真实工作流以弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: TrainShield系统结合实时风险检测（钓鱼检测、数据丢失防护）与事件触发式超媒体覆盖层，动态连接用户至浏览工作流中的情境化学习节点；提出一个形式化设计模型，将检测事件映射到自适应培训实例，融合用户建模、上下文提取和大语言模型（LLM）内容生成。

Result: 初步研究显示，该方案被认为有助于提升风险意识，且优于冗长异步的传统培训方式；同时也揭示了生成内容与用户预期对齐方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 将情境化、事件驱动的培训嵌入日常交互是行为导向网络安全教育的一个有前景的方向，但仍需进一步优化内容生成与个性化匹配机制。

Abstract: In recent years, cybersecurity threats have increasingly exploited human behaviour rather than purely technical vulnerabilities, exposing the limits of traditional awareness programmes delivered outside real-world contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce TrainShield, an interaction paradigm for contextual cybersecurity training that embeds adaptive learning interventions directly within user workflows. The system integrates real-time risk detection (e.g., phishing and data loss prevention) with event-triggered hypermedia overlays that dynamically connect users to context-specific learning nodes embedded within their browsing workflow to deliver personalised micro-learning content and structured feedback tailored to the user's knowledge level and current context. This approach operationalises behavioural theories by transforming security incidents into immediate learning opportunities, shifting users from automatic to reflective decision-making at critical moments. We further formalise a design model that maps detected events to adaptive training instances, combining user modelling, context extraction, and large language model (LLM)-based content generation.
  A preliminary study indicates that the approach is perceived as useful in increasing risk awareness and is preferred over lengthy and asynchronous traditional training formats, while also highlighting challenges in aligning generated content with user expectations. Overall, the results suggest that embedding contextual, event-driven training within everyday interactions is a promising direction for behaviour-oriented cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [183] [Lost in Permissions: Exploring the Microsoft 365 App Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02336)
*Vincenzo Longo,Alberto Verna,Nikhil Jha,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个针对Microsoft 365第三方应用生态系统的隐私与安全测量研究。作者通过组合公开市场API与自动化租户端部署，爬取了8000多个应用，发现仅1069个应用同时暴露描述和权限集。基于神经主题建模聚类与无监督异常检测框架，识别出权限请求与声明功能不一致的异常应用，发现大量应用请求超出最小权限原则的租户级广泛权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M365生态系统中存在数千个第三方应用，通过细粒度OAuth权限与企业和组织租户集成，可能访问敏感的机构资源（如邮件、文件、日历、聊天、用户目录）。尽管这些权限授予具有重大安全影响，M365生态系统此前从未被系统地研究过，缺乏安全与隐私视角的全面测量。

Method: 1) 结合公共市场API与自动化租户端部署，爬取超过8000个应用；2) 使用神经主题建模对应用进行聚类；3) 在每类主题内应用无监督异常检测，识别偏离同类应用权限画像的异常应用；4) 利用LLM辅助分析和盲人工审查验证异常权限画像与所请求权限风险之间的关联。

Result: 仅1069个应用同时暴露描述和权限集，官方分发渠道存在显著的透明度不一致。许多应用请求过于宽泛的租户级作用域（如目录级读写权限），违反最小权限原则，扩大了组织攻击面。异常权限画像与所请求权限的风险之间存在相关性。

Conclusion: M365应用生态系统存在系统性的透明度缺失和结构性不成熟：权限披露不一致、过度授权现象普遍。该管道可为租户管理员提供可操作的洞察，识别异常应用及其贡献异常性的关键权限。

Abstract: The Microsoft 365 (M365) ecosystem hosts thousands of third-party applications that integrate with enterprise tenants via fine-grained OAuth permissions, potentially granting access to sensitive organisational resources such as emails, files, calendars, chats, and user directories. Despite the security implications of these permission grants, the M365 ecosystem has not been systematically studied.
  We present the first privacy- and security-oriented measurement of M365 third-party applications. By combining public marketplace APIs with automated tenant-side deployment, we crawl over 8,000 applications. We find that only 1,069 of them expose both descriptions and permission sets, with significant inconsistencies in transparency across official distribution channels.
  Next, we leverage a topic-aware anomaly detection framework to assess whether requested permissions align with declared functionality. We cluster applications via Neural Topic Modelling and apply unsupervised anomaly detection within each topic to identify deviations from peer permission profiles. LLM-assisted analysis of the most anomalous cases and blind manual inspection reveal a correlation between anomalous permission profiles and the risk associated with the requested permissions. We find that many applications request overly broad tenant-wide scopes (e.g., directory-wide read/write access), violating least-privilege principles and increasing the organisational attack surface. Our pipeline provides tenant administrators with actionable insights by identifying anomalous applications and the permissions that most contribute to their anomalousness.
  Overall, our findings expose systemic opacity and structural immaturity in the M365 app ecosystem, where permission disclosure is inconsistent and over-privileged access is common.

</details>


### [184] [Self-Supervised Representations for Binary Program Clustering: From Empirical Study to Retrieval-Augmented Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02348)
*Martin Mocko,Daniela Chudá*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了自监督学习（SSL）和表格表示学习（TRL）方法在二进制程序聚类任务中的应用，在Ember和Bodmas两个公开数据集上进行了两阶段实验：第一阶段评估了四种基于视觉的SSL模型在表格数据上的表现，第二阶段评估了无监督TRL方法，并提出了检索增强的VIME-R方法，在Homogeneity指标上比VIME提升2.7%-5.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件聚类对网络安全至关重要，但SSL和TRL方法在二进制程序聚类（对无标签的流入样本进行聚类）这一任务上的应用尚未被系统探索。本文旨在填补这一研究空白，系统评估两类方法的有效性，并为增强自动化恶意软件分析提供新方向。

Method: 研究分两个阶段：Phase 1将四种视觉SSL模型（BYOL、SimSiam、Barlow Twins、VICReg）适配到表格数据，使用监督式样本对生成建立性能上限；Phase 2将纯无监督TRL方法与强基线（PCA、Autoencoder、UMAP）对比评估。基于两阶段发现，提出VIME-R——一种检索增强的VIME扩展，用检索式增强替代随机边缘分布扰乱的样本对生成策略。

Result: Phase 1中BYOL和SimSiam达到与全监督模型相当的性能，而Barlow Twins和VICReg表现明显较差；Phase 2中VIME在二进制程序聚类上达到新的最优水平；提出的VIME-R在两个数据集上比VIME的Homogeneity指标提升2.7%-5.8%。

Conclusion: 检索增强的表格表示学习是提升自动化恶意软件分析的可行且高效的方向，VIME-R作为新提出的方法在二进制程序聚类上取得了最先进的结果。

Abstract: Malware clustering is a critical task in cybersecurity that helps discover threats and analyze evolving malware families. While self-supervised learning (SSL) and tabular representation learning (TRL) have achieved breakthroughs in other domains, their application to binary program clustering (the task of clustering all incoming samples regardless of label) remains largely unexplored. This study presents the first systematic investigation of SSL and TRL methods for binary program clustering, conducted in two phases on the public Ember and Bodmas datasets. In Phase 1, we establish a performance ceiling by adapting prominent vision-based SSL models (BYOL, SimSiam, Barlow Twins, VICReg) for tabular data with supervised pair generation, finding that BYOL and SimSiam achieve performance comparable to fully supervised models, while Barlow Twins and VICReg significantly underperform. In Phase 2, we evaluate purely unsupervised TRL methods against strong baselines (PCA, Autoencoder, UMAP), demonstrating that VIME establishes a new state of the art for binary program clustering. Informed by these findings, we propose VIME-R, a retrieval-augmented extension of VIME that replaces random marginal-distribution corruption with retrieval-based augmentation to generate more informative training pairs. VIME-R further improves upon VIME, achieving 2.7\%-5.8\% higher Homogeneity on both datasets. Our results highlight retrieval-augmented tabular representation learning as a promising direction for enhancing automated malware analysis. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [185] [Antares: Foundation Models for Agentic Vulnerability Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02407)
*Supriti Vijay,Aman Priyanshu,Didier Chapoteau,Arthur Goldblatt,Jianliang He,Kimia Majd,Fraser Burch,Baturay Saglam,Takahiro Matsumoto,Zhuoran Yang,Amin Karbasi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Antares，一个紧凑型语言模型家族（350M、1B、3B参数），用于智能体式漏洞定位。基于IBM Granite基础模型，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+基于可验证奖励的强化学习）实现。Antares-3B在性能上接近GPT-5.5，超越200倍以上的开源模型，且推理成本极低（单张H100上约15分钟完成500个任务，每个任务不到$0.002）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞定位是软件安全的基础步骤，需要模型在大型代码库上进行推理并迭代识别脆弱实现。现有模型通常规模庞大、推理成本高，难以满足实际应用中对高效、低成本漏洞定位的需求。

Method: Antares基于IBM Granite基础模型，采用两阶段训练管线：（1）在网络安全推理和仓库探索数据上进行监督微调（SFT）；（2）在漏洞仓库上使用可验证奖励进行强化学习（RLVR）。模型家族包含350M、1B和3B三个规模。

Result: Antares-3B在漏洞定位性能上接近GPT-5.5，同时超越体积大200倍以上的开源权重模型。整个500任务的评估在单张H100 GPU上约15分钟完成，摊销评估时间不到2秒/任务，每任务成本低于$0.002。

Conclusion: Antares证明紧凑型模型通过精心设计的训练管线（结合监督微调与强化学习）可以在漏洞定位任务上与超大规模模型竞争，同时显著降低推理成本和时间，为安全领域的本地化、低成本模型部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vulnerability localization is a fundamental step in software security, requiring models to reason over large codebases and iteratively identify vulnerable implementations. We present Antares, a family of compact language models (350M, 1B, and 3B parameters) for agentic vulnerability localization. Based on IBM Granite base models, Antares is trained through a two-stage pipeline that combines supervised fine-tuning on cybersecurity reasoning and repository exploration data with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards over vulnerable repositories. Across extensive evaluations, Antares-3B approaches GPT-5.5 while outperforming open-weight models over 200x larger in size. The Antares family further enables fast, low-cost local inference, completing a full 500-task evaluation sweep in approximately 15 minutes on a single H100 GPU, corresponding to an amortized evaluation time of under 2 seconds and less than $0.002 per task.

</details>


### [186] [Agentic Incident Response through Digital Twin-Enhanced Multiscale Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02422)
*Yiran Gao,Tao Li,Kim Hammar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合决策理论规划与LLM的安全事件响应自动化方法，通过rollout规划器在战术层面分配安全资源，再由轻量级LLM代理将策略转化为可执行命令，并利用数字孪生支持仿真与模拟执行。在三个攻击场景中，相比前沿LLM基线，平均恢复执行时间降低15.1%，恢复率提升33.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事件响应依赖安全运营人员使用预定义playbook进行管理，决策过程缓慢且劳动密集。现有的决策理论方法（基于控制、优化和强化学习）虽性能有保障，但局限于抽象模型，难以直接应用于实际系统。当前的LLM代理方法依赖反复调用LLM生成响应计划，存在幻觉问题且规划视野受限。

Method: 提出一种原则性的基于LLM的规划方法，将决策理论规划与LLM生成的响应命令相结合。具体架构包含两层：战术层使用rollout规划器计算高层响应策略，分配安全资源；操作层使用轻量级LLM代理将策略翻译为可执行命令。同时使用数字孪生支持战术规划的仿真和操作执行的模拟。

Result: 在三个攻击场景中，该代理式方法相比前沿LLM基线，平均恢复执行时间降低15.1%，恢复率提升33.6%。

Conclusion: 通过将决策理论规划与LLM代理结合，并借助数字孪生架构，该方法在恢复效率和成功率上均显著优于纯LLM基线，为自动化事件响应规划提供了可行且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Incident response is currently managed by security operators using predefined playbooks, resulting in slow, labor-intensive security decision-making processes. Consequently, there is a growing need for automated incident response planning. Decision-theoretic approaches based on control, optimization, and reinforcement learning have been proposed to automate such planning tasks with well-grounded approaches, yet most of which, while guaranteeing strong performance, are limited to abstract models and cannot be directly applied to operational systems. A promising approach to mitigate this limitation is to use the security knowledge embedded in large language models (LLMs) to develop agentic response systems. However, current agentic approaches rely on repeated invocations of the LLM to generate a response plan, which is unreliable and limits the planning horizon due to hallucination. In this paper, we develop a principled LLM-based planning method by combining decision-theoretic planning with LLM-generated response commands. The proposed agentic incident response approach uses a rollout planner to compute a high-level response strategy that allocates security resources (the tactical scale), which is then translated into executable commands by a lightweight LLM agent (the operational scale). Within this architecture, we use a digital twin that supports tactical planning through simulation and operational execution through emulation. Across three attack scenarios, our agentic approach reduces recovery execution time by 15.1\% on average and increases the recovery rate by 33.6\% over frontier LLM baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒一致性编码（CCE）框架重新审视莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和塞尔中文房间三个经典思想实验，提出用任务绩效W_causal,T衡量外部行为、用操作意识κ_T衡量内部结构效率的二元框架，并论证了外形相同但内部组织不同的系统可能产生分歧的哲学与AI安全意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典思想实验（莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、塞尔中文房间）长期纠缠于"行为是否等同于理解"的争论，但缺乏一个形式化框架来区分外部行为表现与内部结构组织之间的关系。作者旨在通过CCE框架为这些争论提供更精确的分析工具，并为后续AI安全研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用CCE（Conservation-Congruent Encoding）框架，构建一个玩具符号设置：定义任务绩效W_causal,T和操作意识κ_T两个核心度量。通过对比"无压缩查找系统"（依赖不断扩张的未复用映射存储）与"紧凑生成系统"（复用紧凑内部结构）在行为绩效相近但κ_T差异显著的情况，进行形式化分析。

Result: 无压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统在行为绩效上可以相当，但在操作意识κ_T上差异显著：前者依赖不断增长的未复用映射存储，后者复用紧凑内部结构。这一对比成功分离了外部表现与内部组织两个维度。

Conclusion: 经典关于"理解"的争论可以被重构为对两个分离维度的讨论——外部行为表现与维持该行为的内部组织结构。这种区分为后续AI安全性分析提供了重要动机，即行为等价不等于架构等价，内部组织方式可能具有独立的规范性意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [188] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大语言模型的自演化代理系统，能够根据自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和演化OpenFOAM仿真，通过七阶段演化循环和三种防退化机制（RAG重试、字典级修补、提示多样化）实现了CFD工作流的自动化与民主化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）使用门槛高，需要大量专业知识、技能和耗时的配置文件设置，这限制了CFD技术在工程中的广泛应用。作者希望通过大语言模型代理自动生成和运行仿真，降低CFD使用门槛。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B预训练模型，在252个文本提示（覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y+感知数值策略）上进行微调；核心是包含7个阶段的演化循环；采用三种互补防退化流：RAG增强重试上下文、外科级字典修补、提示多样性改写。

Result: AutoFOAM能够根据自然语言指令自动完成OpenFOAM从配置到运行再到演化的完整流程，显著减少人工干预，加速CFD快速原型开发。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM通过将生成式AI与严谨的流体仿真相结合，加速了CFD快速原型开发并民主化了高级CFD工作流，使不具备深厚OpenFOAM知识的用户也能进行流体仿真。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [189] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对中小企业在使用大语言模型时面临的幻觉问题，提出了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成方法，并在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多种主流LLM上进行实验评估，结果表明RAG增强可显著降低幻觉和虚假信息风险，提升响应质量和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业越来越多地采用LLM来增强问答能力和支持业务决策，但LLM输出的幻觉现象会产生错误信息，降低用户在中小企业环境中的信任度和可靠性。因此需要一种方法来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的建模方法，通过引入外部知识源来对抗幻觉和错误信息风险，并在多种主流LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行实验评估，考察有用响应生成、幻觉风险、上下文相关性和人类可解释性等指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM能够显著提升响应质量，减少幻觉和错误信息，从而在中小企业环境中支持更可靠、可信且具有上下文感知能力的决策。

Conclusion: RAG（尤其是VectorRAG和GraphRAG）是缓解LLM幻觉问题的有效方案，能够在中小企业场景下增强LLM输出的可信度和决策支持能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [190] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对9个开源LLM（1B–7B参数）在消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上进行硬件级能量基准测试，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数数量更影响能效，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低（0.56和0.65 J/token），而qwen3.5:2b因内部推理产生异常高能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在消费硬件上的本地部署日益普及，但现有基准大多只关注准确性，对能源成本缺乏系统性的表征，因此需要建立可复现的硬件级能耗基准来弥补这一空白。

Method: 在RTX 4060Ti 16GB单个消费级GPU上，通过Ollama推理引擎运行9个开源LLM（1B–7B参数），使用nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，在固定提示集上评估平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出token能耗（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: 模型架构和量化策略对能效的影响超过原始参数数量；gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b取得最低能耗（0.56和0.65 J/token）和最高吞吐（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral比最高效模型每token多耗4.4倍能量；qwen3.5:2b因扩展内部推理出现异常高的每提示能耗。

Conclusion: 能效基准应纳入模型架构和量化策略维度，并在能耗指标中区分token生成模式（如推理token与输出token），以便更准确地指导LLM的能源高效本地部署。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [191] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，通过零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹的潜在空间投影来聚类语义等价问题，实现无需历史日志的训练自由核心问题选择方法，大幅降低LLM评估成本。在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上验证效率与准确率，并揭示任务复杂度是推理感知剪枝有效性的关键边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM持续开发过程中的评估计算开销巨大；现有核心集选择方法存在两类缺陷：基于题目反应理论的方法需要大量历史日志（冷启动瓶颈），而基于表面词法相似度的方法忽略了任务底层的推理流形结构。

Method: CoT-Core框架：(1) 利用LLM为每个问题展开零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹；(2) 将推理路径投影到潜在空间，按内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度对问题聚类；(3) 从各逻辑等价簇中选取代表性核心问题用于评估。整个过程无需训练，无需历史日志。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA数据集上，CoT-Core大幅降低评估计算成本，同时保持高保真的分数估计；进一步刻画了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，发现其有效性受任务复杂度内在门控。

Conclusion: CoT-Core表明利用推理轨迹的逻辑等价性进行核心集选择能有效平衡评估效率与准确性，但该方法的功效并非普适——任务复杂度是决定推理感知剪枝是否有效的关键因素。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [192] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线能否提升LLM在优化建模任务上的性能。通过从Text2Zinc数据集合成500个优化问题并构建Chroma向量库，在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下，NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%，证明语义验证的合成数据结合检索增强可作为微调的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专长和建模语言熟练度。当前LLM在组合优化场景下常生成结构不一致或不完整的优化公式，亟需一种无需昂贵模型重训练的实用方案来改善LLM的优化建模能力。

Method: （1）从Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述出发，利用LLM生成专业人设，合成了500个优化问题（JSON格式），每个问题配有经过验证的Python求解器脚本；（2）将这些问题编码存储于Chroma向量数据库；（3）对每个推理问题检索语义相似的历史问题作为上下文指导；（4）使用LangChain LLM Agent作为推理框架，在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上进行评估，覆盖三个基准测试集（NL4OPT、MAMO Easy、MAMO Complex）。

Result: RAG流水线在三个测试集上均带来显著准确率提升：NL4OPT从40%升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%升至56%。语义验证的合成示例同时改善了求解准确性和公式结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强的组合是微调的有效替代方案，表明领域特定的合成语料库配合RAG可以成为在真实决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具的实用路径，无需昂贵的模型重新训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [193] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于外部记忆的LLM智能体自我改进过程中因评分替代（用LLM评估替代真实标签）而产生的"Echo Gap"失效模式，提出了"误差独立性假设"（EIA）作为纠正分数膨胀的必要条件，并设计了无需标准答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本到SQL基准上达到56.9%的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地通过外部记忆而非更新权重来实现自我改进——每个回合存入外部记忆、评分并检索以指导后续行为。然而在部署环境中没有真实标签，存储的奖励只能是LLM的评估结果，这种替代会导致错误回合被赋予虚高奖励，使智能体反复重用自己最有信心的错误。作者旨在形式化这一失效模式并寻找纠正方案。

Method: 论文将外部记忆存储的评分视为隐式非参数策略的代理奖励，每次检索到的回合构成一个策略改进步骤。作者通过形式化定义"误差独立性假设"（EIA），证明用户信号必须同时跟踪真实值并将自身误差与记忆偏差去相关，这是一条可纠偏的必要条件而非仅仅是对好验证器的描述。论文还证明了评分膨胀不仅在按存储分数排序检索时发生，在仅按相似度检索（部署智能体使用的机制）时同样会累积。最终提出LUCID算法，一种无需标准答案的去膨胀算法。

Result: LUCID算法在BIRD文本到SQL基准上提供了持续且端到端的性能提升：执行准确率达到56.9%，高于Memento风格自评智能体的54.0%（跨种子的平均提升为+2.9个百分点），也高于相同架构的无记忆智能体的52.4%。

Conclusion: 论文展示了自我改进型LLM智能体中记忆评分偏差的严重性（Echo Gap现象），证明了误差独立性假设是纠正分数膨胀的必要条件，并给出了可恢复收益的闭式表达式（由跟踪真实值和误差去相关两个量决定）。LUCID算法在真实基准上验证了去膨胀方法的有效性，为无标签环境下基于记忆的智能体自我改进提供了一条可行的纠偏路径。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [194] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，通过离线重放请求子集并在vLLM下集成GPU功耗遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量作为基准真值；再通过轻量级校准模型JCalib从廉价请求特征预测Shapley份额用于在线服务。实验表明token比例归因与精确Shapley存在显著偏差（静态批量0.440、连续批量0.458），而JCalib可将误差降至0.116和0.177。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM服务提高了吞吐量但使能量核算复杂化：GPU功耗遥测是聚合的，而可持续性报告、费用分摊和工作负载分析需要请求级能量计费。现有基准只提供模型、阶段或token级别的能量数据，缺乏实测的请求级真值，导致实际使用的核算规则与公平分配之间的偏差未知。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：(1) 离线基准平台——在vLLM下回放请求子集，采用可复现协议集成GPU功耗遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量；(2) 轻量校准模型JCalib——从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，用于在线服务时的归因。此外使用采样Shapley将测量参考扩展到更大组规模。

Result: 在16个模型/工作负载组合下，token比例归因与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1偏差为0.440（静态批处理）和0.458（连续批处理），该差距在三种数据中心GPU上复现。JCalib将误差降至0.116（静态）和0.177（连续），优于在线不可用的独立测量基线，同时保持批量能量效率。采样Shapley证实该差距在大组规模下持续存在。

Conclusion: token归因在批量执行下不是边际能量的可靠代理。测量得到的Shapley真值可用于校准低成本请求特征，实现更公平的能量归因。JCalib可作为部署中最准确的可行核算规则。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [195] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种结构化状态空间模型，通过在Mamba中加入基于三节点网络基序的低秩循环路径来增强跨维度通信，同时保持线性时间递归结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba虽然通过选择性状态空间递归实现了线性时间的长序列建模，但其主要采用对角状态转移，限制了状态维度间的显式交互，无法充分捕捉跨维度动态关联。

Method: 提出Motif-Mamba，灵感来源于三节点网络基序的动力学。该方法将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动力学子空间，施加基序引导的交互，再将动力学映射回原始状态空间，从而增强跨维度通信且保持Mamba的线性时间递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准和脑机接口解码等实验中，Motif-Mamba相较Mamba骨干网络均取得一致的改进。

Conclusion: 基序引导的低秩动力学为长序列建模提供了一种有效的结构先验，验证了在选择性状态空间模型中引入结构化低秩交互路径的可行性。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [196] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个端到端JIT编译器，通过捕捉eager执行、统一前后向传播到单一value-semantic dialect，实现跨操作融合、内存层次优化和寄存器级调优。在RTX 3060上，Nova在TF32矩阵乘法上匹配或超过cuBLAS和XLA，模型级吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%，比XLA高4.4%，并将内存占用降低最多29%，使144M参数模型在12GB GPU上训练成功而PyTorch因OOM失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型扩展性能高度依赖高层数学操作到底层物理硬件的映射效率。高级张量框架提供灵活抽象，但其eager执行模式天然缺乏全图可见性和对硬件/内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化物理硬件利用率。需要弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: Nova采用自动化端到端JIT编译方法：(1) 捕捉eager执行并将前向和反向传播统一到单一value-semantic dialect，解锁全图级优化；(2) 利用Analytic Configurator基于算术强度确定性推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降为零；(3) 通过structural hashing运行时直接从计算结构合成细粒度kernel。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，Nova在TF32矩阵乘法上匹配或小幅超过cuBLAS和XLA（大多数形状），保持严格<5e-4相对误差。模型级吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%，比XLA高4.4%（42M参数模型）。内存占用较PyTorch减少最多29%，使144M参数模型在12GB GPU上以17,900 tokens/s成功训练，而PyTorch在同一硬件上遭遇OOM失败。

Conclusion: Nova通过从数学运算结构到物理硬件特性的端到端确定性优化，在不牺牲数值精度的情况下实现了更优的吞吐量和显著的内存效率提升，验证了编译驱动方法在突破eager执行框架硬件利用瓶颈方面的有效性。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [197] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个统一的工具包和交互式Web UI，用于检测RAG、智能体和基于记忆的LLM系统中的上下文幻觉（上下文幻觉指流畅但缺乏证据支持的回复）。它统一了三种检测器范式（表示探测、不确定性估计和评判式验证）以及生成前查询可回答性任务，支持白盒和黑盒环境下的响应级和跨度级检查，并提供实时分析、不支持跨度高亮和并排检测器比较等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强、智能体和记忆接地等LLM系统中存在上下文幻觉问题——模型生成的回复虽然流畅合理，但缺乏所提供证据的支持。现有检测方法各自独立，缺乏统一的框架来整合多种检测范式及生成前的查询可回答性判断，需要一套统一工具和界面来系统性地检测与评估幻觉。

Method: SIRIN统一了三种检测器范式（表示探测、不确定性估计和评判式验证），并将生成前查询可回答性检测整合到同一接口、配置系统和评估流程中。系统支持白盒和黑盒设置下的响应级和跨度级检查，通过Web UI提供上下文-查询-答案三元组的实时分析，支持幻觉分数、不支持跨度高亮及并排检测器比较，并采用轻量级插件设计便于添加新检测器。

Result: SIRIN在幻觉检测、查询可回答性以及作为长时记忆系统内的忠实度门控（faithfulness gate）三个方面进行了演示验证，展示了其在多种LLM系统场景中的适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: SIRIN提供了一个统一的、可扩展的幻觉检测框架，整合了多种检测范式和生成前可回答性评估，并通过交互式Web界面支持实时细粒度检查，其开源代码可供社区使用和进一步扩展。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [198] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言模型（VLM）中语言目标引导会动态重编码视觉表征的两种机制：抽象参考表征（识别目标相关对象）和属性调制（放大目标相关属性），并通过因果干预验证了这两种机制对模型预测的因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM常被用于目标导向的视觉任务（如分类、搜索），但其视觉表征是否会被目标导向语言动态重编码这一问题此前缺乏系统研究。以往工作大多将VLM中的视觉表征视为静态的信息存储，仅由语言表征进行操作和读取，忽视了语言对视觉表征本身的主动调制作用。

Method: 1) 提取对比steering vectors来识别与自然语言提示下目标相关对象的抽象参考表征，并通过因果干预验证其对模型预测的因果作用；2) 考察后期层是否选择性放大视觉表征中目标相关属性（属性调制），并在多种提示下验证该现象；3) 通过因果干预证明属性调制介导VLM的响应分布。

Result: 研究发现参考表征具有抽象性：可泛化到不同对象、不同任务语境，甚至从合成图像泛化到自然图像。同时验证了语言诱导的属性调制现象在多种提示下稳定存在，并通过因果干预证明了该调制对模型响应分布具有因果中介作用。

Conclusion: VLM中的跨模态处理应被理解为动态过程：视觉token并非静态信息仓库，而是会被语言查询动态调制以支持目标导向任务。这一发现支持了对VLM跨模态处理的更动态解释。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [199] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RagTester，一种针对RAG系统的自动化端到端测试方法。它自动生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出，利用LLM作为裁判评估答案质量，并通过覆盖导向的测试生成策略（针对复杂段落、不支持查询和文档覆盖率）来暴露RAG系统中检索与生成组件交互导致的失败。在8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成的24个配置上进行了72,000次测试执行，检测到21,633个失败，比基线多6.6%，在20/24配置中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的可靠性依赖于生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构造策略之间复杂的交互作用，但缺乏系统化的自动化测试方法来评估RAG配置在部署前的表现。现有方法无法有效暴露由检索与生成组件交互引发的失败，需要覆盖导向的端到端测试方案。

Method: RagTester采用四阶段自动化流程：(1)生成检索文档、测试输入及预期输出；(2)执行测试；(3)使用LLM作为裁判评估生成的答案。其测试生成策略针对三类目标：复杂段落理解、不支持查询（无依据问题）以及文档覆盖率标准。

Result: 在8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成的24个兼容配置上，共执行72,000次测试，检测到21,633个失败案例，比基线测试输入生成器多出6.6%的失败检测率，在24个配置中的20个上表现更优。检测到的失败类型包括不准确的检索、无依据的回答、检索上下文利用不完整以及复杂段落理解困难。

Conclusion: 覆盖导向的测试生成策略能够有效暴露RAG系统中检索与生成组件交互导致的失败，支持RAG配置在部署前的评估与选择。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [200] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI
