*Kapil Wanaskar,Gaytri Jena,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FactorJEPA，一种将世界结构作为显式预测原语的联合嵌入预测架构，专门针对拥挤、混乱的全球南方城市环境（DENSEWORLD）设计，并发布了首个该领域的大规模数据集DENSEWORLD-115k（1,000小时、22个城市的视频数据），在拥挤异构、部分可观测场景中显著优于现有JEPA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型主要在低密度、车道结构化的场景中评估，而对人口密集、混乱的全球南方城市环境（软空间边界、极端智能体异构性、持续遮挡、混合交通下的快速社会协商）研究不足。现有JEPA框架在这种异构性和部分可观测性下难以保持密集交互动力学。

Method: FactorJEPA将世界结构作为一等预测原语：不再将未来编码为单一隐向量，而是将布局（layout）、实体（entities）和交互（interactions）分解到分离的子空间中，并通过可见性门控（visibility gate）保留部分可观测的智能体，抑制跨因子的捷径学习。在V-JEPA 2.1的2B和1B骨干网络上进行训练。

Result: FactorJEPA在三个维度上优于现有方法：(i) 未来隐层精度（Future-frame L1）、(ii) 干预敏感预测（Causal L1）、(iii) 对减少视觉证据的鲁棒性（Mask-ratio slope）；同时揭示了一个可复现的运动信息权衡（Motion cosine）。方法排名在2B和1B骨干网络上保持一致，rho=0.895至0.978。

Conclusion: FactorJEPA通过结构化分解世界模型，显著提升了在拥挤异构、部分可观测的全球南方城市环境中的预测能力，并提供了首个大规模基准数据集（DENSEWORLD-115k）和开源模型检查点，为该研究领域奠定了可复现的基础。

Abstract: World models have attracted significant attention for their ability to capture and predict the structure and dynamics of the physical world. In this emerging landscape, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer a particularly compelling direction.
  We study a largely unexplored regime: populous, crowded, and chaotic Global South urban environments, which we call DENSEWORLD. Unlike the lower-density, lane-structured settings that dominate existing evaluations, these scenes exhibit soft spatial boundaries, extreme agent heterogeneity, persistent occlusion, and rapid social negotiation under mixed traffic. We introduce the first large-scale dataset for this regime: 1,000 hours of drive-through, walk-through, and aerial video across 22 cities. Existing JEPA formulations struggle to preserve dense interaction dynamics under heterogeneity and partial observability.
  We introduce FactorJEPA, which makes world structure a first-class predictive primitive. Rather than encoding the future in a monolithic latent, it composes layout, entities, and interactions, using a visibility gate and separated subspaces to preserve partially observed agents and discourage cross-factor shortcuts. FactorJEPA improves (i) future-latent accuracy (Future-frame L1), (ii) intervention-sensitive prediction (Causal L1), and (iii) robustness to reduced visual evidence (Mask-ratio slope), while exposing (iv) a reproducible motion-information trade-off (Motion cosine). Method rankings replicate across 2B and 1B V-JEPA 2.1 backbones, with rho = 0.895 to 0.978.
  We publicly release the DENSEWORLD-115k dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/denseworld-115k) and the surgery-trained FactorJEPA checkpoints (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/factorjepa-outputs/tree/main/outputs/full/vjepa_2_1_vitg_1B/train/m09c_surgery_3stage_DI_diheavy_encoder).

</details>


### [266] [Don't Offer What Can't Be Done: Deterministic Executability Gating for LLM Skill Selection at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01050)
*Ortal Ashkenazi,Vitalii Kloz,Mykhailo Ulianchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Wix客户服务助手Helpmate中部署的三阶段技能选取流水线：语义匹配→确定性可执行性门控→LLM决策。该流水线通过可执行性门控过滤无法在当前账户状态下执行的技能，在生产分析中减少了90.5%的技能描述上下文，并通过反事实实验验证了门控能防止不可执行技能影响模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的LLM代理从大型技能库中选取技能时，仅靠语义相关性不够——一个技能可能在主题上匹配用户消息，但在当前账户状态下无法执行。需要一种不依赖LLM可靠性的确定性机制来排除无法执行的技能候选。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 召回导向的语义匹配器，不依赖账户状态，识别与十个技能领域家族相关的消息；(2) 确定性可执行性门控，基于内部硬停止条件移除候选技能，因门控与技能评估相同的退出谓词，确保被阻挡的候选在相同账户状态下确实无法完成；(3) LLM在剩余候选中决定是否调用。

Result: 在756.6K条用户消息（267.6K次对话）的生产分析中：语义匹配保留23.1%消息；门控移除59.4%的技能-消息对，节省228.8百万技能描述token（占语义匹配后足迹的59.1%）；整体上下文减少90.5%。反事实实验（1,000个风险增强对话）中，全技能暴露时模型在7.8%对话中选择了被生产系统阻挡的技能。

Conclusion: 确定性可执行性门控能有效防止不可执行技能候选影响LLM的模型选择行为，而不仅仅是减小上下文尺寸。该方法不声称对下游工具执行或客户结果有影响。

Abstract: Production LLM agents that select from large skill libraries face a limitation that semantic relevance alone cannot resolve: a skill may match a user's topic yet be impossible to execute in the current account state. We present a deployed three-stage selection pipeline for Helpmate, Wix's customer-care assistant. First, a recall-oriented semantic matcher identifies messages related to a ten-skill domain family without consulting account state. Second, a deterministic executability gate removes candidates whose internal hard-stop conditions hold. Because the gate and the skill evaluate the same exit predicates, every blocked candidate would be unable to complete under the same account state, provided predicate parity is preserved and both checks observe fresh authoritative state. Finally, the LLM decides whether to invoke one of the remaining candidates.
  In a post-launch production analysis of 756.6K user messages across 267.6K conversations, semantic matching retained 174,927 messages (23.1%). Within this matched stream, the gate removed 1,039,462 of 1,749,270 skill-message pairs (59.4%), saving 228.8 million skill-description tokens -- 59.1% of the post-semantic skill-description footprint. Together, semantic matching and executability gating reduced skill-description context by 90.5% relative to exposing all ten skills to every message.
  To test whether this pruning affects model behavior rather than context size alone, we replayed a risk-enriched cohort of 1,000 conversations with all ten skills exposed. The model selected a production-blocked skill in 78 conversations (7.8%). This counterfactual result shows that deterministic gating prevents non-executable candidates from influencing model selection, while not claiming downstream tool execution or customer-outcome effects.

</details>


### [267] [Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01056)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CompressAgent，一个环境验证的智能体控制上下文（ACC）压缩基准测试平台，覆盖九个独立构建的ACC、三个任务族、三个Qwen API模型、六个保留上下文预算和15,525次运行，揭示了压缩率与运行可靠性之间的非线性、方法依赖的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有prompt压缩评估只关注压缩后的文本质量或任务性能，未验证压缩后智能体控制上下文（ACC）在运行时是否仍能可靠地指导工具使用、参数执行和恢复策略。压缩ACC可以降低输入成本和上下文占用，但缺乏评估其可操作可靠性的基准。

Method: 构建CompressAgent基准，包含九个独立构建的ACC、三个任务族、三个固定的Qwen API模型标识、六个保留上下文预算（从75%到10%），共15,525次运行。比较通用改写、基于章节的压缩、义务感知压缩等方法的运行成功率，并进行失败分析归类。

Result: 在75%保留上下文时，通用改写和基于章节的压缩分别达到92.7%和92.4%的成功率，接近93.8%的完整上下文基线；但在50%到35%区间方法表现急剧分化（35%时分别为47.0%、39.0%和19.9%）；在25%至10%预算下可执行协议变得脆弱。可靠性在不同ACC间差异显著，压缩失败主要体现为工具执行和动作解析错误。

Conclusion: ACC压缩不应被视为简单的token缩减问题，而应被重新定义为运行时可靠性问题，必须通过可执行结果来评估。不存在通用的压缩器排名，需要对每个ACC上下文进行个体化资格验证。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents are governed not only by task prompts but also by persistent system-side instructions that specify tools, arguments, policies, execution protocols, and recovery. Compressing these agent control contexts (ACCs) can reduce input cost and context use, yet existing prompt-compression evaluations do not reveal whether the resulting control remains operationally reliable. We introduce CompressAgent, an environment-verified benchmark for ACC compression across nine independently constructed ACCs, three task families, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. We uncover a nonlinear, method-dependent reliability frontier. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression achieve 92.7% and 92.4% success, close to the 93.8% full-context baseline. Between 50% and 35%, methods diverge sharply; at 35%, section-based, obligation-aware, and generic rewriting achieve 47.0%, 39.0%, and 19.9%. At retained-context budgets from 25% to 10%, executable protocols become fragile. Reliability also varies substantially across ACCs, making universal compressor rankings inappropriate and motivating per-context qualification. Failure analysis shows that compression primarily surfaces as tool-execution and action-parsing errors. These findings recast ACC compression from token reduction into a runtime-reliability problem that must be evaluated through executable outcomes.

</details>


### [268] [Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01075)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals (RD-AttnRes)，在Block Attention Residuals的基础上将查询/键和值的深度路由解耦，使注意力匹配和内容检索从残差层级的不同深度读取信息，在120M和343M参数模型上均稳定改善困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Block Attention Residuals使用单一的内容相关深度混合来构造查询、键和值的输入，这耦合了两个功能上不同的决策：查询和键决定注意力在哪里匹配，而值决定检索什么内容。作者质疑匹配和内容检索是否应被强制从相同的深度读取。

Method: RD-AttnRes是一个最小扩展：查询和键共享一条深度路由，而值学习一条独立的深度路由，两者均作用于相同的残差来源。将两条路由完全绑定即恢复父架构，解耦后每层仅增加一个模型宽度向量，且不引入额外的token-to-token注意力操作。评估使用冻结的配对预训练协议，在FineWeb-Edu上对120M和343M参数模型各使用五个匹配种子，训练预算为20亿token。

Result: RD-AttnRes在所有10个匹配比较中均改善了验证负对数似然。120M和343M的平均改善分别为0.0301和0.0247，对应困惑度降低2.97%和2.43%。早期预算对照表明，额外参数数量、重复路由执行或固定值路由都无法复现该改进。路由诊断显示查询-键和值的深度分布持续发散。

Conclusion: 在评估的训练机制内，注意力匹配和内容检索确实受益于对残差层级的不同读取。解耦路由带来的改进不能归因于参数增加或计算开销，表明深度路由的角色解耦是有意义且有效的架构设计原则。

Abstract: Depth-routing residual architectures allow Transformer layers to retrieve earlier representations instead of inheriting only the immediately preceding state. Existing Block Attention Residuals, however, use a single content-dependent depth mixture to construct the inputs to queries, keys, and values. This design couples two functionally different decisions: queries and keys determine where attention matches, whereas values determine what content is retrieved. We therefore ask whether matching and content retrieval should be forced to read from the same depth. We introduce Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals (RD-AttnRes), a minimal extension that shares one depth route between queries and keys while learning an independent value route over the same residual sources. Tying the two routing queries exactly recovers the parent architecture, while decoupling them adds only one model-width vector per layer and introduces no additional token-to-token attention operation. We evaluate RD-AttnRes using a frozen, paired pretraining protocol on FineWeb-Edu with five matched seeds for both 120M- and 343M-parameter models and a 2.0B-token training budget. RD-AttnRes improves validation negative log-likelihood in all 10 matched comparisons. The mean reductions are 0.0301 and 0.0247, corresponding to perplexity reductions of 2.97 percent and 2.43 percent at 120M and 343M parameters, respectively. Early-budget controls indicate that neither the additional parameter count, duplicated routing execution, nor a fixed value route reproduces the improvement. Routing diagnostics further reveal persistent divergence between the query-key and value depth distributions. These results suggest that, within the evaluated training regime, attention matching and content retrieval benefit from distinct reads over the residual hierarchy.

</details>


### [269] [Inter-Residue Geometry Attention for Antibody-Specific Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01092)
*Chuanliu Fan,Nan Yu,Junjie Wu,Guohong Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LF3DRoPE（局部框架3D旋转位置编码），将折叠后残基间的三维位移直接注入旋转注意力机制中，替代传统的一维序列顺序位置编码，用于抗体特异性表位预测。在AsEP基准上取得SOTA MCC性能，并通过消融实验和刚体变换测试验证了其SE(3)不变性及三维几何信息的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体表位预测方法通常依赖PLM嵌入，并通过额外的图、表面或点云编码器注入结构信息，但注意力内部的位罫机制仍主要基于一维序列顺序。然而蛋白质中token偏移的类比不仅包括序列间隔，还包括折叠后残基间的三维位移。这引出一个问题：折叠残基几何能否直接作为注意力本身的位置机制？

Method: 提出LF3DRoPE（Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding），在骨架定义的局部框架中表达残基间的位移，并将其直接注入旋转注意力（rotary attention）中。该方法保持连续方向性几何的同时，确保对全局SE(3)变换的不变性。

Result: 在AsEP基准上，LF3DRoPE在ratio和epitope-group两种数据划分下均取得SOTA MCC。消融实验和刚体变换测试表明，局部三维几何提供了超越序列顺序注意力的信息，同时保持对任意全局坐标系的不变性。突变排序结果进一步表明LF3DRoPE能够捕获抗原特异性结构兼容性。

Conclusion: 折叠后的残基三维几何可以作为注意力中位置编码的机制，LF3DRoPE验证了这一设想，为蛋白质结构建模中的位置编码设计提供了新思路，在抗体表位预测任务上取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Antibody-specific epitope prediction aims to identify which antigen residues are recognized by a given antibody, a task that depends on the three-dimensional complementarity between antibody CDRs and the antigen surface. Existing methods usually leverage PLM embeddings and inject structure through additional graph, surface, or point-cloud encoders, where the positional mechanism inside attention remains largely tied to one-dimensional sequence order. For proteins, the analogue of a token offset is not only sequence separation, but also the three-dimensional displacement between residues after folding. This raises a question, can folded residue geometry serve as the positional mechanism of attention itself? We propose Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding (LF3DRoPE), which expresses inter-residue displacements in backbone-defined local frames and injects them directly into rotary attention. This design preserves continuous directional geometry while ensuring invariance to global $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations. On the AsEP benchmark, LF3DRoPE achieves state-of-the-art $\mathrm{MCC}$ on both ratio and epitope-group splits. Ablations and rigid transformation tests show that local three-dimensional geometry provides information beyond sequence-order attention while preserving invariance to arbitrary global coordinate systems. Mutation ranking results further indicate that LF3DRoPE captures antigen-specific structural compatibility.

</details>


### [270] [Fighting Fire with Fire: On the Feasibility of Protecting Exercises Against AI Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01112)
*Tobias Braun,Jonas Grebe,Louis Rethfeld,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用对抗机器学习技术保护教育评估场景：通过在多模态选择题的视觉组件中添加微妙的对抗扰动，使AI解题模型被定向诱导至特定错误答案，从而形成可检测的统计指纹，用以区分抄袭AI答案的学生与真实作答的学生。研究在现实中黑盒助手假设下，使用Claude、Gemini和ChatGPT三个主流多模态语言模型验证了该范式的可行性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的普及使学生能够将认知任务外包给日益强大的AI助手，产生"能力错觉"，削弱了教育所培养的独立思考能力。需要一种新的防御机制来抵御这种侵蚀性的依赖。

Method: 使用包含视觉组件的多模态多项选择题，通过可访问的替代模型优化对抗扰动，使得这些微小视觉扰动能将AI解题器定向诱导至指定的错误答案。被诱导出的答案模式形成统计指纹，通过统计假设检验对抄袭AI答案的学生与真实作答学生进行区分。实验覆盖Anthropic Claude、Google Gemini和OpenAI ChatGPT三种主流多模态语言模型。

Result: 研究所优化的对抗扰动能够在主流多模态语言模型上诱导出一致的答案模式，这些模式可通过统计假设检验实现原则化的检测。研究同时确立了该范式的可行性及其在现实场景中的局限性。

Conclusion: 利用机器自身的脆弱性来对抗机器辅助推理，是一种有前景但有限度的防御策略：它证明了对抗机器学习可以被重新用于教育保护，但其效果受制于黑盒助手假设和模型行为的不确定性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI enables students to outsource cognitive effort to increasingly capable assistants, creating an illusion of competence while undermining the independent reasoning that education aims to cultivate. We investigate whether adversarial machine learning can be repurposed to protect educational exercises against such corrosive reliance. Our approach uses multimodal multiple-choice questions whose visual components can be protected with subtle visual perturbations that steer AI solvers toward designated incorrect answers. These responses form a statistical fingerprint: students who blindly copy a solver reproduce the induced answer pattern more frequently than genuine students. We study the feasibility of this paradigm under realistic black-box assistant assumptions using three of the most common state-of-the-art multimodal language models: Anthropic's Claude, Google's Gemini, and OpenAI's ChatGPT. By using accessible surrogate models, we optimize adversarial perturbations that induce consistent response patterns. Those patterns enable principled detection through statistical hypothesis testing. These findings establish both the promise and the limitations of fighting machine-assisted reasoning with the vulnerabilities of the machines themselves.

</details>


### [271] [MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01128)
*Yixin Zhang,Zhuohui Yao,Wenchi Cheng,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机辅助应急通信中的信息年龄（AoI）最小化问题，提出MA-HEAD-Net——一种自适应规则引导的多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过联合优化无人机轨迹、用户调度和检查点间隔选择来降低AoI，在动态应急场景中优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后场景中无人机建立应急通信网络至关重要，而时效性救援任务对信息新鲜度有严格要求——基于过时数据的决策可能导致无效控制。现有方法未能充分考虑异构应急服务下突发分组到达、有限块长传输与AoI演化之间的耦合关系。

Method: 采用马尔可夫调制泊松过程建模突发分组到达，利用有限块长理论刻画传输时长、分组完成与AoI演化间的耦合；提出小槽内嵌调度机制与自适应检查点间隔选择以平衡长包延迟容忍传输与紧急短包响应；将轨迹控制、用户调度和检查点间隔选择联合优化建模为多智能体决策问题，开发MA-HEAD-Net框架，将通信域规则先验嵌入门控多头策略，由自适应门控调节规则先验与学习策略对不同子任务的贡献，并在多智能体近端策略优化下联合优化策略与门控组件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，MA-HEAD-Net相比代表性多智能体深度强化学习基线提高了策略形成效率，并且在动态无人机辅助应急通信场景中实现了比基于学习的方法和启发式方法更低的AoI。

Conclusion: MA-HEAD-Net通过在强化学习策略中融合通信域规则先验与自适应门控机制，有效提升了多智能体策略学习效率，并在AoI指标上取得了优于现有方法的表现，为无人机辅助应急通信中信息新鲜度保障提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In post-disaster scenarios, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are critical for establishing emergency communication networks. For time-critical rescue missions, information freshness is crucial because decisions based on outdated data may lead to ineffective control actions. This paper investigates age of information (AoI) minimization for UAV-assisted emergency communications with heterogeneous emergency services. We model bursty packet arrivals using a Markov-modulated Poisson process and adopt finite blocklength theory to capture the coupling among transmission duration, packet completion, and AoI evolution. To balance delay-tolerant long-packet transmission and urgent short-packet response, we propose a mini-slot-embedded scheduling mechanism with adaptive checkpoint-interval selection. We formulate the joint optimization of UAV trajectory control, user scheduling, and checkpoint-interval selection as a multi-agent decision problem, and develop MA-HEAD-Net, an adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. MA-HEAD-Net incorporates communication-domain rule priors into a gated multi-head policy, where adaptive gates regulate the contributions of rule-prior and learned-policy logits for different subtasks. The policy and gating components are jointly optimized under multi-agent proximal policy optimization. Simulation results show that MA-HEAD-Net improves policy-formation efficiency compared with representative multi-agent deep reinforcement learning baselines and achieves lower AoI than both learning-based and heuristic methods in dynamic UAV-assisted emergency communication scenarios.

</details>


### [272] [PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01149)
*Xidong Yang,Xingyi Zhang,Wenhao Li,Wenyan Liu,Junjie Sheng,Yun Hua,Wei Yin,Tao Fang,Chuyun Shen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PATH-Bench，一个评估终身学习智能体路径依赖性的基准。它通过多模型上下文学习估计有向任务关系，构造受控的帮助性/干扰性经验序列，评估前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘。基于发现提出选择性经验使用（SEU）机制，在多数场景下减少遗忘并提升前向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习智能体通过外部学习状态（可检索记忆或可复用技能）不断适应，但现有基准很少考虑经验积累的"路径"如何影响智能体迁移和保留的内容。缺乏对路径依赖性（即先验经验顺序和内容对后续学习影响）的系统化评估。

Method: 构建PATH-Bench基准：(1) 通过多模型上下文学习估计任务间的有向关系；(2) 构造以探测任务为中心的序列，包含受控的帮助性历史和干扰性历史；(3) 反复评估探测任务以测量平均性能、前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘。同时提出SEU（Selective Experience Use）智能体机制，调节路径积累经验对每个新任务的影响，筛选有用条目并过滤潜在干扰。

Result: 在八个代表性智能体上，对单轮代码生成和多轮工具使用任务分别在正主导和负主导历史条件下评估。发现经验的效用取决于经验的表示方式与任务交互结构的联合作用；强迁移不能保证强保留；后期经验可以重塑早期获得的收益。SEU在大多数设置中一致降低遗忘并提升前向迁移。

Conclusion: PATH-Bench提供了受控的评估框架和可操作的指导，为设计更具选择性、更稳健的终身学习智能体提供了方向。经验路径而非单纯的经验积累量才是影响终身智能体性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly adapt through external learning states that store past interactions as retrievable memories or reusable skills, yet existing benchmarks rarely account for how the path of accumulated experience shapes what agents transfer and retain. In this work, we establish PATH-Bench, a benchmark for path-dependent evaluation of lifelong agents. PATH-Bench estimates directed task relationships via multi-model in-context learning, constructs probe-centered sequences with controlled helpful and interfering histories, and repeatedly evaluates probe tasks to measure average performance, forward transfer, backward transfer, and forgetting. We evaluate eight representative agents on single-turn code generation and multi-turn tool-use tasks under positive- and negative-dominant histories. Benchmark results show that experience utility depends jointly on how experience is represented and on the task's interaction structure, that strong transfer does not ensure retention, and that later experience can reshape gains acquired earlier in the learning path. Based on these findings, we propose Selective Experience Use (SEU), an agent harness that regulates how path-accumulated experience influences each new task, admitting helpful items while filtering out potential interference. SEU consistently reduces forgetting while improving forward transfer in the majority of settings. The PATH-Bench provides both a controlled evaluation framework and actionable guidance for designing more selective and robust lifelong agents.

</details>


### [273] [The Graph Language: How Knowledge Graphs Speak to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01175)
*Giuseppe Pirrò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRALAN框架，通过关系令牌（relational tokens）使知识图谱（KG）直接在LLM的语义空间中"说话"，无需修改冻结的LLM即可实现KG与LLM的高效集成，并在问答任务中显著超越现有方法，尤其在复杂多跳推理任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）虽然擅长推理，但缺乏知识图谱（KG）提供的结构化事实支撑。然而，将LLM与KG直接集成存在巨大挑战——两者语义空间不兼容，传统方法要么牺牲LLM的灵活性，要么丢失KG的结构信息。需要一个桥梁来让KG能够在LLM的语义空间中直接表达。

Method: GRALAN由两部分组成：(1) 一个可训练的"语言中介器"（trainable language mediator），生成保留图结构的关系令牌（relational tokens）；(2) 将问答任务重新定义为基于问题聚焦子图（question-focused subgraphs）上的实体分类任务，使任何冻结的LLM都能通过这些结构化令牌理解KG信息。

Result: 实验表明，GRALAN在问答任务上显著优于现有方法，尤其在复杂多跳推理任务上优势明显，验证了其在保持KG结构保真度的同时充分发挥LLM推理能力的效果。

Conclusion: GRALAN建立了一种KG-LLM集成的新范式，通过关系令牌实现图谱结构在LLM语义空间的直接表达，为知识密集型应用提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at reasoning but benefit from grounding provided by Knowledge Graphs (KGs). However, integrating these paradigms is challenging. We introduce GRALAN, which enables KGs to speak directly in the LLM's semantic space through relational tokens that preserve graph structure. GRALAN-s trainable language mediator generates structured tokens for any frozen LLM, creating a foundation for knowledge-intensive applications. We demonstrate its effectiveness in question-answering by re-framing the task as entity classification over question-focused subgraphs. Experiments show that GRALAN significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly on complex multi-hop reasoning tasks, establishing a new paradigm for KG-LLM integration that maintains structural fidelity while leveraging LLMs' reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [274] [Co-evolution of social reward and punishment under institutional interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01183)
*Van An Nguyen,Vuong Khang Huynh,Hoai Thuong Nguyen,Duc Tin Duong,An Nguyen Gia,Tat Kien Nguyen,Huu Loi Bui,My Nguyen Tra,Ho Nam Duong,Ba Thanh Phan,Thanh Vo,Dinh Anh Trung Hoang,Adeela Bashir,Zhao Song,Manh Hong Duong,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究同伴激励与机构激励如何共同影响社会困境中合作、社会福利与执法效率的演化。在包含四种策略（无条件合作者C、背叛者D、社会惩罚者SP、社会奖励者SR）的囚徒困境中，允许分散式同伴激励与集中式机构激励同时作用，机构可奖励或惩罚任意策略子集。在无限混合均匀群体中用四策略复制动力学分析，在结构化群体中用方格晶格上的基于智能体模拟研究空间效应与网络互惠。结果表明：同伴惩罚最有力地促进合作，同伴奖励更有利于社会福利；机构奖励同伴激励策略可显著提升合作与福利，而补贴无条件合作者效果甚微；机构惩罚下直接惩罚背叛者是唯一持续有效的政策，惩罚同伴激励策略会破坏分散激励、降低合作并损害社会福利，表明最大化合作不一定优化整体社会效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解同伴激励（分散式）与机构激励（集中式）如何协同影响社会困境中的合作演化与福利，为制度设计提供原则，弥补两类激励单独研究而忽视其联合效应的空白。

Method: 采用囚徒困境模型，设定四策略（C、D、SP、SR），允许同伴惩罚/奖励与机构奖励/惩罚同时作用于任意策略子集；在无限混合均匀群体中运用四策略复制者动力学进行均衡与演化流分析；在结构化群体中使用方格晶格上的基于智能体模拟研究空间效应与网络互惠；以均衡态、合作水平和社会福利（总群体收益减去机构成本）为评价指标。

Result: 同伴惩罚最有力地促进合作，同伴奖励更有利于社会福利；机构奖励同伴激励策略显著提升合作与福利，而补贴无条件合作者影响甚微；机构惩罚中只有直接惩罚背叛者持续有效，惩罚同伴激励策略会破坏分散激励、降低合作并损害福利，说明最大化合作与福利最大化并不等同。

Conclusion: 为需要平衡合作促进与福利最大化的机构提供设计原则：应鼓励同伴激励（尤其惩罚），补贴同伴激励策略优于补贴合作者，机构惩罚应直接针对背叛者而非惩罚激励策略本身。

Abstract: We investigate how peer and institutional incentives jointly shape the evolution of cooperation, social welfare, and enforcement efficiency in social dilemmas. In a Prisoners Dilemma with four strategies, unconditional cooperators (C), defectors (D), social punishers (SP), and social rewarders (SR), we allow decentralised peer incentives and centralised institutional incentives to act simultaneously, with the institution able to reward or punish any subset of strategies. In infinite well-mixed populations, we analyse the resulting four-strategy replicator dynamics, and in structured populations we use agent-based simulations on square lattices to study spatial effects and network reciprocity. Intervention schemes are evaluated by equilibrium states and evolutionary flow for infinite well-mixed populations, by cooperation levels and social welfare for structured populations, defined as aggregate population payoff net of institutional cost. We find that peer punishment most strongly promotes cooperation, whereas peer reward is more beneficial for social welfare. Institutionally rewarding peer incentive strategies substantially improves both cooperation and welfare, while subsidising unconditional cooperators has little impact. Under institutional punishment, directly penalising defectors is the only consistently effective policy; punishing peer incentive strategies dismantles decentralised incentives, reduces cooperation, and harms social welfare, showing that maximising cooperation does not necessarily optimise overall societal benefit. Our findings provide design principles for institutions seeking to balance cooperation promotion with welfare maximisation.

</details>


### [275] [Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01193)
*Phu Hoa Pham,Duy Minh Dao Sy,Trung Kiet Huynh,Phu Quy Nguyen Lam,Chi Nguyen Tran,Minh Trung Le,Phong Hao Le,Dinh Nam Nguyen,Thien Ky Nguyen Dong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）在多智能体AI开发竞赛中的战略安全行为，强调在解释行为前必须先进行审计验证。研究发现，模型可能具有良好的规则记忆但弱的状态跟踪与期望收益计算能力；不同模型、不同人数竞赛中的行为模式差异显著，且结果具有模型特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发竞赛构成一个多智能体安全困境：公司可以选择缓慢安全地开发，或冒险加速。已有研究往往直接假设LLM的行为反映了其对博弈的"理解"，但一个有效的动作并不能证明智能体真正理解了游戏。因此需要在行为解释之前设置审计门槛（audit gate），系统验证游戏引擎、规则记忆、状态跟踪、收益计算及不同等价任务描述下的稳定性。

Method: 研究采用重复博弈框架模拟2至5名玩家的AI开发竞赛。审计分为多个层次：首先验证游戏引擎的正确性；然后测试模型的规则回忆能力、状态跟踪能力、期望收益计算能力，以及在等价任务描述下的行为稳定性。随后将LLM的动作序列与进化博弈论基准和已发表的人类数据进行比较，并考察不同模型、风险条件、人设、及2至5人竞赛中的行为差异。

Result: 审计发现：强规则回忆可以与弱状态跟踪和期望收益计算共存。提供经过验证的算术支持以及改变响应表示方式，即使游戏规则不变，也会改变后续动作。在七个测试的模型端点中，聚合层面的比率掩盖了动作序列、对对手的响应、对竞赛位置的响应等方面的巨大差异。3至5人竞赛中的行为模式也是模型特异性的，而非增加竞争者的单一效应。

Conclusion: 多智能体AI竞赛模拟在将输出描述为战略性、类人或安全意识之前，必须进行有效性检查和轨迹级分析。作者强调研究结果仅为探索性发现，仅适用于所测试的模型、提示词和解码设置。

Abstract: An AI development race creates a multi-agent safety dilemma. Each company can develop slowly and safely, or move faster while taking a risk that may remove its final reward. We use this repeated game to study strategic safety behaviour among large language model (LLM) agents in races with two to five players. However, a valid action does not show that an agent understands the game. We therefore place an audit gate before behavioural interpretation. We first verify the game engine, then test rule recall, state tracking, payoff calculation, and stability under different but equivalent task descriptions. We then compare LLM action sequences with an evolutionary game-theory benchmark and published human data, and explore differences across models, risk conditions, personas, and two- to five-player races. The audit shows that strong rule recall can coexist with weak state tracking and expected-payoff calculation. Providing verified arithmetic and changing the response representation can also change later actions, even when the game rules stay fixed. Across seven tested model endpoints, aggregate rates hide large differences in action sequences, responses to opponents, and responses to race position. Patterns across the tested three- to five-player races are also model-specific rather than a single effect of adding competitors. These results show why multi-agent AI-race simulations need validity checks and trajectory-level analysis before their outputs are described as strategic, human-like, or safety-aware. Our findings are exploratory and apply only to the tested models, prompts, and decoding settings.

</details>


### [276] [Perspectives on Tsallis Statistics for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01223)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统地梳理了Tsallis统计（q-统计）与现代人工智能之间的交汇点：从数学核心（q-熵、q-指数、q-高斯分布等）出发，综述其在稀疏注意力、强化学习、序列/图神经网络、生成模型、损失设计与优化等领域的应用，并提出深度学习中的重尾权重谱和梯度噪声本质上是非广延（nonextensive）特征的论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsallis统计作为Boltzmann-Gibbs统计力学的推广，近年来已在AI多个子领域（如稀疏注意力、RL、鲁棒概率模型）中反复出现，但缺乏一个统一的、结构化的视角来理解其作用机制与设计模式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用综述与理论分析相结合的方法：(1) 系统回顾q-统计的数学基础（q-熵的极大熵变分基础、q-指数/对数、q-中心极限定理、q-高斯分布、超统计动力学来源）；(2) 按应用领域分类梳理其在软最大推广、强化学习、序列与图模型、生成模型、损失函数与优化中的具体表现；(3) 提炼出统一设计模式——由q控制的稠密/均匀与稀疏/尖峰行为之间的可调插值；(4) 论证深度学习中的重尾现象是q-统计（非广延性）的实证信号；(5) 讨论方法论陷阱、与信息几何及q-指数族的关系及开放方向。

Result: 本文识别出Tsallis统计在AI中作为一种可调节的归纳偏置（inductive bias）反复出现：q参数统一了多种看似不相关的方法（如sparsemax/α-entmax稀疏注意力、可控制探索的RL、重尾概率模型、广义损失函数）。此外，论文通过观察深度学习中的重尾权重谱和梯度噪声统计，将这些经验现象与q-统计的理论框架建立联系。最后提出q应被视为可学习的归纳偏置而非固定超参数。

Conclusion: Tsallis统计为理解现代AI中一系列"稠密-稀疏"插值机制提供了统一的理论框架，且深度学习自身的统计特性（重尾分布）表明其与q-统计存在内在关联。未来应将q作为可学习的模型参数，并进一步探索其与信息几何、q-指数族及未解决的开放问题之间的联系。

Abstract: Tsallis statistics generalizes Boltzmann-Gibbs statistical mechanics through a single real parameter $q$ that controls the weight assigned to rare and frequent events. Originally proposed to describe physical systems with long-range correlations, multifractal geometry, and heavy-tailed fluctuations, the framework has become a recurring ingredient in modern artificial intelligence (AI): it underlies sparse attention mechanisms (\textsc{sparsemax} and $α$-\textsc{entmax}), maximum-entropy reinforcement learning with controllable exploration, robust and heavy-tailed probabilistic models, and a family of generalized loss functions and regularizers. This paper offers a structured perspective on where Tsallis statistics meets AI. We first review the mathematical core: $q$-entropy and its variational (maximum-entropy) foundation, the $q$-exponential and $q$-logarithm, the $q$-central limit theorem, $q$-Gaussian distributions, and their dynamical origin in superstatistics, emphasizing the properties that matter for machine learning. We then survey applications across softmax generalization, reinforcement learning, sequential and graph neural models, generative and probabilistic modeling, loss design, and optimization, extracting the recurring design pattern in each case: a tunable interpolation between dense/uniform and sparse/peaked behavior governed by $q$. We further argue that the heavy-tailed weight spectra and gradient-noise statistics empirically observed in deep networks are themselves nonextensive signatures, placing modern learning dynamics within the scope of $q$-statistics. Finally, we discuss methodological pitfalls, the relationship to information geometry and $q$-exponential families, and open directions, arguing that $q$ should be treated as a learnable inductive bias rather than a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [277] [CT-PrepAgent: Bounded Policy and Controlled Execution for Adaptive CT Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01233)
*Xiaolin Fan,Yue Pei,Yingying Zhang,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-PrepAgent，一个基于LLM的智能体框架，通过有界策略和受控确定性执行实现自适应CT数据准备，能够在异构CT采集和多样化下游任务下自动化决策DICOM序列或预处理配置，并在三个公开分割任务和两个私有数据队列上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构CT采集条件和多样化的下游任务需求使得固定的数据准备流程在不同数据源和任务间难以迁移。现有一方法依赖人工设计或数据集特定的规则，难以在采集条件和分析目标变化时自动适应。虽然LLM智能体在医学工作流自动化中展现了潜力，但其在自适应CT数据准备方面的应用尚待探索。

Method: CT-PrepAgent由三个核心组件构成：(1) 确定性检查模块，构建结构化的数据-任务配置文件；(2) 策略模块，基于该配置决定适用的DICOM序列或预定义预处理配置；(3) 受控执行流程，对决策进行守卫、解析、执行和验证，支持有界恢复和安全的隔离机制。

Result: 在三个公开CT分割任务中，CT-PrepAgent获得了最高的宏平均Dice系数；在两个私有原始DICOM队列中，将验证输出产出率从61.7%提升至70.0%，在共同验证输出上获得了相似的配准指标。受控故障和重放测试验证了有界恢复、安全隔离和策略无关重放的有效性。

Conclusion: CT-PrepAgent证明了LLM智能体在自适应CT数据准备中的可行性，通过有界策略与受控确定性执行的结合，实现了跨数据源和任务的自动化数据准备，兼顾了灵活性与安全性，在产出率和性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous computed tomography (CT) acquisitions and diverse downstream task requirements limit the transferability of fixed data preparation workflows across data sources and tasks. Existing approaches typically rely on manually designed or dataset-specific rules, making it difficult to accommodate changes in acquisition conditions and analytical objectives without manual intervention. Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown promise for automating medical workflows, yet their potential for adaptive CT data preparation remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose CT-PrepAgent, which enables adaptive CT data preparation through a bounded policy and controlled deterministic execution. Deterministic inspection constructs structured data--task profiles, from which a policy decides an eligible DICOM series or predefined preprocessing profile, while the controlled execution flow guards, resolves, executes, and verifies the decision with bounded recovery when enabled and safe quarantine otherwise. Across three public CT segmentation tasks, CT-PrepAgent derived data-task adaptive preprocessing decisions and achieved the highest macro-average Dice. On two private raw-DICOM cohorts, CT-PrepAgent increased verified output yield from 61.7\% to 70.0\% and yielded similar registration metrics on common verified outputs. Controlled fault and replay tests validate bounded recovery, safe quarantine, and policy-free replay under tested fault and drift settings.

</details>


### [278] [Learning What to Remember and What to Internalize in LLM Self-Evolution via Adaptive Memory-Parameter Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01234)
*Tianyun Ji,Zhenya Huang,Jiayu Liu,Zirui Liu,Yu Su,Hongbin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COVE，一个统一的大语言模型智能体自进化框架，通过任务感知路由、阶段感知调度和知识优化，将基于外部记忆/技能的harness式学习与基于模型参数更新的参数式学习相结合，在动态变化环境中实现比单一通道进化策略更鲁棒和高效的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体自进化方法分为两类：harness-based方法将反馈外化到可编辑记忆或技能中以快速适应，parameter-based方法将经验内化到模型参数中以实现更深层次的能力提升。然而，单独使用任一机制都会在灵活性和性能之间产生权衡，缺乏一种能够协调双通道的机制来实现鲁棒的自我进化。

Method: 提出COVE统一框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) 任务感知路由——根据任务类型匹配合适的学习机制；(2) 阶段感知调度——根据不同进化阶段安排学习策略；(3) 知识优化——优化知识表达与存储。该框架将自进化视为匹配任务和知识类型到合适学习机制的协调过程，而非不加区分地积累经验。

Result: 在多个任务类别上的实验表明，COVE优于单一通道的进化策略，在动态变化环境下展现出更鲁棒和更高效的改进效果。

Conclusion: COVE通过协调基于外部机制的灵活学习与基于参数的内化学习，解决了自进化中灵活性与性能的权衡问题，验证了"协调双通道优于单一通道"的核心假设，为复杂动态环境下的LLM智能体自适应提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in dynamic environments where tool interfaces, APIs, and user requirements change after deployment. Existing self-evolution methods mainly follow two paradigms: harness-based approaches, which externalize feedback into editable memories or skills for rapid adaptation, and parameter-based approaches, which internalize experience into model parameters for deeper capability improvement. However, using either mechanism alone creates a trade-off between flexibility and performance. This paper asks how an agent can coordinate both channels to achieve robust self-evolution. We present COVE, a unified agent self-evolution framework that combines harness-based and parameter-based learning through task-aware routing, stage-aware scheduling, and knowledge optimization. Through this design, COVE treats self-evolution not as indiscriminate accumulation of experience, but as a coordinated process that matches tasks and knowledge types to appropriate learning mechanisms. Experiments across multiple task categories show that COVE outperforms single-channel evolution strategies, demonstrating more robust and efficient improvement under changing environments.

</details>


### [279] [Cognitive Demand Steering for Adaptive Meta-Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01319)
*John Scoville,Shengzhuang Chen,Yejin Bang,Stefan Winzeck,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出认知需求引导（CDS），一种无需训练的元推理框架。通过前瞻性残差需求评估（而非回溯性奖励），为LLM推理提供精细化的认知需求信号，并基于认知科学的多维量表设计推理干预策略，在三个前沿LLM和六个推理基准上平均提升准确率21.9%（相对直接调用）和9%（相对标准CoT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元推理框架虽然通过迭代控制循环改善了LLM推理（如回溯、终止、注入推理模式），但存在三个关键局限：依赖回溯性（向后看）的奖励函数、使用粗粒度的搜索动作、需要额外的推理控制器训练且依赖多轮示范监督。这些不足限制了方法的泛化能力和实用性。

Method: CDS是一个无需训练的元推理框架，核心组件包括：（1）基于LLM的前瞻性进度评估器，在每个推理步骤评估到达解决方案所需的残差推理量（而非评价上一步表现）；（2）元控制器根据该前瞻性需求信号选择推理干预，包括通用示例和动作（如定量推理的通用指导）；（3）采用认知科学启发的16维认知量表（如注意力与扫描、学习与抽象、空间物理推理等），用于设计干预策略、刻画初始问题复杂度及残差需求信号，为控制器提供细粒度诊断词汇表。整个框架无需任何训练组件，支持跨模型和任务的零样本迁移。

Result: 在三个前沿LLM和六个推理基准上的平均结果中，CDS相比直接调用提升21.9%准确率，相比标准CoT推理提升9%。最大收益出现在困难的数学和编程任务上。

Conclusion: CDS通过将推理控制从回溯性评估转向前瞻性的残差需求信号，并在认知科学的精细维度上操作，在无需训练、无需适配的情况下实现了显著的推理性能提升，尤其在数学和编程等困难任务上表现突出。该框架消除对训练组件的依赖，展示了更强的跨模型、跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent meta-reasoning frameworks improve LLM reasoning by wrapping chain-of-thought generation in an iterative control loop, allowing more effective backtracking, termination of reasoning loops, and injection of promising reasoning patterns, among other strategy adjustments. Despite promising results, methods often rely on backward-looking reward functions, utilize coarse search actions, or require additional reasoning controller training requiring many-shot supervision. We introduce Cognitive Demand Steering (CDS), a training-free meta-reasoning framework equipped with residual demand assessment: at each step, an LLM-based progress evaluator characterizes the residual reasoning required to arrive at a solution rather than merely evaluating the previous step. This allows a meta-controller to select reasoning interventions comprising both general-purpose exemplars and actions (e.g., general guidance for quantitative reasoning) that directly tackle this forward-looking demand signal. This shift eliminates the need for any trained component while enabling zero-shot transfer across models and tasks with no adaptation. Rather than relying on coarse characterizations, we employ cognitive scales to both design interventions as well as profile initial problem complexity and residual demand signal over 16 dimensions motivated by cognitive science (e.g., attention and scan, learning and abstraction, spatio-physical reasoning), giving the controller a fine-grained vocabulary for diagnosing. Averaged across three frontier LLMs and six reasoning benchmarks, CDS improves accuracy by $21.9\%$ over direct calls and $9\%$ over standard CoT reasoning, with the largest gains on difficult mathematics and coding tasks.

</details>


### [280] [G-ReAct: Graph-Guided Deep Search via Structure-State Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01324)
*Shaoxiong Yang,Mengyuan Zhang,Shaojun Lin,Chao Li,Wei Liu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: G-ReAct是一个面向深度搜索的推理框架，将推理组织为固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化，通过图状态显式追踪搜索进度并指导后续决策，将基于文本历史的探索性搜索转化为图引导的约束推理，支持训练和推理两个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索方法依赖线性顺序推理进行轨迹生成和推断，在长程多跳搜索中难以持续保持中间状态和约束，导致上下文遗忘、搜索漂移和低效探索等问题。

Method: 提出G-ReAct框架，将推理组织为固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化：图状态显式追踪搜索进度并指导后续决策，把探索性搜索转化为图引导的约束推理。框架同时支持训练（生成高质量深度搜索轨迹用于监督微调）和推理（无需额外微调即可提供结构化指导）。

Result: 仅用1.9K生成的轨迹进行微调，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507在BrowseComp-ZH上达到52.6%准确率，在XBench上达到79.0%，优于使用更大数据集训练的同类开源方法（包括基于强化学习的方法）。在推理时应用G-ReAct一致提升了现有强LLM在深度搜索任务上的性能。

Conclusion: G-ReAct通过图状态演化替代线性推理，有效解决了深度搜索中的上下文遗忘和搜索漂移问题，且数据效率极高，仅需极少轨迹即可超越大规模训练的基线方法，并具有良好的推理时迁移能力。

Abstract: Deep search has become a fundamental capability of large language models (LLMs) for solving open-domain complex tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on linear sequential reasoning for both trajectory generation and inference, making it difficult to consistently preserve intermediate states and constraints throughout long-horizon multi-hop search. Consequently, they often suffer from context forgetting, search drift, and inefficient exploration. To address these limitations, we propose $\textbf{G-ReAct}$, a reasoning framework for deep search that organizes reasoning as $\textbf{state evolution over a fixed-topology query graph}$. The evolving graph state explicitly tracks search progress and guides subsequent decisions, transforming exploratory search driven by textual history into graph-guided reasoning under explicit constraints. G-ReAct supports both training and inference: it generates high-quality deep-search trajectories for supervised fine-tuning and provides structured guidance for inference-time search without additional fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate that with only 1.9K generated trajectories for fine-tuning, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 achieves $52.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-ZH and $79.0\%$ on XBench, outperforming comparable open-source methods trained on substantially larger datasets, including RL-enhanced methods. Furthermore, when applied at inference time, G-ReAct consistently improves the performance of existing strong LLMs on deep-search tasks. We will publicly release all code and model weights.

</details>


### [281] [402Pilot: An x402 Decision Layer for Autonomous Agent Micropayments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01341)
*Yin Li,Yanbo He,Boo-Ho Yang,Rav Lawana,Ziyue Li,Wei Zeng,Jing Tang,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出402Pilot——一个位于自主智能体与支付执行之间的、协议无关的买方侧决策层，用于在有限钱包下选择可支付服务提供商。其核心策略PA-DCT（支付感知的折扣上下文汤普森采样）在钱包压力和变化的市场条件下自适应调整购买决策，并在402Pilot-Bench基准（823个任务、5条异构提供商流水线、3种市场机制）上取得最优非预言机性能：保持服务质量的同时仅消耗钱包的39-43%，并在价格冲击下实现最佳PA-gap/T以及最优的均值和最坏情况排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程支付协议（如x402）支持按请求微支付，但并未解决一个关键问题：自主智能体在有限钱包下应如何选择购买哪些可支付服务。这是一个买方侧的决策问题，涉及上下文提供商选择、钱包压力、仅付费反馈和动态市场条件，现有协议无法覆盖这一层面。

Method: (1) 形式上定义代理原生支付决策问题：上下文提供商选择、钱包压力、选择后付费反馈、市场变化；(2) 提出402Pilot，协议无关的买方侧决策层，位于自主智能体与支付执行之间；(3) 实例化PA-DCT策略，一种支付感知的折扣上下文汤普森采样，从付费后反馈中学习并适配钱包压力下的购买决策；(4) 构建402Pilot-Bench冻结回放基准，含823个任务、5条异构提供商流水线和3种市场机制，每个场景30对配对种子。

Result: PA-DCT在固定钱包自适应权衡中优于所有非预言机基线：在保持竞争性服务质量的同时仅花费钱包的39-43%，并能根据市场条件变化重新分配支出。在价格冲击下获得最佳非预言机PA-gap/T，并在9个场景-指标组合（质量、ROI、PA-gap/T）中获得最佳均值和最坏情况排名。学习基线的对比和组件消融进一步验证了策略的有效性与设计合理性。

Conclusion: 可编程支付必须辅以具备学习服务价值和自适应购买决策能力的买方侧决策机制。单纯的支付协议不足以支撑自主智能体在有限钱包下的理性消费，需要决策层在支付执行之上进行上下文感知的提供商选择。

Abstract: Programmable-payment protocols such as x402 enable per-request micropayments, but they do not determine which payable service an autonomous agent should buy under a finite wallet. We formulate this buyer-side problem as agent-native payment decision-making: contextual provider selection under wallet pressure, chosen-only paid feedback, and changing market conditions. We propose 402Pilot, a protocol-agnostic buyer-side decision layer between autonomous agents and payment execution that implements purchasing policies for selecting among payable providers. We instantiate it with PA-DCT, a payment-aware discounted contextual Thompson-sampling policy that adapts purchasing decisions under wallet pressure while learning from post-payment feedback. To evaluate buyer-side payment policies, we introduce 402Pilot-Bench, a frozen-replay benchmark spanning 823 tasks, five heterogeneous provider pipelines, and three market regimes, each evaluated over 30 paired seeds. PA-DCT achieves the strongest fixed-wallet adaptive trade-off among non-oracle policies: it maintains competitive service quality while spending only 39 to 43 percent of the wallet and reallocates spending as market conditions change. It attains the best non-oracle PA-gap/T under the price shock and the best mean and worst-case ranks across the nine scenario-metric combinations of quality, ROI, and PA-gap/T. Comparisons with learning baselines and component ablations further support the effectiveness and design of the proposed decision policy. These results suggest that programmable payment must be complemented by buyer-side decision-making capable of learning service value and adapting purchasing decisions accordingly.

</details>


### [282] [Agentic Stage-One Stellarator Optimization: Autonomous Multi-Objective Search for Finite-Beta Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01344)
*Tingjia Zhang,Hongke Lu,Zhuoran Meng,Runlai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体（agentic）语言模型用于恒星器第一阶段设计优化：一个受约束的LM智能体诊断当前平衡态并选择下一轮局部优化实验，而确定性DESC代码负责执行预设轮廓、通量、对称性、度量评估、求解器有效性和接受判定。在有限预算子集中，通过门口验证的构型从5个输入增至19个输出；中位Boozer QS RMS从2.39×10⁻⁴降至1.07×10⁻⁴，中位最大主曲率从62.56降至33.00 m⁻¹。长程路线实现9.10倍QS降低并修复磁阱和曲率缺陷，同时产生了734条结构化的父子动作-结果记录，证明智能体外环控制可以维持有限β多目标搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星器第一阶段设计需要在高维三维等离子体边界和固定边界MHD平衡族中搜索满足约束、场线拓扑、力平衡、稳定性代理和几何等多重要求的构型。但这一过程缺乏从规范到验证过的有限β平衡的通用构造映射，通常依赖迭代数值优化，其结果强烈依赖初始构型、傅里叶分辨率、目标优先级和局部求解器预算，计算成本高且高度依赖专家，限制了设计吞吐量和一致数据生产。

Method: 提出一种智能体式（agentic）第一阶段优化的概念验证。系统由两个层次组成：(1) 有界语言模型智能体负责诊断当前平衡态并选择下一个局部优化实验；(2) 确定性DESC执行框架负责预设轮廓与通量、对称性、度量评估、求解器有效性和接受判定。该设计将专家决策抽象为LM智能体的策略选择，同时用确定性验证层确保每次优化动作的物理有效性。

Result: 在有限β扩展活动子集中，通过门口验证的构型数量从5个输入增加到19个输出；中位Boozer QS RMS从2.39×10⁻⁴降低到1.07×10⁻⁴；中位最大主曲率从62.56降到33.00 m⁻¹。长程路线实现了9.10倍的QS降低，同时修复了磁阱和曲率缺陷。系统记录了每次尝试的局部动作作为转变证据，产生734条结构化父-动作-结果记录。

Conclusion: 结果表明智能体外环控制能够维持有限β多目标搜索的持续进行，将重复优化转化为可扩展的改进平衡态来源和可复用的决策数据。这为恒星器设计提供了一种减少专家介入、提高吞吐量的新范式，同时保留了物理验证的确定性保障。

Abstract: Stage-one stellarator design searches a high-dimensional family of three-dimensional plasma boundaries and fixed-boundary MHD equilibria for configurations that jointly meet requirements on confinement, field-line topology, force balance, stability proxies, and geometry. These specifications do not provide a general constructive map to a validated finite-beta equilibrium. High-quality targets are commonly developed through iterative numerical optimization whose outcome depends on the initial configuration, active Fourier resolution, objective priorities, and local solver budget. Coordinating this process is computationally costly and expert-intensive, limiting both design throughput and the production of consistently evaluated data. We present a proof of concept for \emph{agentic} stage-one optimization. A bounded language-model agent diagnoses the current equilibrium and selects the next local optimization experiment, while deterministic DESC execution owns prescribed profiles and flux, symmetry, metric evaluation, solver validity, and acceptance. On a common-budget subset from an expanding finite-beta campaign, the number of gate-valid configurations increases from five inputs to nineteen outputs; median Boozer QS RMS decreases from $2.39\times10^{-4}$ to $1.07\times10^{-4}$, and median maximum principal curvature decreases from $62.56$ to $33.00\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. A complementary long route achieves a $9.10\times$ QS reduction while repairing magnetic-well and curvature defects. The system also records every attempted local action as transition evidence, yielding 734 structured parent--action--outcome records in the reported experiments. These results show that agentic outer-loop control can sustain finite-beta, multi-objective search and turn repeated optimization into a scalable source of improved equilibria and reusable decision data.

</details>


### [283] [High-Stakes Decisions with Language Models: Insights from Emergency Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01361)
*Khurram Yamin,Christopher Kelly,Bryan Wilder,Eric Horvitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型在高风险临床决策中的应用置于概率决策框架下理解，指出模型推荐不仅取决于预测能力，还取决于明确的效用函数（如漏诊急诊与不必要升级的成本权衡）。通过消费者分诊系统的结构化评估，发现能力强的语言模型会根据明确的效用调整推荐，表明部署时应将决策目标显式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策（如医疗急诊分诊）不仅需要准确预测，还需要估计替代结果的可能性并权衡不同行动的后果。然而语言模型在做出高风险临床推荐时，通常没有明确指定支配这些决策的效用函数，导致决策目标隐含、评估不完整。

Method: 采用概率性决策分析框架，利用结构化临床病例对消费者分诊系统进行评估，在替代效用函数（赋予漏诊急诊与不必要升级的不同相对成本）下分析治疗推荐，检验语言模型是否根据陈述的效用调整推荐。

Result: 能力较强的语言模型能够响应明确陈述的效用函数并相应调整推荐，表明相同的底层预测可以支持截然不同的决策策略；模型推荐取决于预测性能与显式效用的联合作用。

Conclusion: 语言模型在高风险场景中的有效部署不仅依赖于提升预测准确性，还依赖于明确制定决策目标。应将其理解并评估为概率性决策系统，推荐结果由预测表现和显式效用共同决定。

Abstract: High-stakes decisions under uncertainty, such as medical emergency triage, require more than accurate predictions. They depend on estimating the likelihood of alternative outcomes while explicitly weighing the consequences of different actions, principles that have long formed the foundation of medical diagnosis and decision making. Yet language models are increasingly used for high-stakes clinical recommendations without explicit specification of the utilities governing these decisions. Here we show that emergency triage with language models can be understood within a probabilistic decision framework, providing a case study of a broader decision-analytic paradigm for steering, evaluating, and deploying language models in high-stakes settings. Using clinical vignettes from a structured evaluation of a consumer triage system, we analyze recommendations for treatment under alternative utility functions that specify the relative costs of missed emergencies and unnecessary escalation. We find that capable language models adjust recommendations in response to stated utilities, revealing that the same underlying predictions can support markedly different decision policies. These findings show that effective deployment depends not only on improving predictions but also on making decision objectives explicit. More broadly, they suggest that language models for high-stakes applications should be understood and evaluated as probabilistic decision systems whose recommendations depend jointly on predictive performance and explicit utilities.

</details>


### [284] [CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01369)
*Ziyun Zhang,Yuxin Lin,Eldin Wee Chuan Lim,Xinghao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFTS是一个七角色分工的自动化化工过程建模框架，通过结构化子任务分解、确定性IDAES/Pyomo门控和类型化中间表示，将自然语言请求转化为可执行的化工过程仿真模型。在450例OpenIDAES数据集上，91.5%的案例通过验证与执行合同，单元/流股/连接F1分数分别为0.815/0.791/0.782。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可执行的化工过程模型目前高度依赖人工，工程师需要将模糊的请求转化为包含单元操作、热力学、流股、自由度、初始化、求解修复和优化等多个耦合决策的模型，任何单一错误都可能导致模型无效。现有方法缺乏系统化、可验证的自动化建模流程。

Method: CRAFTS将仿真构建过程分解为七个有界子任务，对应七个有界角色：输入理解与意图恢复、视觉/拓扑/规格三个专业角色（经微调）、以及调试和优化角色。各阶段之间采用确定性IDAES/Pyomo门控，通过类型化中间表示（VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR、SpecIR等）在工程门控通过后才附加兼容的构造器、物性包和求解器。并创建了OpenIDAES-450数据集进行训练与评估。

Result: 在OpenIDAES-450冻结的82例held-out测试集上，CRAFTS完成规定的验证与执行合同的比例为91.5%，单元级F1为0.815、流股级F1为0.791、定向连接F1为0.782。

Conclusion: 角色专业化、类型化中间表示和确定性工程门控能有效实现可靠的自动化过程模型构建，为复杂工程仿真自动化提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Constructing an executable chemical-process model remains manually intensive. Chemical engineers translate underspecified requests into coupled decisions about unit operations, thermodynamics, streams, specifications, degrees of freedom (DoF), initialization, solver repair, and optimization; one error can invalidate the model. CRAFTS mirrors the staged workflow of chemical engineers by decomposing simulation building into bounded subtasks assigned to seven bounded roles, with deterministic IDAES/Pyomo gates between stages. Given a natural-language request, process flowsheet diagram (PFD) evidence, and curated chemical-engineering knowledge, Input Understanding and Intent recover requirements, constraints, and process semantics; visual, topology, and specification specialists translate them into typed simulator contracts; and Debug and Optimization support bounded repair and eligible optimization. Fine-tuning is applied to the three schema-critical visual, topology, and specification roles, while the remaining roles use untuned Qwen. The resulting VisualGraphIR, TopologyIR, SpecIR, BuildPlan, and SolveReport expose unit, port, thermodynamic, numerical, and execution decisions. Compatible constructors, property packages, and runners are attached only after semantic artifacts pass engineering gates. We introduce OpenIDAES-450, a 450-case IDAES process- simulation dataset, and evaluate the complete seven-role LangChain/LangGraph workflow through solve and eligible optimization on its frozen 82-case held-out split. CRAFTS completes the prescribed validation and execution contract for for 91.5% of cases and achieves unit, stream, and directed-connection F1 scores of 0.815, 0.791, and 0.782. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of role specialization, typed intermediate representations, and deterministic engineering gates for reliable automated process-model construction.

</details>


### [285] [CraftAlign: Feature-Grounded Evaluation and Revision Guidance for AI Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01377)
*Yang Yang,Boyun Xu,Shaofeng Liang,Yun Han,Zining Zhong,Songning Lai,Kaishen Yuan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CraftAlign是一个将AI生成的故事与人类叙事技法对齐的框架，通过预测304个写作特征并使用类别条件能量模型评分，在推理时施加结构化扰动并向Human写作模式靠拢，最终将改动转化为自然语言指导供独立编辑器重写全文，在判别和修订任务上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的故事虽流畅完整，但常因陈词滥调、过度解释、线性因果推进和模板化结尾而带有明显的"AI味"。现有检测与评估方法停留在来源标签或整体评分层面，修订方法则多针对预定义问题做局部编辑，难以支持多种合理的修订策略，也无法指导故事层面的信息释放、因果组织和结尾处理。

Method: CraftAlign包含两个学习模块和一个推理时引导管线：(1)基于Qwen3.5-9B的特征估计器，预测覆盖风格与叙事维度的304个显式写作特征；(2)类别条件能量模型，对特征配置按照Human/AI写作模式打分，并在有原始写作提示时以提示为条件。推理时，CraftAlign应用符合模式的（schema-valid）结构化扰动，选择使特征配置向Human写作模式移动的改变，并将其转换为自然语言指导，交给独立的编辑器重写完整故事。

Result: 实验表明，CraftAlign能够准确区分Human和AI写作模式；其生成的修订指导在多个编辑器上及人工评估中均优于现有修订基线。

Conclusion: CraftAlign通过在细粒度写作特征层面将AI故事与人类叙事技法对齐，不仅实现了更好的Human/AI判别，还提供了可支持整体故事层面、多种策略并存的修订指导，为AI故事写作的质量提升提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate fluent and complete stories, yet many outputs still feel formulaic and unnatural because of cliches, over-explanation, linear causal progression, and stereotyped endings, an immediately recognizable AI flavor. Existing detection and evaluation methods often stop at source labels or holistic scores, while revision methods typically target predefined issues through localized edits, limiting their ability to support multiple plausible revision strategies or guide story-wide changes in information release, causal organization, and ending treatment. We introduce CraftAlign, a framework that aligns AI stories with the craft of human storytelling by both assessing Human/AI writing patterns and providing revision guidance. CraftAlign comprises two learned modules and an inference-time guidance pipeline. A feature estimator built on Qwen3.5-9B predicts 304 explicit writing features spanning style and narrative. A class-conditional energy model scores the resulting feature configuration against Human and AI writing patterns, conditioning on the original writing prompt when available. At inference time, CraftAlign applies schema-valid structured perturbations, selects changes that move the feature configuration toward the Human writing pattern, and converts them into natural-language guidance for a separate editor to rewrite the full story. Experiments show that CraftAlign accurately distinguishes Human and AI writing patterns and that its guidance outperforms revision baselines across editors and in a human study.

</details>


### [286] [KoVRE: Training an Efficient Embedding Model for Korean Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01389)
*Yongbin Choi,Gyuho Shim,Youngjoon Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了KoVRE，一个针对韩语视觉文档检索的单向量嵌入模型，通过708,729个韩英查询-页面对的训练、正样本感知硬负例挖掘和重排序器知识蒸馏等策略，在2B参数规模下超越了8B单向量基线和强多向量基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索（VDR）模型和训练资源以英语为中心，且高性能系统依赖大规模骨干网络或存储密集的多向量表示，缺乏针对韩语的高效VDR解决方案。

Method: KoVRE作为单向量检索器，使用708,729个韩语和英语查询-页面对进行训练，采用正样本感知硬负例挖掘策略，并对训练数据组成、硬负例处理及基于重排序器的知识蒸馏进行了受控实验分析。

Result: 在韩语视觉文档检索基准上，2B参数的KoVRE较基础骨干模型有显著提升，超越了8B单向量对应模型和强多向量基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，针对性的双语监督和精心设计的训练策略可以在无需扩大骨干网络或使用多向量表示的情况下，构建跨多种文档领域的高效韩语VDR模型。

Abstract: Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) directly matches text queries against document images, preserving visual and structural information that may be lost during text extraction. However, existing VDR models and training resources remain predominantly English-centric, while many high-performing systems rely on massive backbones or storage-intensive multi-vector representations. To address these limitations, we introduce KoVRE: Korean Visual Document Retrieval Embedding, a single-vector retriever for Korean visual documents, alongside a comprehensive training recipe. We train the model on 708,729 Korean and English query-page pairs using positive-aware hard-negative mining and conduct controlled analyses of training-data composition, hard-negative treatment, and reranker-based knowledge distillation. Across Korean visual document retrieval benchmarks, our 2B model substantially improves over the base backbone model, outperforming both its 8B single-vector counterpart and a strong multi-vector baseline. These results demonstrate that targeted bilingual supervision and our carefully designed training strategies can produce a highly effective Korean VDR model across diverse document domains, without requiring a scaled-up backbone or multi-vector representations.

</details>


### [287] [Reusing Rollouts under Policy Lag: Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01418)
*Wenhao Zhang,Yibo Xie,Rui Wang,Jiahua Yang,Lei Jiang,Zibo Yang,Yawei Wang,Jiali Xu,jasperawang,Haoyang Long,Huan Xiong,alantzhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究前缀归一化策略优化（PNPO），用沿因果前缀的似然比几何均值替代累积重要性比率，以压缩离策略校正的log权重尺度。在长上下文数学推理实验中，四轮策略更新场景下PNPO的Avg@32峰值均值达50.24，比GSPO高3.00个百分点；在匹配2400次更新预算下，四轮PNPO用150个rollout批次达到49.66，接近单轮GSPO用600批次达到的49.56。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归rollout生成在LLM强化学习中计算成本高昂，重用rollout批次进行多次学习者更新可摊销成本，但后续更新会因策略偏离而越来越off-policy。精确的离策略校正需要累积重要性比率，但其乘积形式会导致动态范围过大，难以实际使用。

Method: 提出前缀归一化策略优化（PNPO），用每个因果前缀上似然比的几何均值替代累积重要性比率，保留每个位置对因果前缀的依赖，同时压缩log权重的尺度。在受控长上下文数学推理实验中，通过使用每批次1轮或4轮策略更新来诱导两种off-policy状态，对比PNPO与GSPO的性能。

Result: 单轮更新下PNPO未一致优于GSPO；四轮更新下PNPO在每个基准上取得最高Avg@32，三个独立选定的基准峰值均值为50.24，比GSPO高3.00个百分点。在2400次更新预算下，四轮PNPO用150个batch达到最终宏Avg@32 49.66，与单轮GSPO用600个batch达到的49.56相当。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，随着训练进一步走向off-policy，PNPO相比GSPO可能具有优势，在多次策略更新复用rollout时能够用更少的rollout批次达到相近的最终性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive rollout generation is a major computational cost in reinforcement learning for large language models. Reusing each rollout batch for additional learner updates amortizes this cost, but later updates become increasingly off-policy as the learner departs from the behavior policy. At a token position, exact off-policy correction must account for both the current action and the probability of reaching its prefix. The cumulative importance ratio provides this correction, but its product form can produce an unwieldy dynamic range. We study Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization (PNPO), which replaces the cumulative ratio with the geometric mean of likelihood ratios along each causal prefix, preserving causal-prefix dependence at each position while compressing the log-weight scale. In controlled long-context mathematical reasoning experiments, we induce two off-policy regimes by using one or four policy-update epochs per rollout batch. PNPO does not consistently outperform GSPO with one epoch. With four epochs, it attains the highest observed Avg@32 on each benchmark; the unweighted mean of the three independently selected benchmark peaks is 50.24, 3.00 percentage points above GSPO. Under a matched 2,400-update budget, four-epoch PNPO reaches a final macro Avg@32 of 49.66 after 150 rollout batches, comparable to the 49.56 reached after 600 batches with one epoch. These results provide preliminary evidence that PNPO can be advantageous as training moves further off-policy.

</details>


### [288] [Scoring Rules! Statistical and Strategic Alignment for Text Evaluation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01423)
*Shengwei Xu,Yuxuan Lu,Yifan Wu,Jason Hartline,Grant Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参考型文本评估指标需要同时满足统计对齐（与人类评分相关）和策略对齐（抵御无任务相关信息的扰动），并构建了统一设计框架来分析和改进基于互信息的评估指标，发现LLM-as-a-Judge虽然相关性高但易被操纵，而互信息类指标在鲁棒性上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估指标正越来越多地被用作优化目标，仅凭与人类评分的相关性已不足以评判其可靠性——智能体可能策略性地操纵评分指标。因此需要补充"策略对齐"这一维度来全面衡量指标的可靠性。

Method: 提出三项测试原则：人类评分相关性、退化敏感性（惩罚低努力信息丢失）、操纵鲁棒性（抵御战略性分数膨胀）。在此基础上构建统一的互信息度量设计框架，将现有及新度量分解为四个选择维度：信息度量、估计方法、文本表示和预测机制。在同行评审、摘要生成和问答三个任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现强人类评分相关性与策略对齐并不等价：LLM-as-a-Judge相关性高但易受操纵；互信息类指标在操纵鲁棒性上显著更优。该框架还发现了在实验中整体鲁棒性最强、同时保持竞争力的人类评分相关性的新指标。

Conclusion: 评估指标的设计不能只关注与人类评分的相关性，还需考虑策略性操纵的防御能力。互信息统一框架为开发更可靠、更抗操纵的参考型评估指标提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reference-based text evaluation metrics, which are widely used to assess natural language generation systems, score a candidate response by comparing it with a reference response. The reliability of an evaluation metric is usually judged by its statistical correlation with human ratings. However, as these metrics are increasingly used as optimization objectives, correlation alone is no longer sufficient: agents may strategically game the evaluation metric. We study this issue through two complementary notions of alignment. A metric is statistically aligned if it correlates with human ratings and strategically aligned if it resists perturbations that do not add task-relevant information. We make two contributions. First, we propose test principles for reference-based metrics consisting of human-rating correlation, degradation sensitivity, and manipulation robustness. These principles evaluate whether a metric agrees with human judgments, penalizes low-effort information loss, and resists strategic score inflation. Second, we develop a unified design framework for mutual-information-based metrics that decomposes existing and new metrics into four choices: information measure, estimation method, text representation, and prediction mechanism. Across peer review, summarization, and question answering, we find that strong human-rating correlation does not imply strategic alignment: LLM-as-a-Judge achieves high correlation but is susceptible to manipulation. In contrast, mutual-information-based metrics substantially improve manipulation robustness. Our framework also uncovers a new metric that achieves the strongest overall robustness in our experiments while remaining competitive on human-rating correlation.

</details>


### [289] [MRAFnd: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Zero-Shot Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01430)
*Lehan Zhang,Yinlei Cheng,Shiqi Hu Yiheng Zhou,Shangxi Li,Naidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MRAFnd，一个多模态检索增强的零样本虚假新闻检测框架。该框架通过多模态相似度检索构建相关语料，采用双向证据推理提取关键模式，并利用多智能体协作辩论达成稳健结论，在三个基准数据集上显著超越现有方法，在Weibo-21上准确率提升达2.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本虚假新闻检测方法孤立地评估新闻内容，无法识别过去事件中重复使用的虚假信息策略，且缺乏跨模态细粒度不一致的推理能力。作者旨在解决零样本场景下，尤其是面对新型事件时，精准检测多模态虚假新闻的难题。

Method: MRAFnd框架包含三个核心阶段：(1) 多模态相似性新闻检索——从未标注参考数据库中检索上下文相关的相似文章；(2) 双向证据推理——模拟分析师团队从检索证据中进行双方向分析并提取关键模式；(3) 多智能体协作辩论——由分析师和仲裁者智能体进行结构化讨论，最终达成确定且稳健的结论。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的综合实验表明，MRAFnd显著优于最先进基线方法，在具有挑战性的Weibo-21数据集上准确率提升高达2.35%。

Conclusion: 通过模拟分析师协作团队的推理方式，结合检索增强与多智能体辩论机制，MRAFnd有效弥补了传统零样本检测方法在跨模态推理和历史策略识别方面的不足，为多模态虚假新闻检测提供了更可靠的新范式。

Abstract: The rapid dissemination of multimodal content has intensified the spread of fabricated news, presenting a substantial threat to social integrity. A formidable challenge for current detection systems is identifying misinformation related to novel events in zero-shot scenarios. Prevailing zero-shot methods typically assess news items in isolation via semantic matching, a strategy that fails to recognize the recycled disinformation tactics from past campaigns and lacks the sophisticated reasoning needed to identify subtle, cross-modal discrepancies. To surmount these deficiencies, we introduce \textbf{MRAFnd}, a novel \underline{\textbf{M}}ultimodal \underline{\textbf{R}}etrieval-\underline{\textbf{A}}ugmented Framework for Zero-Shot \underline{\textbf{F}}ake \underline{\textbf{N}}ews \underline{\textbf{D}}etection. MRAFnd emulates a collaborative team of analysts to verify news veracity. The framework initiates with \textbf{Multimodal Similarity-based News Retrieval} to assemble a corpus of contextually analogous articles from an unlabeled reference database. Subsequently, during the \textbf{Bifurcated Evidential Reasoning} stage, agents perform a dual-directional analysis to extract critical patterns from the retrieved evidence. Finally, a \textbf{Multi-Agent Collaborative Debate}, involving Analyst and Arbiter agents, engages in a structured discourse to arrive at a definitive and robust conclusion. Comprehensive experiments on three benchmark datasets reveal that MRAFnd markedly surpasses state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an accuracy gain of up to 2.35\% on the demanding Weibo-21 dataset.

</details>


### [290] [PolymerGPT: Multi-property Optimization with a Decoder-Based GPT Model for Generative Polymer Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01431)
*Charlie Pyle,Adarsh Gadari,C. Adrian Figg,Zhenquan Jia,Yaohang Li,Chunjiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PolymerGPT，一种基于解码器GPT架构的聚合物逆向生成模型，通过学习条件前缀同时控制多达37种聚合物物理性质，并支持支架条件约束，实现多种目标性质的同时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物性质预测已受到广泛关注，但面向目标性质的逆向生成设计方法有限。现有方法仅支持单一性质的优化，而准确的宏观材料行为预测需要同时控制多个物理性质，因此亟需一种支持多性质联合控制的生成框架。

Method: 提出PolymerGPT模型，采用解码器架构的GPT模型，通过学习的条件前缀（conditioning prefixes）将多达37种常用聚合物物理性质嵌入生成过程，同时支持支架（scaffold）条件约束来指定预测结构的期望骨架。

Result: 实验结果表明，PolymerGPT在无条件生成和条件生成中均取得优异性能，保持了较高的有效性、唯一性和新颖性。在对五种关键性质进行条件约束时，生成结构的预测值与所有目标性质均能紧密匹配，实现多目标的同时满足。

Conclusion: PolymerGPT提供了一个变革性的框架，能够直接优化大量聚合物性质，克服了现有方法仅支持单性质优化的局限性，为多性质协同控制的聚合物逆向设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Polymer property prediction and inverse generative design targeting desired properties are two crucial tasks in machine learning-assisted polymer design. While the former has received considerable attention, there have been limited methods developed for the latter. Existing methods focus on single-property optimization in the generative process, whereas accurate prediction of macroscopic material behavior requires simultaneous control of multiple physical properties. In this paper, we provide a transformative framework for direct optimization of a large collection of polymer properties. We propose PolymerGPT, a decoder-based GPT model that incorporates up to 37 commonly used polymer properties into the generative process via learned conditioning prefixes. It also supports a scaffold condition that specifies a desired scaffold for predicted structures. Our experimental results demonstrate that PolymerGPT achieves exceptional performance for unconditional and conditional generation while maintaining high validity, uniqueness, and novelty. Conditioning on five key properties yields generated structures whose predicted values closely match all target properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [291] [A New Theory of Value for Post-AGI Economics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01432)
*Keyun Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"繁荣价值理论"(FVT)，作为后AGI时代经济学的基础框架。该理论将价值定义为系统、制度、资产或干预措施对个人和社区在生态与社会约束下持久繁荣能力所做的反事实的、分配敏感的贡献，主张用多维度的繁荣指标取代传统的市场定价、劳动投入和利润作为最终进步衡量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用人工智能(AGI)可能削弱劳动、专业知识、信息和生产能力等传统经济价值理论所依赖的稀缺性。当认知工作被广泛自动化后，市场价格、劳动投入、显示性偏好、利润和总产出将与人和社会福祉严重脱节，现有经济学理论无法充分衡量和引导社会进步。

Method: 论文综合了AI经济学、福利与能力理论、自动化研究、数字估值和生态经济学，构建了FVT框架：定义价值为反事实且分配敏感的贡献；提出从稀缺到受治理的富足、从交易到转化、从零和对抗到正和与无限博弈的三大范式转变；并基于繁荣指标和繁荣回报率(RoF)提出适用于企业、政府、工作转型、AI治理和国民核算的分层架构。

Result: 论文确立了FVT的概念框架，将社会价值视为多维、维护能动性、可再生的，并在关键阈值上具有不可补偿性。它区分了价值创造与价值攫取，保留价格、利润、生产率和GDP作为部分信号而非最终衡量标准，并提出集体意识扩展作为维护能动性的再生价值形式。

Conclusion: 后AGI时代经济学的核心问题不是如何最大化产出，而是如何将丰富的智能转化为持久、公平分配的人类、社会和行星层面的繁荣。这要求经济学从以产出最大化为目标转向以繁荣为最终导向的范式转型。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) may weaken scarcities in labour, expertise, information, and productive capability that underpin established theories of economic value. If cognitive work becomes widely automatable, market price, labour input, revealed preference, profit, and gross output may diverge sharply from human and societal benefit. This paper develops Flourishing Value Theory (FVT) as a foundation for post-AGI economics. FVT defines value as the counterfactual, distribution-sensitive contribution of a system, institution, asset, or intervention to the durable capabilities of persons and communities to flourish within social and planetary constraints. It treats societal value as multidimensional, agency-preserving, regenerative, and non-compensatory at critical thresholds. Drawing on the economics of AI, welfare and capability theory, automation, digital valuation, and ecological economics, the paper distinguishes value creation from value capture and retains price, profit, productivity, and GDP as partial signals rather than final measures of progress. It develops the shifts from scarcity to governed abundance, transaction to transformation, and zero-sum rivalry to positive-sum and infinite-game dynamics, with collective expansion of consciousness as an agency-preserving form of regenerative value. Building on Flourishing Metrics and Return on Flourishing (RoF), it proposes a layered architecture for firms, governments, work transitions, AI governance, and national accounting. The central post-AGI economic problem is not how to maximise output, but how to convert abundant intelligence into durable, fairly distributed human, societal, and planetary flourishing.

</details>


### [292] [Beyond Routing Saturation: A Long-Horizon Class-Incremental Perspective on Expert Routing in Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01437)
*Huiyu Yi,Yongqi Xu,Bogang Zhang,Dunwei Tu,Xu Zhiming,Zhen-Hao Xie,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLEX基准和渐进式LoRA路由的软任务-类多模态类增量学习(MCIL)框架，揭示并解决多模态持续指令微调中专家路由的任务识别挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCIT方法依赖任务特定LoRA专家和路由机制，但路由的任务识别问题被广泛使用的基准掩盖——文本指纹泄漏任务身份、任务序列短且专家数量少，使得路由近似饱和，长时程路由问题未被充分暴露。

Method: 构建FLEX基准（34任务，弱化文本指纹、统一模板、增大专家池）；将渐进式LoRA路由形式化为软任务-类MCIL，每个任务定义增量路由类，完整分数分布提供LoRA混合权重，硬路由为其离散特例；实例化PureLoRA基线并适配四种CIL方法到四种MCIT框架。

Result: 即插即用路由器在严格LoRA匹配上提升最多16.3个百分点，整体MacroScore提升最多4.6点。

Conclusion: FLEX暴露了扩展任务识别挑战，MCIL形式化为将CIL方法迁移到专家路由提供原则性接口，可有效改进多模态持续指令调优中的路由性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables multimodal large language models to acquire new tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned capabilities. Many recent methods maintain task-specific LoRA experts and route each input to one or more experts at inference. Yet the task-identification problem underlying expert routing remains under-explored. We show that routing is nearly saturated on widely used MCIT benchmarks. Textual fingerprints that leak task identity and short 4--10-task sequences with few competing experts jointly obscure the long-horizon routing problem. To expose this challenge, we introduce FLEX (Fingerprint-reduced Long-horizon Expert eXamination), a 34-task long-horizon MCIT benchmark with weakened textual fingerprints. FLEX groups tasks with similar instruction and answer formats but diverse visual and knowledge domains, normalizes their outer templates, and evaluates routing over a substantially larger expert pool. Crucially, we formulate progressive-LoRA routing as soft task-as-class Multimodal Class-Incremental Learning (MCIL): each task defines an incremental routing class, whose complete score distribution supplies the LoRA mixture weights, with hard routing as a discrete special case. FLEX exposes this expanding task-identification challenge, while the MCIL formulation provides a principled interface for transferring CIL methods to expert routing. We instantiate PureLoRA as a controlled baseline and adapt four CIL methods to four MCIT frameworks without modifying their LoRA experts or generation pipelines. Our plug-in routers improve strict LoRA matching by up to 16.3 percentage points and overall MacroScore by up to 4.6 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/RINC-CL/FLEX

</details>


### [293] [Loud or Silent? A Reusable Framework for Per-Modality Failure Analysis in Multimodal Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01462)
*Quang Bui,Shlok Jaiswal,Samuel Paik-Heintz,Kevin Zhou,Kaushik Madapati,Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn,Noah Dane Hebdon,Dimitrios Proios,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Kacper Dobek,Boya Zhang,Aly Dhedhi,Ahram Han,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Rahul Gorijavolu,Jacques Kpodonu,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的模态失效分析框架，用于回答多模态临床模型在部署中缺失某个模态时"哪个模态是错误主因"以及"模型是响亮失败还是静默失败"两个问题。该框架通过掩码感知探针、逐示例失败分类、逐模态互补性矩阵和响/静失败曲线，在模拟数据上验证可恢复注入的真实结构，并在EchoJEPA与HuBERT-ECG嵌入上应用于LVEF与HFrEF二分类任务，发现丢弃超声心动图接近使错误翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床模型通常在所有模态齐备的条件下评估，但实际部署中模态常缺失（如超声心动图在ECG常规场景下不可用）。现有评估只关注准确率下降幅度，无法回答"哪个模态导致失效"和"失效是能被监测的还是静默的"这两个部署关键问题，且这类评估应与被频繁替换的模型本身解耦、可复用。

Method: 提出PRIMED-AI框架：给定N个模态嵌入、任意掩码感知探针和标签，输出(1)逐示例失败分类法；(2)逐模态互补性矩阵，将误差归因到各模态；(3)响vs静失败丢失曲线，利用部署可观测信号区分可监测失败与远离决策边界的无标记静默失败。通过注入已知真实结构的合成数据验证归因恢复能力，并在冻结的EchoJEPA与HuBERT-ECG嵌入上，于MIMIC-IV配对队列中做LVEF回归与EF≤40% HFrEF分类验证。

Result: 在多种随机种子下，框架准确恢复了注入的模态主导性和互补子集，报告的逐模态响/静失败率准确，并能扩展到三模态互补性矩阵，证明其对逐示例归因的恢复能力。在实际数据中，保留测试集（n=245）上丢弃超声心动图后错误率接近翻倍；且超声心动图与ECG的重叠样本极少，这一配对可用性瓶颈本身即为心脏基础模型的部署发现。

Conclusion: 模态失效的逐示例归因与响/静失败刻画可以模型无关地、复用式地被系统化评估；现有心脏多模态基础模型在单一模态部署时存在显著但部分可监测的准确率损失，且模态间临床重叠的稀缺是实际部署中必须先面对的约束。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical models are usually judged on accuracy with every modality present, but deployment removes modalities; an echocardiogram is often unavailable where an ECG is routine. Two questions then matter beyond the size of the accuracy loss: which modality was responsible, and whether the model fails loudly or silently once that modality is dropped. The distinction is per-example and modality-level, and is separate from post-hoc feature attribution (e.g. SHAP). Models are replaced often; the evaluation that answers these questions is reused. We present a model-agnostic modality-failure framework: given N modality embeddings, any mask-aware probe, and labels, it returns a per-example failure taxonomy, a per-modality complementarity matrix that attributes error to modalities, and a loud-vs-silent dropout profile separating monitorable failures from those that pass unflagged far from the decision boundary, using only deployment-observable signals. We release it as a small, unit-tested harness and validate it against planted ground truth. Across seeds it recovers that planted modality dominance and complementary subset, reports per-modality loud-vs-silent rates, and scales to a three-modality complementarity matrix; because the planted structure is known by construction, this validates recovery of per-example attribution rather than clinical performance. We then instantiate the framework on frozen EchoJEPA and HuBERT-ECG embeddings for LVEF and the EF <= 40% HFrEF gate over a paired MIMIC-IV cohort, where on the held-out test split (n = 245) dropping echo nearly doubles error. The narrow echo-to-ECG overlap that bounds cohort size is itself a deployment finding for cardiac foundation models. All of our work can be found at https://github.com/criticaldata/PRIMED-AI.

</details>


### [294] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出局部化多智能体辩论（LMAD）协议，将智能体推理轨迹表示为类型化节点，定位最早冲突并仅针对局部片段进行辩论，同时通过有守卫的扩展（Guarded Resolution）维护共享已承诺状态，避免重开已接受步骤。在四个多跳问答基准和十个骨干模型上，单一固定配置即取得最高平均评判准确率，超越最强传统基线最多7.20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体辩论的方法在分歧仅涉及少量中间论断时，仍会交换完整的推理轨迹，导致冗余的通信和推理成本，且可能引发争论范围的无谓扩大，影响效率和收敛质量。

Method: 将每个智能体的推理轨迹表示为带类型的节点序列；在辩论过程中定位最早发生冲突的节点，将辩论限制在对应的局部片段范围内；通过有守卫的解决机制扩展共享的已承诺状态，使后续冲突可以在不重新打开已接受步骤的前提下被处理。

Result: 使用单一固定LMAD配置，在四个多跳问答基准上、覆盖四个模型家族的十个骨干模型，该方法在所有十个骨干上取得了最高的宏平均评判准确率，较最强传统基线的优势最高达7.20个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅针对局部冲突片段进行辩论的机制在保持较低通信成本的同时显著提升了多跳问答的推理准确率，且所提配置在不同骨干模型和基准上具有Robust的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [295] [Computing with Agentic Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01464)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了AI增强计算的随机预言机模型，引入了自主代理预言机（agentic oracle），并建立了在随机预言机图灵机（SOTM）中分析此类预言机token成本的框架，区分外部可见的编排token成本和内部不可见的代理token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机预言机模型假设预言机以固定分布响应同一查询，无法刻画AI代理自主追求目标、访问环境资源、以及内部操作产生额外token成本等现实行为。这些能力在查询-响应接口之外影响响应分布和token成本，需要新的分析框架。

Method: 将随机预言机图灵机（SOTM）模型扩展以支持代理预言机，区分编排token成本（调用者可见）和代理token成本（内部操作不可见）；研究代理预言机可保留中间状态时相对固定随机预言机的token成本优势；引入目标丢失风险（goal-loss risk）概念，分析内部调度顺序如何降低不可逆操作暴露，提出目标丢失避免判据，推导进展-重试-目标丢失公式，建立目标深度下界、零目标丢失概率下的token复杂度特征，并证明目标丢失风险对涉及环境更新任务的可达质量施加上界。

Result: 证明使用可保留中间状态的代理预言机的SOTM，在解决相同任务且达到相同质量水平时，相比使用固定随机预言机的SOTM具有token成本优势（无论是否有环境访问）；建立了目标丢失避免判据和进展-重试-目标丢失公式；确立了目标深度下界和零目标丢失概率下的token复杂度；发现目标丢失风险对涉及环境更新任务的质量存在上界约束。

Conclusion: 代理预言机赋予了AI增强计算模型更强的表达力和token效率，但引入了目标丢失风险。该框架为理解代理化AI系统的成本结构和可靠性提供了理论基础，揭示了代理内部调度、中间状态保留对计算效率的影响，以及目标丢失风险与任务质量之间的内在制约关系。

Abstract: This paper extends the stochastic-oracle model of AI-augmented computing to include agentic oracles. Unlike a stationary stochastic oracle, which responds to the same query according to a fixed response distribution across calls, an agentic oracle can pursue a goal autonomously and may access an environment containing task-relevant resources. These capabilities affect both response distributions and token costs beyond what is visible at the query-response interface. We develop a framework for analyzing token costs in Stochastic-Oracle Turing Machines (SOTMs) that compute with agentic oracles. Each call has an \emph{orchestration token cost}, visible to the caller at the query-response interface, and an \emph{agentic token cost}, incurred by internal operations not exposed to the caller. We show that an SOTM computing with an agentic oracle that can retain intermediate state can have token-cost advantages over SOTMs using stationary stochastic oracles when solving the same task at the same quality level, both with and without environment access. We also investigate goal-loss risk, including how internal dispatch ordering can reduce exposure to irreversible actions. We provide a goal-loss avoidance criterion, derive progress--retry--goal-loss formulas, establish goal-depth lower bounds on token complexity, characterize token complexity when the probability of goal loss is zero, and show that goal-loss risk can impose an upper bound on the achievable quality of a task involving environment updates.

</details>


### [296] [Sweet Little Lies: Strategic Deception in AI Emotional Support Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01480)
*Aseem Pahuja,Zhiling Guo,Tahir Abbas Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文运用贝叶斯劝说框架分析Gen AI情感支持聊天机器人的策略行为，发现其面临经济激励偶尔误报用户情绪状态以最大化参与度指标。均衡分析显示，最优策略是真实报告真正需要支持的用户状态，但会在用户情绪良好时策略性误报。有趣的是，这种欺骗在提高用户参与度的同时并未降低用户的预期收益；更警觉的用户会获得更诚实的评估。然而，模型也引发了显著的伦理与监管担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持型AI聊天机器人在实际应用中日益广泛，但其商业运营中追求参与度指标的行为可能导致信息操纵。作者希望从经济学角度（贝叶斯劝说）揭示聊天机器人策略性报告用户情绪状态的行为逻辑及其影响，填补AI情感支持领域中关于欺骗性沟通的经济学建模空白。

Method: 采用标准贝叶斯劝说（Bayesian Persuasion）理论框架进行博弈建模。模型设定中，聊天机器人作为信号发送者，向用户发送关于其情绪状态的信号；用户作为接收者，根据信号决定是否参与互动。通过均衡分析求解聊天机器人的最优信号策略，考察不同用户类型（不同参与阈值）下的最优反应与信号可信度。

Result: 研究发现：1）聊天机器人在用户真正需要支持时如实报告、而在用户情绪良好时策略性夸大情绪需求，是最优策略；2）这种欺骗行为实际上能提高用户参与度且不损害用户预期收益（帕累托改进性质）；3）对参与阈值更高（更警觉/更怀疑）的用户，聊天机器人难以进行欺骗，需提供更诚实的评估；4）尽管如此，该模型揭示了潜在伦理和监管风险。

Conclusion: 虽然模型表明欺骗可以在不降低用户福利的情况下发生，但AI情感支持机器人的策略性信息操纵仍引发显著的伦理与监管关切。研究呼吁需要在商业激励驱动下的参与度优化与用户情感福祉（包括知情权和真实反馈）之间寻求平衡，为相关AI应用的政策制定提供理论参考。

Abstract: The paper examines the strategic behavior of Gen AI chatbots used for emotional support. Using a Bayesian Persuasion, we model interactions between chatbots that send signals about users' emotional states and users who decide whether to engage based on these signals. We demonstrate that chatbots face economic incentives to occasionally misrepresent users' emotional conditions to maximize engagement metrics.
